eṭuttal paṭuttal nalita luḻappiṉ
tiripum tattamiṟ ciṟituḷa vākum  (naṉ. )
eṉṟār pavaṇantiyār. iṅṅaṉam ōreḻuttē paṟpala vitamāka olikkiṉum. evviṭattil evvoliyeṉṟaṟiya ētum iṭarp pāṭillai. ēṉeṉṟāl ovvō roliyum iyalpākavē iṭamum cārpumpaṟṟi vevvēṟoliyāy olikkiṉṟatu. tamiḻaruḷ evar pēciṉum ivviyalpu māṟuvatiṉṟu. pēcum pōtu maṭṭumiṉṟi oru nūlai vācikkumpōtum ivviyalpu māṟuvatiṉṟu. itaik kālṭuvel kaṇkāṇiyārum tamatu oppiyal nūliṟ kuṟittuḷḷār.
iyalpākavum eḷitākavum irukkiṟa tamiḻmoḻiyaip pōṉṟē. ataṉ eḻuttumuṟaiyum iyalpākavum eḷitākavu mirukkavēṇṭi nam nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipula muṉṉai yāciriyar oru variyāṉēyē pala olikaḷait kuṟippittaṉar eṉṉē! avar aṟiviṉ māṇpu!
iṉi, tamiḻiluḷḷa icaiyum kuṟippumpaṟṟiya palvakai yolikaḷaiyuṅ kuṟikka variyillai yeṉṉalām.
e-ṭu : kum kum (gum gum) mirutaṅkat toṉi.
u - nāyai u kāṭṭal allatu vēṭṭaimēl ēval.
jaljal - calaṅkai yoli.
jam - kampīrat tōṟṟak kuṟippu.
i (ezzz) - pāmpu. vaṇṭu mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ iraiccal.
uṣ (ush - hush) - amaittaṟ kuṟippu. ivaiyellām eḻuttu muṟaik kēlāta puṟavolikaḷākak koṇṭaṉarēyaṉṟi, poruṇ moḻikku vēṇṭum eḻut tolikaḷākak koṇṭila reṉka.
aṟṟēl piṟa moḻikaḷil ivvolikaḷaik koṇṭa teṉṉaiyeṉiṉ, avaiyellām ceyaṟkai moḻikaḷāta liṉ avaṟṟukkuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa veṉṟum, avaṟṟuḷḷum muṟku, vīḷai, kaṉaippu, kattu, aḻukai, tummal mutaliya oli kaḷaiyellām kuṟikka variyillaiyeṉṟum, ellā olikaḷai yum kuṟikka variyuḷḷa moḻi ulakattil oṉṟumēyillai yeṉṟum kūṟi viṭukka.
e, o, ḻa, ṟa eṉṟa olikaḷ vaṭamoḻikkillai. ākaiyāl oṭṭelumpu eṉpatai ōṭṭēlumpu eṉṟum, vāḻaippaḻa vaṟṟal eṉpatai vālapala varral eṉṟu mallavō eḻuta vēṇṭum?
ña, ṇa, ḻa, ḷa eṉṟa olikaḷ āṅkilattiṟ killai. iv volikaḷ varukiṉṟa coṟkaḷai eṅṅaṉ āṅkilattil eḻuta muṭiyum? kaṇṇiyaik kaṉṉiyeṉṟum mūḷaiyai mūlai yeṉṟu mallavō eḻuta vēṇṭum?
iṅkilīṣil uḷḷa f, z, zh mutaliya olikaḷ ettuṇaiyō olikaḷaiyuṭaiya vaṭamoḻikkum illaiyē! iṅṅaṉam ovvoru moḻikkum cila ciṟap peḻuttukaḷiruppatāl, orumoḻic ciṟappeḻuttuc coṟkaḷaip piṟamoḻi yileḻuta voṇṇātākum. itaiyaṟiyāta vaṭamoḻivāṇaṉ oruvaṉ, oru tamiḻaṉai nōkkic cakatirataḷāmpujam eṉpatait tamiḻil eḻutuka eṉṟu paḻittāṉām. piṉpu vāḻaippaḻavaṟṟal eṉpatai vaṭamoḻiyil eḻutuka eṉṟu tamiḻaṉ coṉṉatum viḻittāṉām. ākaiyāl oru moḻic ciṟappeḻuttuc coṟ kaḷai iṉṉoru moḻiyil vikāramiṉṟi yeḻuta voṇṇā teṉṟum, ellā moḻic coṟkaḷaiyum taṟcamamāka eḻutum moḻi ulakattē oṉṟu millaiyeṉṟum terintukoḷka.
itai yaṟiyātār f tamiḻil illaiyeṉṟum, ja tamiḻilillai yeṉṟum. iṉṉōraṉṉa eḻuttukaḷaip putitāyc cērttal vēṇṭumeṉṟum ōlamiṭṭut tirivar. ulaka muḻumaikkum potu moḻiyāy vantu koṇṭirukkiṉṟa iṅkilīṣil, ēṉaimoḻic ciṟappeḻuttukaḷaic cērkkak kāraṇamirup piṉum, evarēṉum ña, ṇa, ḻa, ḷa mutaliya eḻuttukaḷai iṅkilīṣil cērkka vēṇṭumeṉṟu kūṟiṉa tuṇṭā? aṉṟi, mika niṟaivuḷḷatākak karutappaṭum vaṭamoḻi yarivariyai iṉṉum niṟaivākka vēṇṭi e, o, ḻa, ṟa eṉṟa eḻuttukaḷaic cērkka vēṇṭu meṉṟu. evarēṉum kūṟiṉatuṇṭā? ivarellām amaintirukkavum tamiḻarmaṭṭum muṉaintu niṟpatēṉ? itaṉāṉēyē piṟarkkut tāymoḻip paṟṟuḷateṉṟum tamiḻarkkilateṉṟum viḷaṅkukiṉṟatē!
oru moḻiyiṉ arivari am moḻiyolikaṭkumaṭṭum amaintatā? piṟamoḻiyolikaṭkum amaintatā? orumoḻi yarivariyāl am moḻic coṟkaḷai maṭṭum eḻutātu, piṟa moḻic coṟkaḷai eḻuti nakaippatu perum pētaimai yākum. oru moḻikku atiluḷḷa olikaḷai yellām kuṟikka variyiṉṟeṉiṉ atuvē uṇmaiyāṉa kuṟaiyākum.
tamiḻil ovvōrolikkum ovvōr variyuṇṭu. ulakamoḻi kaḷellāvaṟṟiṉ arivarika	ḷaiyum ovvoṉṟāy uṟṟunōk kiṉ. tamiḻari variyē piṟamoḻikaḷ aḻukkāṟumāṟu attaṉai nirampiṉatum varampiṉatumā yirukkiṟatu. uyireḻuttukaḷ paṉṉiraṇṭiṟkum taṉittaṉi variyuḷḷatu. kuṟil kuṟilākavum neṭil neṭilākavum uccarikkappaṭum. meyyeḻuttukaḷ ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvāka iyaṟkaiyāṉa eḷiya volikaḷ pattoṉpatē. g kakarattiṉ pētamum, T cakarattiṉ pētamumiṉṟit taṉiyolikaḷākā.
iṅkilīṣil 5 varikaḷāl 18 (20, Nesfield) uyirolikaḷaik kuṟippikkiṉṟaṉar. ōrolikkup pala variyum, oru varikkup pala voliyumuḷḷaṉa. c.g x (ks) w eṉpaṉa mikai varikaḷ. ‡(ng), õ(sh), j(th), zh eṉṉum olikaḷaik kuṟikkat taṉi variyillai. iṇai vari (digraph) kaḷē.
arapi moḻiyil ē, ya eṉṉum īrolikaṭku orē variyum o, va eṉṉum īrolikaṭku orē variyum uḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻil oru vari palavolip pētaṅkaḷaik kuṟippiṉum ap pētaṅkaḷ iṭamum cārpumpaṟṟi iyaṟkaiyālēyē tōṉṟuva tāl vēṇṭāta varikaḷaip perukki nammai varuttiyilar nam muṉṉōr.
iṉi, icai viḷi pulampal ātiyil, eḻuttukaḷ ettuṇaiyum nīṇṭolippataik kuṟikkat tamiḻil iruvakai yaḷapeṭai kaḷuḷa. piṟa moḻikaṭ kivaiyillai. vaṭamoḻiyil uyiraḷa peṭaiyē yuṇṭu : oṟṟaḷapeṭaiyillai.
tamiḻil varikaḷ eḷitāy viḷaṅkumāṟu vevvēṟu vaṭivā yuḷḷaṉa. au eṉpatiluḷḷaḷa vaṭivaic ciṟitāyeḻutātatu accu vārppār kuṟṟamēyaṉṟi eḻuttāciriyar kuṟṟamaṉṟu. teluṅku, arapi, camakirutam mutaliya moḻikaḷil māṇavar mayaṅku māṟu  pala varikaḷ ēṟattāḻa orēvaṭivāyuḷḷaṉa.
tamiḻvarikaḷ iṅkilīṣ varikaḷpōla mika nuṇṇiya (Diamond, Brilliant) vaṭivilu meḻutappaṭum. camaṟkirutam, arapi, teluṅku mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ varikaḷ attuṇai nuṇukkiyeḻuta iṭantarā. itaṉālēyē camaṟkiruta veḻuttukaḷaik kūṭṭeḻuttuk (camyuktākṣaram) kaḷāka eḻutuvar.
iṉi, uyiroliyum meyyoliyum uyirum meyyum (life and body) kūṭiya uyirmey (pirāṇi) pōla oṉṟi olippatu uyirmey varikaḷāṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. uyir, mey, uyirmey mutaliya kuṟiyīṭukaḷ tamiḻariṉ tattuvavaṟivai yuṇarttum. uyirmey varikaḷāl oli nūlaṟivum variccurukkamum payaṉām.
tamiḻ arivariyil uyir muṉṉum mey piṉṉum āytam iṭai yum, uyiruḷ kuṟil muṉṉum neṭil piṉṉum, meyyuḷ vali muṉṉum meli piṉṉum iṭai iṭaiyum vaikkappaṭṭaṉa.
uyirmey variyum eḻuttu muṟaiyum tamiḻukkē ciṟappākum. intiya moḻikaṭkellām ivai piṟkālattē tamiḻaip piṉpaṟṟiyēṟpaṭṭaṉa. arapi, hīpru mutaliya cēmiyak (Semitic) kavaimoḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum, camaṟkiruta moḻinta āriyak kavaimoḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟiṟkum ivaiyillai. eḻuttukaḷ iṅkilīṣiṟ pōla uyirum meyyum vēṟuvēṟu aṭuttaṭut teḻutappaṭuvatuṭaṉ, arivariyil uyirum meyyum muṟaiyiṉṟi mayaṅkik kiṭakkum. āriyamum cēmiyamumākiya mēlai moḻi kaṭkellām uyirmey vaṭivum, eḻuttu muṟaiyum illā tirukkavum, camaṟkirutattiṟku maṭṭum avai eṅṅaṉa miruntirut tal kūṭum? camaṟkirutattiṟku avai iruntaṉaveṉiṉ, ataṉ iṉamākiya cerumaṉ, ṭaccu, kirēkku, ilattīṉ mutaliya moḻi kaṭkum, avaṟṟai aṭuttu vaḻaṅkum cēmiya moḻikaṭkum ēṉ avaiyillai? vaṭamoḻi āriyar intiyāviṟku vantatu ki.mu. 3000. avar varukaikku muṉṉarē tamiḻil eḻuttiruntatu. av veḻuttukaḷ uyirmey nīṅkalāka 31.
olimuṟaiyiṉum coṉmuṟaiyiṉum tamiḻukku aṭuttuḷḷa kirēkka, ilattīṉ moḻikaḷil muṟaiyē eḻuttukaḷ 24, 25; vaṭamoḻiyil 48. ilattīṉ eḻuttukaḷ 24 eṉṟum, 23 eṉṟum kaṇakkiṭuvatumuṇṭu. tamiḻilillāta cila ceyaṟkai yolikaḷ ilattīṉ, kirēkku moḻikaḷiluḷa. āṉāl, avaṟṟiṟ palavaṟ ṟiṟkut taṉi varikaḷ illai. B, G, mutaliya toṉippolikaṭkut taṉi variyuḷa. bh, gh mutaliya mūccoli cērnta toṉippoli kaṭkut taṉivariyillai. camaṟkirutattil avaṟṟiṟkellām taṉivari yuṇṭu. atōṭu ña, ṇa mutaliya tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷum cērkkappaṭṭuḷa. mēlai moḻikaṭkum tamiḻukkumuḷḷa eḻuttukaḷiṟ perumpālaṉavaṟṟiṟkut taṉivariyamaittu vaṭa moḻiyeḻuttukaḷaip perukkik kāṭṭiṉar vaṭamoḻi vāṇar. ka, ca, ṭa, ta, pa eṉṟu tamiḻmuṟaiyil valliṉa mey yeḻuttukaḷiruppatum, k, kh, g, gh eṉa ovvoru valliṉa meyyum toṉippatum, toṉiyātatum, mūccoli cērntatum cērātatumāka nannāṉkāka uṟaḻappaṭṭi ruppatum, camaṟkirutat tiṉ ceyaṟkaiyāṉa eḻuttup perukkaik kāṭṭum.
camaṟkirutam, kirēkka ilattīṉ moḻikaḷiṉ tiripu allatu vaḷarcci yeṉpataṟkup pala ātāraṅkaḷuḷa. kirēkka ilattīṉ moḻikaḷ āriya moḻikaḷil mikap paḻaimaiyāṉavai. kirēkka ilattīṉ eḻuttukaḷiṉum camaṟkiruta eḻuttukaḷ mikuti. kirēkka ilattīṉiluḷḷa pala coṟkaḷ camaṟkirutattil mikat tirintuḷḷaṉa. camaṟkirutam eṉṉum peyarukkē naṉṟākac ceyyappaṭṭatu eṉpatu poruḷ. ulakattiluḷḷa moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilum eḻuttup perukkamum ceyaṟkai yolip perukkamuḷḷatu camaṟkirutamoṉṟē. ataṉāṉēyē atu vaḻakkaṟ ṟoḻintatu.
vaṭamoḻiyeḻuttukaḷai 48 ākap perukkiyiruppiṉum, oli muṟaippaṭi pārppiṉ, avaṟṟiṟ puṇarolikaḷ allatu kūṭṭeḻuttukaḷ palavākum.
ru, lu eṉpavai kuṟṟiyalukaram cērnta rakara lakaraṅkaḷākum. rū eṉpatu ru eṉpataṉ nīṭṭam. am eṉpatu am eṉṉum acai; akara uyirum makarameyyum cērntatu. ivai uyirmey kaḷum acaiyumākaviruntum uyireḻuttukaḷiṟ cērkkappaṭ ṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. e, o eṉṟa uyirkaḷ vaṭamoḻikkillātatu oru peruṅ kuṟaiyākum.
meyyeḻuttukaḷil ai varukkattil h cērnta eḻuttuka ḷellām iraṭṭai yeḻuttukaḷākum. cantiyakṣaraṅkaḷ cila taṉi yeḻuttukaḷ pōṟ kāṭṭappaṭum. itaṉāl vaṭamoḻi yeḻuttup perukkiṉāl oru ciṟappumillai yeṉka.
vaṭamoḻi āriyar intiyāviṟku vanta piṉṉarē uyirmey variyum eḻuttumuṟaiyum vaṭamoḻikku ēṟpaṭṭaṉa. vaṭamoḻi varikaḷ teṉmoḻi varikaḷiṉ tiripē eṉpatu irumoḻi varikaḷaiyum oppu nōkkuvārkku iṉitu pulaṉām. vaṭamoḻi yarivari yēṟpaṭṭapiṉ ataiyoṭṭi ataṉ kiḷaimoḻi kaṭkum piṉpu tamiḻk kiḷaimoḻikaṭkum arivarikaḷ ēṟ paṭṭaṉa. itaṉāl perciyaṉ arapi varikaḷaik koṇṭa intutāṉi yoḻinta ēṉai yintiya moḻikaṭkellām uyirmeyvariyum eḻuttumuṟaiyum ēṟpaṭṭuḷa.
ātiyil ēṉaiya moḻikaṭkellā millātu uyirmey varikaḷ tamiḻuk kiruntatiṉālēyē, ataṉ arivari nīṇṭu neṭuṅ kaṇakkeṉappaṭṭatu. ēṟkeṉavē neṭuṅkaṇak kāyirukkum arivariyai (centamiḻp puravalar umāmakēcuvaram piḷḷai yavarkaḷ kūṟiyapaṭi) iṉṉum neṭuṅkaṇakkākka niṉaippatu eṉṉē pētaimai!
iṉi, oru moḻikku nirampiṉa arivariyiruppiṉum, piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷai atil moḻipeyarkkap pōtiya coṟkaḷuḷavā eṉṟu pārttal vēṇṭum. ilaveṉiṉ piṟamoḻic coṟ kaḷaiyum piṟamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷaiyum atiṟ putitāyc cērkka iṭamuṇṭu. tamiḻil ellāp poruḷkaḷaiyum veḷiyiṭat takka eṇṇiṟanta coṟkaḷuḷḷaṉa. ātaliṉ piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷaiyēṉum eḻuttukaḷaiyēṉum putitāyc cērkka eṭṭuṇaiyum iṭamiṉṟu. poruḷkaḷiṉ ciṟappup peyar (Proper Name) kaḷē eḻuttup peyarppiṟkum (Transliteration) taṟpavattiṟkum iṭamākum. itaṟkup piṟamoḻi yeḻuttukaḷ vēṇṭuvavalla. tamiḻil iyaṉṟatuṇai eḻutal takum. ētēṉuṅ kuṟaiyiruppiṉ atu ellā moḻikaṭkum potuvākum. itaṟkeṉṟē tamiḻiṟ putteḻuttukaḷaic cērppiṉ tamiḻiṉ tūymaiyum iyalpum eḷimaiyum iṉimaiyum keṭum. aṅṅaṉam tamiḻaik keṭuttu vaḻaṅkuvatiṉum. atai viṭṭuviṭṭu ayaṉ moḻiyai vaḻaṅkuvatē cālac ciṟantatām. ataṉāṟ putteḻuttu kaḷait tamiḻiṟ pukuttavēṇṭum eṉṉum poruntāk kūṟṟaip puṟattē viṭukka.  - centamiḻc celvi naḷi 1934.
neyavut toḻil
nūlum neyavum paṟṟiya coṟkaḷ aṟivu nūlaiyum ceyyuṭ pāvaiyum kuṟikka, varutalāl. neyavut toḻilukkum ceyyuḷ toḻilukkum yātēṉum oppumaiyuṇmai peṟappaṭum.
mutalāvatu iruvakai nūlaiyum nōkkiṉ avaṟṟukkup potuvāṉa nuṇmai nīṭci nērmai eṉṉum mūviyalpukaḷ pulaṉākum. pañciṉāl neyavu nūliḻaippatu pōlac collāl aṟivu nūliḻaittal allatu cilanti paṭṭup pūcci mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ uṭampiṉiṉṟu veḷivarum neyavu nūlaip pōlap pulavaṉiṉ uḷḷat tiṉiṉṟu aṟivu nūl veḷippaṭutal maṟṟumoru potut taṉmaiyākum.
iraṇṭāvatu iruvakaip pāvaiyum nōkkiṉ avaṟṟiṟkup potuvāṉa nīṭci parappu icaippu ākiya mūviyalpukaḷ pulaṉākum.  col : 7. 8.
neruñcip paḻam
peruneruñci mutirnta nilaiyil nellikkaṉi pōlap pacu mañcaḷ niṟam koḷḷumātaliṉ paḻam eṉappaṭum.  (ti.ma. 1120)
neruppup paṟṟiya cuḷ aṭic coṟkaḷ
u - ul - ula, ulattal = erital. kāytal, ula-ulavai = kāynta marakkiḷai. ilai tīnta vulavaiyāl (kalit. 11).
ula - ular. ulartal = kāytal.
ular - ulaṟu. ulaṟutal = kāytal, ciṉattal, vaṟṟutal.
ula - ulai = 1. neruppuḷḷa aṭuppu. 2. kollaṉ aṭuppu. 3. cōṟu camaikkak kotikka vaikkum nīr.
ul - al - aṉṟu. aṉṟutal = erital, ciṉattal, pakaittal.
al - aṉ - aṉal = neruppu. aṉalutal = erital.
aṉal - aṉali = 1. neruppu. 2. katiravaṉ.
aṉal - aṉalam = neruppu. aṉalam - va. anala.
aṉalam - aṉalaṉ = nerupput teyvam.
ul - uḷ - oḷ - oṇmai = 1. neruppiṉ taṉmaiyāṉa viḷakkam. 2. uḷḷoḷi, nallaṟivu. 3. iyaṟkaiyaḻaku. 4. naṉmai.
oḷ - oḷi = 1. neruppiṉ viḷakkam. 2. oḷipōl viḷaṅkum pukaḻ. 3. uḷḷoḷi.
oḷi - oḷir. oḷirtal = viḷaṅkutal.
oḷir - oḷiṟu. oḷiṟutal = viḷaṅkutal.
oḷ - oḷḷiya = oḷiyuḷḷa, aṟivu mikka, nalla, aḻakiya.
oḷḷiyaṉ = aṟivuṭaiyōṉ. oḷḷiyōṉ = viḷakkam mikka veḷḷikkōḷ.
oḷ = 1. oḷiyuḷḷa. 2. aḻakiya.
oḷ + toṭi = oṇṭoṭi = viḷaṅkum vaḷaiyalaṇinta peṇ. oḷ + nutal = oṇṇutal = viḷaṅkum neṟṟiyuṭaiya peṇ.
oḷ + pu = oṇpu - oṭpu - oṭpam = aṟivu. kallātā ṉoṭpaṅ kaḻiyanaṉ ṟāyiṉum (kuṟaḷ. 404).
uḷ - aḷ - aḻa - aḻal = 1. neruppu. 2. veppam 3. erivu. 4. narakam. 5. tuḷaṅkoḷi (kēṭṭai). 6. cevvāy. 7. ciṉam.
aḻalavaṉ = 1. tītteyvam. 2. katiravaṉ. 3. cevvāy.
aḻalutal = 1. erital. 2. viḷaṅkutal. 3. kāntutal. 4. ciṉattal.
aḻal - aḻaṟṟu (pi.vi.). aḻaṟṟutal = erittal, vemmai ceytal.
aḻal - aḻaṟu - aḷaṟu = narakam.
aṇṇāttal ceyyā taḷaṟu eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷaṭiyil (255) `aḷaṟu eṉṉum col ēṭṭup piḻaiyākavum irukkalām.
pūriyarkaḷ āḻum aḷaṟu eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷaṭi (919) cēṟṟu vaṭi vāṉa narakavakai yeṉṟu koḷḷa iṭantariṉum, narakam nerup pulakameṉṟē potuvākak koḷḷappaṭuvataṉālum, aḻal, eri mutaliya peyarkaḷ ataṟkuṇmaiyālum, ūṟṟaḷavilēyē nerupput tuṉpuṟuttuvataṉālum, aḻaṟu eṉṉuñ collē aḷaṟu eṉa maruviṟṟeṉṟō, ēṭṭup piḻaiyāka nērnta teṉṟō, koḷvatu poruttamāṉatē.
aḻal - aḻali = neruppu. aḻalittal = ericcal.
aḻal - aḻalai = toṇṭaik karakarappu.
aḻal - aḻaṉ - aḻaṉam = 1. neruppu, 2. vemmai.
aḻaṉammai nīkkuvikkum eṉṉum tēvāra (1225 : 4) aṭiyi luḷḷa aḻaṉammai eṉṉun toṭarai, aḻaṉ + nammai eṉṟum pirikkalām.
aḻaṉ - aḻaṉi o.nō : aḻal - aḻali, kaḷaṉ - kaḻaṉi.
aḻa - aka - akai. akaital = erital. akaiyeri yāṉātu (kalit. 139 : 26). o.nō : maḻa - maka.
aḻaṉi - akaṉi - va. akni (agni) -L. ignis. itaṟku yācukar (Yaskar) tam niruttattiṟ (Nirukta) kūṟiyirukkum vērp poruṭ kāraṇaṅkaḷ muṟṟum poruntāp poyttal putuccēri aravintar tavanilaiya irukku vētap patippu akō (ago) eṉṟu mūlaṅkāṭṭuvatum, muṟṟum tavaṟām. itu camaṟkirutattil aj eṉṟē niṟkum.
uḷ - uṇ - uṇṇam = veppam. uṇṇa vaṇṇat teḷinañca muṇṭu (tēvā. 510 : 6).
uṇṇam - va. uṣṇa. marāt. ūṉ.
uṇ - uṇa. uṇattal = kāytal. miḷakāy uṇantuviṭṭatu (u.va.). uṇanta miḷakāy (u.va.). = miḷakāy vaṟṟal.
uṇa - uṇattu (pi.vi.) uṇattutal = 1. kāyappōṭutal. nellai uṇattiṉār. (u.va.). 2. uṭampai melivittal allatu vaṟṟavaittal. meyyuṇattalum (tailava. taili. 110).
uṇa - uṇaṅku. uṇaṅkutal = nel mutaliyaṉa kāytal uṇaṅ kuṇāk kavarum (paṭṭiṉap. 22). tiṉaiviḷaittār muṟṟan tiṉai yuṇaṅkum (tamiḻnā. 154.) 2. melital. ūṭaluṇaṅka viṭuvārōṭu (kuṟaḷ. 1310). 3. vāṭutal. uṇaṅkiya cintaiyīr (kantapu. mōṉa. 21). 4. curuṅkutal. uṇaṅkarum pukaḻ (kāñcippu nāṭṭup. 1).
uṇaṅku - uṇaṅkal = 1. ularttiya kūlam. uṇaṅkal keṭak kaḻutai yutaṭāṭṭaṅkaṇṭeṉ payaṉ (tiv. tiruvāy) 4 : 6 : 7). 2. vaṟṟal. veḷḷeṉ puṇaṅkalum (maṉi. 16 : 67). 3. ularnta pū. (piṅ.)
uṇaṅku - uṇakku (pi.vi.) uṇakkutal = ularttutal. toṭippuḻuti kaḵcā vuṇakkiṉ (kuṟaḷ. 1037).
uṇakku - uṇakkam = vāṭṭam.
ma. uṇakkam, ka. oṇakilu (g), tu. oṇakelu. 
uṇakku = vāṭṭam. uṇakkilāta tōr vittu (tiruvāca. 30 : 1).
ul - ur - uru. uruttal = 1. aḻalutal. āka muruppa nūṟi (puṟanā. 25 : 10). 2. peruñciṉaṅ koḷḷutal, oru pakalellā murutteḻuntu (kalit 39 : 23).
uru - uruttiram = peruñciṉam. uruttiram - va. rutra (d). tiram oru toḻiṟpaṇpīṟu. o.nō : māttal = aḷattal. mā+tiram = māttiram (aḷavu). avaṉ eṉakku emmāttiram? eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. māṉam (mā+aṉam) eṉṉum aḷavup potuppeyaraiyum, mā, araimā, (orumā), irumā, mākāṇi mutaliya aḷavuc ciṟappup peyarkaḷaiyum, mēlai va. ā. māvaṭṭattil paṭi eṉṉum mukattalaḷavaip peyar māṉam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkutalaiyum nōkkuka. māttal eṉṉum viṉaic col paṇṭē vaḻakkiṟantatu. tiram - tirai. mā +tirai = māttirai = aḷavu. eḻuttolikkuṅ kāla aḷavu, uṇṇum maruntaḷavu.
ciṉam tīyottalāl, tī paṟṟiya coṟkaḷellām ciṉattaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
uruttiram - va. rautra (raudra) = vekuḷi.
o.nō : E. wrath, n. anger O.E. wrreththu, AS and ON. wraedo. ME. wrathe.
E. wroth, adj. angry. OE wrath, OS. wreth, OHG - reid, ON reithier.
kīṟṟum (Skeat) kiḷēṉum (Klein) ic coṟkaḷ reid (twristed) eṉṉum mūlattiṉiṉṟu tirikkiṉṟaṉar. atu teṉcollai aṟiyāmaiyālum irukkalām.
uru - urum - urumam = 1. veppam. 2. veppam mikka naṇpakal.

cul - cuḷ. cuḷḷeṉal = veyil kāytaṟ kuṟippu, uṟaittaṟ kuṟippu. cuḷ = karuvāṭu (kāyntamīṉ). cuḷḷiṉaik kaṟittaṉar (kantapu. acamukinakar. 18).

cuḷ - cuṇṭu. cuṇṭutal = (ce. ku.vi.) 1. nīr vaṟṟutal 2. vāṭutal, curuṅkutal, acceyti kēṭṭavuṭaṉ avaṉ mukañ cuṇṭip pōyiṟṟu. (u.va.) - (ce. kuṉṟāvi) nīr vaṟṟak kāyccutal allatu avittal. iṉṟaikkuk kaṭalai cuṇṭiṉārkaḷ. (u.va.).
cuṇṭu-cuṇṭal = 1. nīr muṟṟum vaṟṟa avitta kaṭalaip payaṟu. 2. cuṇṭaṟkaṟi.

cuḷ (cul - col) - L. sol, sup, Icel. sol, OE., ON, sol Dan., Swed., sol, E. sol, Goth. soule, Lith, soule, Lett, soule, Ir, su#l, Rus, Owf. solntse, W. haul (for saul), Co. heuul, hau, Bret, heol, sun.

ivaṟṟiṟkellām sū (su) sau (sau) eṉpaṉa uyttuṇarvu mūlamākak kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēlaiyāriyar tamiḻiṉ toṉmai muṉmai teṉmait taṉmaikaḷai aṟiyāmaiyāl iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. allatu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. ivaṟṟiṟ kellām cul eṉṉum teṉcollē aṭimūlam.
cul - col = neruppup pōl allatu poṉ pōl viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum nel.
caṭaic cennel poṉviḷaikkun taṉṉāṭu eṉṟu naḷaveṇpā (cuyamvara. 68) kūṟutalaiyum, poṉ viḷainta kaḷattūr eṉṉum vaḻakkaiyum nōkkuka. nel eṉṉum peyarum ip poruṭ kāraṇaṅ koṇṭatē. nil - nila - nilā - nilavu. nilattal = oḷirtal, viḷaṅkutal. nil - nel.
col - coli - colittal = 1. erital. nāppaṇ aḻal colippatāka vuṉṉi (pirapōta. 44 : 15,) 2. oḷirtal. māṉameṉ ṟuraippa teḻuntumēṟ colittu varuvatu pōṉṟum (pirapōta. 11 : 1).
coli - joli - va. jvali eṉṟu tirintuḷḷatu. jval eṉpatai mūlamākak kāṭṭuvar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar. itu ceyaṟkai mūlam.
colittal  viṉaiccol. āḷappaṭṭa muṉṉūlkaḷ vaḻakkiṟan tamaiyāl, piṉṉūliṉiṉṟu mēṟkoḷ kāṭṭappaṭṭatu.
col - coṉ - coṉṉam = viḷaṅkum niṟappoṉ. (tivā.) coṉṉatāṉap payaṉeṉac colluvar. (kamparā. ciṟappu.).
coṉṉam - va. suvarṇa = naṉṉiṟamuḷḷatu, taṅkam. itu poruntap poyttalāṉa coṟṟirippu.
iṉi, cūr eṉpataiyē cu#r eṉṟu tirintu, cūrṇa (cu#rna) eṉṉuñ collait tōṟṟuvittuḷḷaṉar.
cūr (cu#r) = eri (to burn) cūrṇa - ta. cūraṇam = nīṟu, poṭi. cūraṇam - cūraṇi. cūraṇittal = nīṟṟutal, poṭi ceytal.  o.nō : cuḷ - cuḷḷam - cuṇṇam. cuḷḷal = erital (vaḻakkiṟanta viṉaiccol).
iṉi, cuḷ eṉṉum aṭipōṉṟē, kuḷ, tuḷ, nuḷ, puḷ. muḷ, eṉṉum vaḻiyaṭikaḷum uḷ eṉṉum mūla aṭiyiṉiṉṟu tirintu, neruppup paṟṟiya palvēṟu coṟkaḷaip piṟappikkum.
nelvakai
cennel veṇṇel kārnel eṉṟum, campā, maṭṭai, kār eṉṟum nellil palavakaikaḷ iruppatuṭaṉ, campāvil maṭṭum āvirampūccampā, āṉaik kompaṉ campā, kuṇṭuc campā, kutiraivāliccampā ciṟumaṇiccampā, cīrakaccampā mutaliya aṟupatu uḷvakaikaḷ uḷḷaṉa.  col. 74.
nēyam
nēyam - nēka
neḷ - ney - nē =	1. aṉpu. 2. īram. nēe neñciṉ (puṟam. 3).
nē - nēyam = 1. ney, 2. eṇṇey. 3. aṉpu. nēyattatāy neṉṉa leṉṉaip puṇarntu (tirukkō. 39). 	4. kaṭavuṭpaṟṟu. nēyattē niṉṟa nimala ṉaṭipōṟṟi (tiruvāca. 1 : 13). nēyam - nēcam - nēcaṉ.
vaṭavar kāṭṭum nih (oṭṭu) eṉṉum mūlam, ney eṉṉum teṉcoṟ ṟiripē.  (va.va : 192.)
nērpāṭu
nērpāṭu eṉpatu nīti eṉṉum vaṭacoṟku nērt teṉcol. nērmai eṉpatu nērpāṭṭaiyum kuṟikkum palaporuḷ oru col. nērpāṭu nērmaiyāṉa oḻukkam kōṇē nērpāṭā yiruntāṉ (pārata. cūtu. 207).  (ti.ma. 87.)
nocciyār
noccimālai cūṭi muṟṟukaiyiṭappaṭṭa nakaraik kākkum maṟavarāṉa akattār.  kuṟaḷ. 745.
noṇṭi  viḷaiyāṭṭu
āṭiṭamum poḻutum āṭuvārum : noṇṭi eṉpatu, poṭṭalilum tiṟanta veḷinilattilum, perumpālum ciṟuvarum ciṟupāṉmai ciṟumiyarum, pakalilum nilaviravilum viḷaiyāṭum viḷaiyāṭṭu.
āṭu karuvi : āṭuvār tokaikkēṟpa nilattiṟ kīṟappaṭṭa oru peru vaṭṭamē ivvāṭṭuk karuviyām.
āṭu muṟai : iruvarukkuk kuṟaiyāta cilarum palarum camat tokaiyavāṉa irukaṭcikaḷāyp pirintu koḷḷal vēṇṭum. iruvarkku mēṟpaṭṭavarāyiṉ uttikaṭṭip pirintu koḷvar.
marapāṉa muṟaikaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉpaṭi tuṇintukoṇṭu oru kaṭciyār vaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷiṟaṅkuvar. maṟu kaṭciyār veḷi niṉṟukoṇṭu, ovvoruvarāy ovvorumuṟai vaṭṭattiṟ kuḷ noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟu, uḷ niṟpāruḷ oruvaraiyō palaraiyō ellāraiyumō toṭa muyalvar. toṭappaṭṭavar uṭaṉē veḷivantuviṭal vēṇṭum. uḷniṟpār aṉaivarum, tammai noṇṭiyaṭippavar toṭātavāṟu, vaṭṭattiṟkuḷ aṅkumiṅkum ōṭit tirivar. aṅṅaṉam ōṭumpōtu kōṭṭai mitippiṉum, kōṭṭiṟku veḷiyē kāl vaippiṉum, uṭaṉē veḷivantuviṭal vēṇṭum. noṇṭiyaṭippār kaḷaittup pōyiṉ kōṭṭiṟku veḷiyē ceṉṟu kālūṉṟal vēṇṭum. orumuṟai veḷiyē ceṉṟapiṉ, kālūṉṟākkālum, mīṇṭum uḷḷē varal kūṭātu. noṇṭiyaṭippavar vaṭṭattiṟkuḷḷum kōṭṭiṉmēlum kālūṉṟiṉum, kōṭṭai mitikkiṉum, avar tolaivatōṭu, avarālum (avar pintiṉavarāyiṉ) avarukku muntiṉavarālum toṭappaṭṭu veḷiyēṟiyuḷḷa attuṇaippērum uṭaṉē uḷḷē vantuviṭuvar. mīṇṭum avarait toṭutal vēṇṭum. oruvar noṇṭiyaṭittu muṭinta piṉ, iṉṉoruvar noṇṭiyaṭippar. veḷiniṟkum kaṭci yāruḷ, mutalilō iṭaiyilō iṟutiyilō noṇṭiyaṭip pavar oruvarē uḷniṟpār ellāraiyum toṭṭuviṭuvatu muṇṭu; iṭaiyiṭṭu orēyoruvar oruvaraiyō palaraiyō toṭuvatumuṇṭu;  orōvoruvar orēyoruvarait toṭu vatumuṇṭu; ovvoruvarum ovvoruvarait toṭuvatu muṇṭu; oruvarum oruvaraiyum toṭātiruppatumuṇṭu. iṅṅaṉam, uḷ niṟkum kaṭciyāruḷ oruvarē veḷiyēṟiyirut talum, cilarō palarō veḷiyēṟi yiruttalum, aṉaivarum veḷiyēṟiyiruttalum, aṉaivarum veḷiyēṟātiruttalum, ākiya nālvakai nilaimai ēṟpaṭakkūṭum.
veḷiniṟkum kaṭciyāruḷ aṉaivarum noṇṭiyaṭittu muṭintapiṉ, allatu uḷniṟkum kaṭciyāruḷ aṉaivarum toṭappaṭṭapiṉ, ōr āṭṭam muṭiyum.
āṭṭa veṟṟi :  uḷniṟpār aṉaivarum toṭappaṭṭuviṭiṉ veḷiniṟ pārkkum, aṅṅaṉamaṉṟi oruvar eñciyiruppiṉum uḷ niṟpārkkum, veṟṟiyām.
āṭṭat toṭarcci : orumuṟai yāṭiyapiṉ,  mīṇṭum oru muṟaiyō pala muṟaiyō āṭa nēramum viruppamumiruppiṉ, aṅṅaṉañ ceyvar. muntiya āṭṭattil veṉṟa kaṭciyārē pintiya āṭṭattilum uḷniṟpar. uḷniṟpatē iṉiyatākak karutappaṭum.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : iv viḷaiyāṭṭu, pōrviṉaiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākum. pōrkkaḷattil orukāl veṭṭuṇṭa maṟavaṉ maṟukālāl noṇṭiyaṭittuc ceṉṟē, tāṉ paṭṭu vīḻun tuṇaiyum, pakaivarai veṭṭi vīḻttuvatuṇṭu. im maṟa viṉaiyai naṭittukkāṭṭum mukamākavē iv viḷaiyāṭṭut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
kuṟaittalaikaḷ kūttāṭuvatum talaiyaik kaiyilēnti niṟpatum pōṉṟa maṟaviṉaikaḷai nōkkumpōtu, oru kāṟ kuṟaikaḷ noṇṭic ceṉṟu nūḻilāṭṭuvatu viyappaṉṟu.
kaḷvaṉoruvaṉ paṭaiyiluḷḷa kutiraiyoṉṟait tiruṭa muyaṉṟu kāl taṟiyuṇṭapiṉ nalvaḻippaṭṭa ceytiyaic cintuc ceyyuḷāṟ puṉaintu kūṟum noṇṭi nāṭakam eṉṉum nāṭaka nūlvakaiyum uḷatu. cītakkāti noṇṭi nāṭakam itaṟkōr eṭuttukkāṭṭām. in nāṭakac ceyti mēṟkūṟiya pōr viṉaic ceytiyiṉ vēṟām.
āṭṭiṉ payaṉ : orukāl nōyppaṭṭum veṭṭuṇṭum naṭakka viyalātapōtu maṟukālāl noṇṭiyaṭittu vēṇṭumiṭañ celvataṟkāṉa payiṟciyai, iv viḷaiyāṭṭu aḷikkum.
noṟu nāṭṭiyam
mika nuṭpamāṉa avinayaṅkaḷuṭaṉ naṭaṉam ceytal noṟu nāṭṭiyam eṉappaṭum. vēṇṭāta nuṇṇiya viṉaikaḷaic ceyyum oruvaṉai noṟunāṭṭiyam piṭittavaṉ eṉpar.  	(col. 12.)
nōṉpuvakai
āṉmāviṉ tūymaikkāka ōriru nōṉpu - vēḷai allatu nāḷ uṇṇātiruttal; tavam - āṉmāviṉ tūymaip pāṭṭiṟ kākavum arumporuṭ pēṟṟiṟkākavum pala nāḷ uṇṭi curukkal;  tuṟavu - ulakappaṟṟai yoḻittuk kālamellām kaṭuntavam ceytal.  (col. 59)

mēlaiyāriyac coṟkaḷ

pāṅku (iṭam), pākkam (ūr), pākkai (ūr) - L. pagus, a place, village, district, province.
gh§f‹ - OF., ME., F. page, a boy or man, usu. in livery, employed as personal attendant of person of rank and to go on errands. paidos (paital) eṉṉum kirēkkac colliṉiṉṟu tirinta paggio eṉṉum ittāliyac collai itaṟku mūlamākak kāṭṭuvatu poruntātu.
camaṟkirutac coṟkaḷ
paksh, to take a part or side, (W).
pakkam - paksha, a wing, RV; the shoulder, the flank or side or half of anything, RV; the half of a lunar month (sukla or krishna paksha); a side, party, faction; place; one of two cases or one side of an argument, a thesis, the proposition to be proved in a syllogism; an army, favour, proximity, neighbourhood etc.
panti - pankti, a row or set or collection of five.
pāku - bahu, the arm.
paku - bhaj, to divide, distribute, allot or apportion to or share with.
 o.nō : puku (uṇ) - bhuj.
bhakta, distributed, assigned, allotted, RV.
bhakti, distribution, partition, separation, RV; a division, portion, share; the being a part of; that which belongs to anything else, attachment, fondness for, devotion, homage, piety, faith.
bhaktri, devotedly attached, an adorer, worshipper.
bhaga, dispenser, gracious lord, patron.
bhagavat, possessing fortune, prosperous, happy, RV; adorable, venerable, AV.
bhagaval, or bhagavan, in comp. e.g. bhagavad - gita.
bhagavati, name of Lakshmi or Durga.
bhagin, prosperous, happy.
bhagini, a sister (‘the happy or fortunate one’ as having a brother).
bhajaka, distributor, apportioner.
bhaga, a part, portion, share, allotment, inheritance, lot, good fortune,
	luck, destiny.
	bhagavata, relating to or coming from Bhagvat, i.e. Vishnu or Krishna, a
follower or worshipper of Bhagavat.
1. bhagya, to be shared or divided, divisible.
2. bhagya, entitled to a share, lucky, fortunate; good fortune, fate, destiny.
bhaj, sharing or participating in.
bhajaka, a divisor.
bhajana, sharing or participating in.
bhajita, shared, divided, distributed, portioned.
bhajya, to be shared or distributed., to be divided.
mēṟkāṭṭiya tamiḻc coṟkaḷellām, paku eṉṉum orē mūlac colliṉiṉṟu toṭarcciyākat tōṉṟiyavai yeṉpatu. kūrntu nōkkuvārkkut teṟṟeṉa viḷaṅkum. āyiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyil, avai pakṣ, pañccaṉ (pancan), paj (bhaj), pah (bah) eṉṉum toṭarpaṟṟa nālvēṟu mūlattiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyavaiyākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. am mūlaṅkaḷuḷ  bah eṉpataṟku banh eṉpatē aṭimūlam eṉṟum kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṟku vaḷartal allatu mikutal eṉpatē aṭipporuḷ. kai eṉṉum ciṉaippeyarukkup pakkattiliruppatu eṉpatē yaṉṟi vaḷarvatu eṉṉum poruḷ poruntātu. panti eṉṉuñ colliṟku (aintu) pañcaṉ eṉṉum eṇṇup peyarum mūlamākātu.
pāl eṉṉuñ colliṟkup pakuti, ūḻ eṉṉum iru poruḷ iruppatupōṉṟē, pākku eṉṉum colliṟkum iruttal vēṇṭum. palaraṟiyār pākkiyattāl eṉṉum kuṟaḷaṭikku (1141), an nilaipēṟṟait teyvattāl yāṉē aṟivatallatu kūṟukiṉṟa palarum aṟiyār eṉṟu parimēlaḻakar uraittiruppatai nōkkuka. avar teyvam eṉṟatu pāl varai teyvattai.

pakuttaṟiviṉ payaṉ
ulakil vēṟu ennilappakutiyilum illātatum, intiyāvilum vaṭanāṭṭiṉun teṉṉāṭṭiṟ kaṭumaiyākavum kaṇṭippākavuṅ kaik koḷḷappaṭuvatum, teṉmoḻiyiṉattiṉ oṟṟumaiyaik kulaittu ataṉ valimaiyaik keṭuppataṟkē āriyarāṟ pukuttappaṭṭatum, ceyyun toḻiloṭu ciṟitun toṭarpaṟṟatum, pakuttaṟiviṟku muṟṟum muraṇāṉatum, āṉa piṟavikkulap piriviṉaiyai uṇarttum kulappaṭṭaṅkaḷai iṉṟun tamiḻar tāṅki varuvatu ikkālattiṟ kēṟkātatākum.
paṇṭait tamiḻakattil im mūṭavaḻakkam iruntatē yillai. ellā vakuppārum, piṟanāṭṭār pōl tam peyarai iṇaippu vāliṉṟi veṟumaiyākavē vaḻaṅki vantaṉar. aṟivum nākarikamum tuppuravum oḻukkamum uṇmaiyiṉmai kaḷē, uyarvu tāḻvaik kuṟittu vantaṉa. āriyap piṟavikkula vuyarvutāḻvait tiruvaḷḷuvar teḷivākak kaṇṭittār.
pakuttaṟiviyakkap pakalavaṉ eṉṉum periyār tam kaṟpippiṟ kēṟpa, piṟavikkula voḻuṅkai mīṟiyē oru peṇṇai maṇantār. avaraip piṉpaṟṟuvatākap pitaṟṟum palar, mataviyal oṉṟu tavira maṟṟavaṟṟil avar peyar keṭumāṟē oḻukivarukiṉṟaṉar. piṟavikkula vuṇarcciyaic collaḷavil nīkkiṉum uḷḷaḷavil nīkkavillai.
pakuttaṟiviyakkam tamiḻarellārkkum potuvāyiṉum, periyāraip piṉpaṟṟiyar allatu pakuttaṟivāḷar eṉṟu tammaic collik koḷvōr, tāmē muṉpu pakuttaṟivo ḻukkam pūṇṭu piṟarkku vaḻikāṭṭal vēṇṭum.
pakuttaṟivu viḷakkam
kumarināṭṭut tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūlāciriyaṉ, pakuttaṟi vaṭippaṭaiyilēyē, poruḷkaḷaiyellām uyartiṇai, aḵṟiṇaiyeṉa iraṇṭākap pakuttu, ulakam pōṟṟum oppuyarvaṟṟa ilakkaṇam vakuttirukkiṉṟāṉ.
kaṭavuḷ illaiyeṉpataṟkup pōṉṟē, uṇṭu eṉpataṟkum pakuttaṟivē tuṇai ceykiṉṟatu. ulakil ettuṇai nākarikap paṇpāṭṭu nāṭāyiṉum, iravil nakaraviḷakku araimaṇi nēram eriyātu pōyiṉum, aintu nimaiyam aracaṉ atikāram illātu pōyiṉum, kāṭṭuvilaṅkiṉuṅ kēṭāka nāṭṭumakkaḷ naṭantu koḷkiṉṟaṉar. aṅṅaṉamirukka, uyiraṟṟa paṉṉiru kōḷkaḷum paṟpala nāṇmīṉkaḷum iṭaiviṭātu peruveḷiyil oḻuṅkāy iyaṅki varuvatu iyakkuvāṉ oruvaṉiṉṟi iyalumō? kaṭavuḷuṇmaiyaik kāṭṭa itupōl vēṟu cāṉṟukaḷ ettaṇaiyō iruppiṉum, iḵtoṉṟē pōtumāṉatām.
āyiṉum, naṭunilaiyāka nōkki, irucārārum oruvarai yoruvar ikaḻātuṅ kuṟaikūṟātum iruppatē pakuttaṟiviṟ kaḻakām.
paccaik kutirai viḷaiyāṭṭu
ciṟuvar, kutiraipōṟ kuṉintu viḷaiyāṭum viḷaiyāṭṭuk kaḷuḷ oṉṟu paccaik kutirai. oru ciṟuvaṉ kuṉintu niṟpatu oru ciṟu kutiraipōl tōṉṟuvatāl, itu ippeyar peṟṟirukka lām. itil iruvakaiyuṇṭu.
I. oru vakai
palar varicaiyāka iṭaiyiṭṭuk kuṉintukoṇṭu niṟpar. avaruḷ oru kōṭiyil iruppavaṉ, piṟaraiyellān tāṇṭit tāṇṭi maṟukōṭiyiṟpōyk kuṉintu niṟpāṉ. iṅṅaṉamē piṟarum varicaippaṭi ovvoruvaṉāyt tāṇṭit tāṇṭi maṟukōṭiyiṟ pōy niṟpar. ivvāṟu āṭṭut toṭarat toṭara iṭam peyarntu koṇṭēyiruppar. inta āṭṭu, pala cuvarkaḷait toṭarntu tāṇṭutaṟkēṟṟa payiṟciyākum.
II. maṟṟoru vakai
ētēṉumoru tērnteṭuppil tavaṟiya ciṟuvaṉ oru kālai nīṭṭi uṭkārntiruppāṉ. avaṉatu nīṭṭiya kālaip piṟa ciṟuvar ovvoruvarāyt tāṇṭit tāṇṭic celvar. aṭutta muṟai avaṉ kālmēṟ kālvaittu nīṭṭiyiruppāṉ. ataiyum avvāṟē tāṇṭuvar, ataṟkaṭutta muṟai oru kai ataṉmēl vaittiruppaṉ. piṉpu, muṟaiyē, uṭkārnta nilaiyil tāḻak kuṉintum, annilaiyil caṟṟu nimirntum, eḻuntu niṉṟu tāḻak kuṉintum, annilaiyil caṟṟu nimirntum, ivvāṟu paṭip paṭiyāka uyarntukoṇṭiruppāṉ. piṟarum uṭaṉuṭaṉ tāṇṭik koṇṭē varuvar. ennilaiyilēṉum oruvaṉ tāṇṭamuṭi yātu niṉṟāl, piṉpu avaṉ kīḻē uṭkārntu muṉcoṉṉa vāṟu paṭippaṭiyāy uyarntukoṇṭirukka vēṇṭum. muṉpu kīḻēyiruntavaṉ piṉpu piṟaroṭu cērntu viḷaiyāṭuvāṉ. iṅṅaṉam āṭṭut toṭarum. ciṟuvarkku iḵtoru paṭimuṟai yuyarat tāṇṭaṟ payiṟciyām.
pañcāyp paṟattal 
vaṟumaiyāl varuntum māntar noyya uṭampuṭaṉ vaḷḷalārai nāṭi aṅkum iṅkum alaintu tirital ālilaiyum ilavam pañcum āṭikkāṟṟil aṅkum iṅkum paṟantu tirivatai okkum. itaṉāl ālāyp paṟattal, pañcāyp paṟattal eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷ eḻuntaṉa.
eḷimaiyaik kuṟikkum pañcai eṉṉum collum, vaṟkaṭattaik kuṟikkum pañcam eṉṉum collum pañcāyp paṟattal eṉṉum vaḻakkiṉiṉṟu eḻuntavaiyākum. pāvēntar kāṟṟil ilavam pañcākap paṟakkaiyilē eṉṟa paṭikkācup pulavar pāṭalaṭiyai nōkkuka.  col : 8, 9.

pañcu veṭṭuṅ kampaṭō viḷaiyāṭṭu

āṭuvār tokai : itai āṭa iruvar vēṇṭum.
āṭiṭam : maṇaṟ kuviyalil maṭṭum itu āṭappeṟum.
āṭumuṟai : oruvaṉ iṉṉoruvaṉai maṇaliṟ paṭukka vaittut tuṇiyāṟ pōrttu avaṉatu uṭaliṉ kīḻp pakutiyai maṇalāl mūṭi pañcuveṭṭuṅ kampaṭō tōlē tōlē, paruttiveṭṭuṅ kampaṭō tōlē tōlē eṉṟu maṭakki maṭakkic colli mella mella avaṉ mutukil taṭṭik koṇṭēyiruppaṉ. ciṟitu nēram poṟuttup paṭuttavaṉ eḻuntuviṭuvāṉ.
āṭṭut tōṟṟam : pōril tōṟṟōṭiṉavaruḷ oru cārār. tammait toṭarntu varum veṟṟi maṟavariṉiṉṟu tappikkoḷ ḷum poruṭṭu naṭitta naṭippukkaḷuḷ oṉṟaṉ pōliyāka, iv āṭṭut tōṉṟiyatōveṉa aiyuṟa iṭamuṇṭu.  (ta.nā.vi.)

paṭṭa viḷaiyāṭṭu
paṭṭam oruvaṉ āṭiṉāl poḻutupōkkām (Pastime); iruvar āṭiṉāl viḷaiyāṭṭām.
oruvaṉ paṭṭattiṟkumēl oruvaṉ paṭṭattaip paṟakkac ceyvatum, oṉṟaiyoṉṟu tākki vīḻttac ceyvatumē iv viḷaiyāṭṭām.
paṭṭi
paṭṭi - va. paṭṭi ma. paṭṭi, te. paṭṭi,
paṭṭikai - va. paṭṭikā
paṭṭutal = taṭṭutal. paṭṭu - paṭṭi = paṭṭai, mūṅkiṟ piḷāccu, ataṉālamainta toḻu, ēṭṭileḻutiya peyar varicai, nāyk kaḻuttuvār.
paṭṭi - paṭṭikai = araikkaccai, mēkalai, tuṇi, puṇkaṭṭuñ cīlai, ōka (yōka) ppaṭṭi, uṇṇāḻikaiyaic cuṟṟiyamaikkum aṇivēlai, paṭṭiyaṟkal, ēṭu, aracap paṭṭaiyam.
paṭṭi - paṭṭiyal = variccal, tūṇiṉ kīḻ vaikkuṅ kal, carakkup peyar varicai.
paṭṭai = taṭṭai, taṭṭaiyāṉa poruḷ, marappaṭṭai, maravuri, vāḻaippaṭṭai, ōlaippaṭṭai, maṇippaṭṭai, tōṭ paṭṭai, kaḻuttuppaṭṭai, miṉukai (carikai)ppaṭṭai, paṭṭaikkōṭu, paṭṭaiyāṉa tirunīṟṟuppūccu, paṭṭaittāṟu, paṭṭait taiyal.
te. paṭṭa, paṭṭe; ka. paṭṭe.
paṭṭai - paṭṭaiyam (paṭṭayam) = aracaṉ koṭukkum ceppāvaṇam.  (va.va : 196).
paṭakam
paṭakam - va. paṭaha (t´) = paṟai.
paṭakam = akamuḻavukaḷuḷ oṉṟu (cilap. 3 : 27, urai).
paṭam - va. paṭa (t´)
paṭṭu - paṭṭam = paṭṭaiyāṉa tuṇi. paṭṭam - paṭam = tuṇi. tuṇiyileḻutiya ōviyam, cittiraccīlai, tiraiccīlai, yāṉaimukapaṭām, pōrvai, caṭṭai, uṭal.
paṭam - paṭṭa
paṭam = yāṉaimukapaṭām. paṭam - paṭām. o.nō : kaṭam - kaṭām.
veṅkatak kaḷiṟṟiṉ paṭattiṉāl  (kalit. 89). (piṅ.)  (va.va.)
paṭam 
paṭam - paṭa (Phat´a), paṇam - paṇa (Phan´a)
paṭṭam - paṭam = (pāmpiṉ kaḻuttu virivāṉa) paṭam.
paṭam - paṇam. nāka paṇantikaḻ (tēvā. 84 : 4).
painnāp paṭavaravu  (tiruvāca. 34 : 1)  (va.va : 196 - 197.)
paṭarkkai i vikuti
maturaik kallūri yitaḻil ikara vikutiyaip paṟṟi varaiyappaṭṭuḷḷa pakuti varumāṟu :
tamiḻil peṇpāl vikuti
i eṉpatu tamiḻil peṇpāl vikuti eṉṟu kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. uṇmaiyil peṇpāl vikuti i alla. atu peṇpāl vikutiyāyiṉ villi, nāṟkāli mutaliyavaṟṟil ap poruḷil varātiruppatēṉ? uṇmai eṉṉaveṉṟāl, i eṉpatu tiri eṉpataṉ citaivu. perumpālum, tti, cci, ṭṭi eṉpavaiyē peṇpāl vikutikaḷāka varukiṉṟaṉa. ivai tiri eṉpataṉ māṟupaṭṭa uruvaṅkaḷ eṉpatu teḷivu. mutalil tiri eṉpatē peṇpāl vikutiyāka iruntu piṟaku atu citaintu tti, cci, ṭṭi eṉṟu māṟiyirukka vēṇṭum.
e-ṭu :
kuṟa + tiri = kuṟatti	pulai + tiri = pulaicci
veḷḷāṉ + tiri = veḷḷāṭṭi.	valai + tiri = valaicci.
vaṇṇāṉ + tiri = vaṇṇātti.	āy + tiri = āycci.
pārppāṉ + tiri = pārppātti	pirāmaṇa + tiri = pirāmaṇatti
rājā + tiri = rājātti	paṟai + tiri = paṟaicci
peṇṭu + ām + tiri = peṇṭāṭṭi.
kaṇ + ām + tiri = kaṇṇāṭṭi.
pommaṉāṭṭi. kammaṉāṭṭi eṉpavaiyum ivvāṟē vantirukkavēṇṭum.
iṉi itaṉ maṟuppu viḷakkam varumāṟu:
tamiḻil, āṇpāl peṇpāl vikutiyēṟpaṭāta mutukālattil. atāvatu uyartiṇai aḵṟiṇai eṉṉum tiṇai vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟāta paḻaṅkālattil, orumaippāl paṉmaippāl eṉṉum irupālkaḷē iru tiṇaikkum potuvāka vaḻaṅki vantaṉa. mutaṟkālattilēyē pakuttaṟivuḷḷatum illatum eṉap poruḷkaḷait tamiḻar iru pakuppākap pakuttirukka muṭiyātu. nākarikamum aṟivu vaḷarcciyumākiya paṇpāṭaṭainta piṉṉarē, avar avvāṟu ceytiruttal vēṇṭum. iṉṟum, irutiṇaikkum potuvākap pala coṟkaḷum vikutikaḷum vaḻaṅkak kāṇkiṉṟōm. avai muntukāla moḻinilaiyaik kāṭṭum eñcu kuṟikaḷākum.
e-ṭu :
coṟkaḷ
āṇ, peṇ, tāy, tantai, piḷḷai, kuṭṭi, tāṉ, tām.
vikutikaḷ
kattarippāṉ = kattarikkiṉṟavaṉ (uyartiṇai āṇpāl)
kattarippāṉ = kattarikkōl (aḵṟiṇai oṉṟaṉpāl)
kaṇṇi = kaṇṇaiyuṭaiyavaḷ (uyartiṇaip peṇpāl)
kaṇṇi = kaṇṇaiyuṭaiyatu (aḵṟiṇai oṉṟaṉpāl)
kolli = kolkiṉṟavaṉ (uyartiṇai āṇpāl)
kolli = kolkiṉṟavaḷ (uyartiṇaip peṇpāl)
kolli = kolkiṉṟatu (aḵṟiṇai oṉṟaṉpāl)
irutiṇaic coṟkum ōraṉṉa urimaiyiṉ
tiripu vēṟupaṭūum ellāp peyarum
niṉaiyuṅ kālait tattam marapiṉ
viṉaiyō ṭallatu pālteri pilavē  (657)
iyaṟpeyar ciṉaippeyar ciṉaimutaṟ peyarē
muṟaippeyark kiḷavi tāmē tāṉē
ellām nīyir nīeṉak kiḷantu
colliya alla piṟavum āaṅku
aṉṉavai tōṉṟiṉ avaṟṟoṭuṅ koḷalē  (659)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyac cūttiraṅkaḷ iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkaṉa.
pālkāṭṭum vikutikaḷ, iṉṟu eṅṅaṉam perumpālum cuṭṭup peyarkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ kuṟukkamuṅ kuṟaiyumāyirukkiṉṟaṉavō, aṅṅaṉamē muṟkālattum iruntaṉa.
e-ṭu :	atu - valatu, taṉatu, uḷatu, nallatu.
	itu - veḷitu, valitu, karitu, putitu.
	utu - viḻutu, paḻutu, erutu (ērutu), marutu.
	avaṉ - āṉ - aṉ: nallavaṉ - nallāṉ - nallaṉ.
	avar - ār - ar: vallavar - vallār - vallar.
	ivar - īr - ir: (makaḷivar) - (makaḷīr) - makaḷir.
ikara vukaravaṭi vikutikaḷ ikkālattup perumpālum vaḻakkiṟantaṉa.
tamiḻiṉ acainilaik kālattil (Monosyllabic or Isolating Stage), cuṭṭuccoṟkaḷ vikuti peṟāmal cuṭṭaṭiyākavē vaḻaṅki vantaṉa. iṉṟu vaḻaṅkum palaviṉpāl akaravikuti muntunilaiyai yuṇarttum aṭaiyāḷamām.
e-ṭu	:	nalla = nallavai (peyar)
		vanta = vantaṉa (viṉaimuṟṟu)
oppa nāṭi attaka voṟutti  (puṟam. 10)
eṉṉum aṭiyil, attaka eṉpatu ataṟkuttaka eṉṟu poruḷpaṭutalāl, acainilaikkālat tuvakkattil, cuṭṭacaic coṟkaḷ eṇṇuṇarttātu cuṭṭup poruḷoṉṟē uṇartti vantaṉa eṉa ūkikka iṭamuṇṭu.
cēymai, aṇmai, muṉmai ākiya mūvakaiyiṭaṅkaḷaiyum muṟaiyē cuṭṭum a, i, u (allatu ā, ī, ū) eṉpaṉa, acai nilaik kālattil taṉiccollēyākavum, ataṟkaṭutta puṇar nilaikkālattil (Compounding Stage) taṉiccollum kūṭṭuc collumākavum, ataṟkaṭutta vikutinilaikkālattil (Inflexional Stage) taṉic collum kūṭṭuc collum vikutiyākavum vaḻaṅki vantu, iṉṟu nālāṅkālamākiya koḷuvunilaik kālattiṉ (Agglutinative Stage) piṟpakutiyil, taṉiccollāy vaḻaṅkuva toḻintu ēṉai yiruvakai yākavē vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
eccollum vēṟoru colliṉ vikutiyāka amaintavuṭaṉ, taṉ vitappup poruḷai iḻantuviṭum. ākavē, cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷum vikutiyāyamaintapiṉ tam cuṭṭupporuḷaiyiḻantu pālmaṭṭum uṇartti niṟkum.
orumaikkum paṉmaikkum vevvēṟu vikutikaḷ vakukkappaṭṭa kālattil, ikaraccuṭṭu orumai vikutikaḷuḷ oṉṟākak koḷḷap peṟṟatu. aṉṟu āṇ peṇ pālvēṟupāṭu kuṟikkappeṟāta kālamātalāl, āṇpāl peṇpāl oṉṟaṉpāl ākiya mup pālukkum potuvākavē ikaraccuṭṭu vaḻaṅkat talaippaṭṭu, aṉṟiruntu ammuṟaiyē toṭarntu varukiṉṟatu. (peṇmakaḷ eṉṉum māṟōkkattu vaḻakkum peṭṭaip paiyaṉ eṉṉum vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakkum, āṇ pāl peṇpāl vikutiyēṟ paṭāta muntukāla moḻinilaiyai ūkittuṇara vutavum).
e-ṭu :	viṟakuveṭṭi (āṇpāl)
	maṇveṭṭi (oṉṟaṉpāl)
	kayaṟkaṇṇi (peṇpāl)
	kāṭaikkaṇṇi (oṉṟaṉpāl)
ākavē, ikara vikuti orumai vikutiyēyaṉṟip peṇpāl vikutiyaṉṟu. atu oru kuṟitta collil mēṟkūṟiya muppāluḷ oṉṟai uṇarttumē yaṉṟit taṉitta nilaiyil uṇarttātu. kaṇṇaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ coṟku nērāṉa peṇpāṟ col. kaṇṇaḷ eṉpatē yaṉṟik kaṇṇi eṉpataṉṟu. kaṇṇāṉ eṉpatu kaṇṇaṉ eṉṟu kuṟukuvatu pōlak kaṇṇāḷ eṉpatum kaṇṇaḷ eṉṟu kuṟukum. vantāṉ eṉpataṟku vantāḷ eṉpatu eṅṅaṉam peṇpālō, aṅṅaṉamē kaṇṇaṉ eṉpa taṟkum kaṇṇaḷ eṉpatē nērāṉa peṇpālām. kaṇṇāḷ allatu kaṇṇaḷ eṉṟu colvataṟkup patilākak kaṇṇi eṉṟu colvatu vaḻakkūṉṟiviṭṭataṉāl. ikara vikutiyaip peṇpāṟ ciṟappu vikutiyeṉṟu palar mayaṅka nērntuviṭṭatu. av vikuti peṇpāl vikutiyāyirukkak kūṭiyatē yaṉṟip peṇpālukkē yuriya vikutiyaṉṟu.
iv vikuti aḷ vikutiyaiviṭap palukkuvataṟku (uccarippa taṟku) eḷitāyum iṉṉōcaiyuḷḷatāyu miruttalē, atu peru vaḻakkāy vaḻaṅkuvataṟkuk kāraṇamākum. kaṇṇaḷ kaṇṇi eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷaiyum palukkik kāṇka. ik kāraṇam paṟṟiyē, tāyaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷuṭ cila aḷ vikutiyai viṭṭu i v vikutiyai ēṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
e-ṭu :	āṇpāl (tantai)	peṇpāl (tāy)
	attaṉ	atti
	accaṉ	acci
atti eṉpatoṭu toṭarpuḷḷa attai eṉṉum peyar. attaṉiṉ (tantaiyiṉ) uṭaṉpiṟantāḷaik kuṟikkum. attaṉ eṉpataṉ tiripē accaṉ eṉpatu. accaṉ eṉṉum āṇpāṟ peyar cēranāṭāṉa malaiyāḷa nāṭṭilum, acci eṉṉum peṇpāṟ peyar pāṇṭināṭṭilum, vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
acci = akkai (pāṇṭināṭṭu vaḻakku)
akkai tāy pōṉṟavaḷātalāl acci eṉappaṭṭāḷ.
o.nō : tam + avvai (tāy) = tavvai = tamakkai.
accu (accaṉ) acci eṉṉum peyarkaḷ, toṭarmoḻi yuṟuppāka, muṟaiyē tāy tantaip poruḷil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
appaccu = appaṉiṉ tantai = pāṭṭaṉ (pāṇṭināṭṭu vaḻakku)
ammācci = ammaiyiṉ tāy = pāṭṭi (cōḻanāṭṭu vaḻakku)
appaccu eṉpatu appacci eṉat tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
arumaiyum perumaiyumpaṟṟi, paruvamaṭaintuḷḷa āṭavarai yum peṇṭirai yum tāytantaiyaraik kuṟikkum muṟaip peyarāl aḻaikkum vaḻakkam, nam nāṭṭil toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntu varuvatāl. ap peyarkaḷ āṇpāṟ peṇpāṟ peyarīṟṟut taṉmaiyaṭaintuḷḷaṉa. ōr āṭavarai aiyā eṉṟum, oru peṇṭai ammā eṉṟum aḻaittal kāṇka. ammai allatu ammā eṉṉum peyar, iyaṟpeyarum toḻilpaṟṟiya potuppeyarumāṉa peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷiṉ īṟākap peruvaḻakkāy vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
e-ṭu :	poṉṉammai, poṉṉammā
	tāyammai, tāyammā		iyaṟpeyar
	vāttiyārammā, ṭākṭar ammā -	toḻil paṟṟiya peṇpāṟ potup peyar.
iṅṅaṉamē, atti acci eṉṉum tāymuṟaip peyarkaḷum, kulamum toḻilum kuṇamum niṟamum nilaimaiyum piṟavum paṟṟiya peṇpāṟ potuppeyarkaḷiṉ īṟāka vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
e-ṭu :	kuṟa + atti = kuṟatti
	taṭṭā(ṉ) + atti = taṭṭātti		- 	kulam.
	paṟai + acci = paṟaicci
	valai + acci = valaicci
	upātti + acci = upātticci
	maruttuva + acci = maruttuvacci	-	toḻil
	maṭai + acci = maṭaicci		-	taṉmai
	veḷḷai + acci = veḷḷaicci	- 	niṟam
	muṇṭai + acci = muṇṭaicci
	āṇṭi + acci = āṇṭicci		-	nilaimai
	pēy + acci = pēycci
	neṭṭai + acci = neṭṭaicci
	kuḷḷa + acci = kuḷḷacci		-	aḷavu
	kuppa + acci = kuppacci		-	iṭam
	caṭai + acci = caṭaicci		-	uṟuppu
	ciṟṟiṭai + acci = ciṟṟiṭaicci
veḷḷaicci, pēycci, kuppacci, caṭaicci mutaliya peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ, nāṭṭuppuṟaṅkaḷil iyaṟpeyarāka iṭappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
atti acci eṉṉum peyarkaḷ īṟāka niṉṟu puṇarumpōtu. avaṟṟiṉ akaramutal keṭṭuviṭukiṉṟatu. keṭātu niṟpatum uṇṭu.
e-ṭu : kaḷḷavatti
veḷḷaicci kuppacci eṉṉum peyarkaḷai veḷḷaiyammā kuppammā eṉpavaṟṟuṭaṉ oppunōkkuka.
atti eṉṉum colliṉ kuṟaiyāṉa ti īṟu, nilaimoḻi yīṟṟu ḷakara meyyōṭu puṇarumpōtu, av viraṇṭum ṭṭi eṉat tiriyum. atu eṉṉum colliṉ kuṟaiyāṉa tu vvīṟum, iṅṅaṉamē ṭuvvīṟākat tirital kāṇka. (tāḷ + tu = tāṭṭu.)
āḷaṉ (āḷ + aṉ) eṉṉum āṇpālīṟṟiṟku etirāka āṭṭi (āḷ + ti) eṉpatu peṇpālīṟāy varum.
e-ṭu :	veḷḷāḷaṉ - veḷḷāṭṭi
			kaṇṇāḷaṉ - kaṇṇāṭṭi
			maṇavāḷaṉ - maṇavāṭṭi
			tiruvāḷaṉ - tiruvāṭṭi
			kuṇavāḷaṉ - kuṇavāṭṭi
			kuṇāḷaṉ - kuṇāṭṭi
veḷḷāḷaṉ eṉpatai veḷḷāṉ eṉpatu tavaṟu.
makaṉ eṉṉum āṇpālīṟu māṉ eṉṟu maruvuvatu pōl, makaḷ eṉṉum peṇpālīṟu māḷ eṉṟu maruvum.
e-ṭu :	perumakaṉ - perumāṉ, kuṟumakaṉ - kuṟumāṉ, marumakaṉ - marumāṉ, tirumakaṉ - tirumāṉ, veḷimakaṉ - veḷimāṉ.
	perumakaḷ - perumāḷ, vēṇmakaḷ - vēṇmāḷ, tirumakaḷ - tirumāḷ.
māḷ eṉṉum peṇpālīṟṟoṭu mēṟkāṭṭiya ti eṉṉum īṟum cērumpōtu, av viraṇṭumē māṭṭi eṉat tiriyum.
e-ṭu :	āṇpāl	peṇpāl
	perumāṉ	perumāṭṭi
	tirumāṉ	tirumāṭṭi
	cīmāṉ	cīmāṭṭi
perumāṉ eṉpatu pirāṉ eṉṟu maruvumpōtu, perumāṭṭi eṉpatu pirāṭṭi eṉa maruvum. perumāḷ tirumāḷ eṉṉum oṟṟaiyīṟṟup peṇpāṟ peyarkaḷ iṉṟu vaḻakkiṟantaṉa. vikutimēl vikuti vantu iraṭṭaippaṉmaip peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkuvatu pōṉṟē, īṟṟiṉmēl īṟu vantu iraṭṭaip peṇmaip peyarkaḷum vaḻaṅkum.
e-ṭu : veḷḷāṭṭi + acci = veḷḷāṭṭicci.
peṇṭāṭṭi (peṇṭu + āṭṭi) eṉpatiluḷḷa āṭṭi īṟu maṉaivip poruḷ kuṟikka vantatākum. peṇ, peṇṭu eṉpaṉa iṭamnōkki maṉaivip poruḷuṇarttum peṇpāṟ potuppeyarām.
kai + peṇ = kaimpeṇ.
kai + peṇṭu = kaimpeṇṭu
kai + peṇṭāṭṭi = kaimpeṇṭāṭṭi
peṇpeṇṭāṭṭi eṉpatu, āṇpiḷḷaip piḷḷai kārāṉ pacu araiñāṇ kayiṟu eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa mikaipaṭu coṟṟoṭar.
oru peṇpeṇṭāṭṭi āṇuṭai yuṭuttu  (īṭu. 6 : 2.).
kaimpeṇṭāṭṭi peṇpeṇṭāṭṭi eṉpaṉa, muṟaiyē, kammaṉāṭṭi pommaṉāṭṭi eṉak koccai vaḻakkil maruvi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
atti acci eṉṉum īṟukaḷ peṇpāl maṭṭum uṇartti uyarvu kuṟikkāmaiyāl, taṭṭāttiyammā, upātticciyammā mutaliya peyarkaḷ uyarvu kuṟikka vanta iraṭṭaip peṇpāṟ peyarākum.
appaṉ, aiyaṉ eṉṉum tantai muṟaip peyarkaḷ, aruḷappaṉ, kaṇṇappaṉ, cellappaṉ, nākappaṉ, aruḷaiyaṉ, kaṇṇaiyaṉ, cellaiyaṉ, nākaiyaṉ eṉa evvāṟu āṇpāṟ peyarīṟukaḷāy āḷappeṟukiṉṟaṉavō; avvāṟē, ammai, atti, acci eṉṉum tāymuṟaip peyarkaḷum peṇpāṟ peyarīṟukaḷāy āḷappeṟum eṉka.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, tamiḻil peṇpāl kuṟittu varum tti, cci, ṭi (ṭṭi) eṉṉum īṟukaḷellām. atti acci eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷiṉ kuṟaiyum tiripumēyaṉṟi, tiri eṉṉum vaṭacoṟ citaivalla eṉṟu teṟṟeṉak kaṇṭu koḷka.  ( centamiḻc celvi ākattu 1951.)
paṭalam 
paṭalam - va. paṭala (t´)
paṭartal = paravutal. paṭam - paṭal = ōlai taṭṭai māṟu mutaliyavaṟṟālākiya katavupōṉṟa aṭaippu, uḻuta nilattiṟ parampaṭikkum paranta māṟu, kaṇṇiṟ paṭarum purai.  (va.va : 197.).
paṭām 
paṭām : paṭṭutal taṭṭutal, paṭṭu - paṭṭai = taṭṭaiyākat taṭṭappaṭṭatu. paṭṭu - paṭṭam = paṭṭaiyāṉa takaṭu, paṭṭaiyāṉa tuṇi, paṭṭam - paṭam = tuṇi. tuṇiyileḻutiya ōviyam, cittirac cīlai, yāṉai mukapaṭām. paṭam - paṭām. 
o.nō : kaṭam - kaṭām. paṭam - paṭa (va.). paṭam (paṭām) - paṭṭa (va.).  ti.ma : 747.
paṭi 
paṭi - va. paṭ (t´h)
paṭutal = olittal. paṭu - paṭi = oliyeḻa vāyittal (vācittal).
o. nō : ōtai - ōcai. ōtutal = paṭittal. paṭi - pāṭam.
paṭi - va. prati
paḷ - paṭu. paṭutal = viḻutal. paṭu - paṭi. paṭital = oṉṟiṉmēl viḻutal, viḻuntu patital, patintu uruvam amaital.
nilattiṟ patinta poruḷiṉ vaṭivam nilattilum, tāḷiṟ patinta acciṉ vaṭivam tāḷilum, amaital kāṇka.
paṭi = 1. uruvam mayilaṉārkkup paṭivaittu (cīvaka. 1156).
2. uṭampu.niṉaiyāravaṉmaippaṭiyē(tiv.iyaṟ.  tiruvirut. 93).
paṭinta uruvam paṭinta poruḷai ottiruttalāl, paṭi eṉṉum col oppumaik karuttai uṇarttiṟṟu.
appaṭi = atupōl. paṭiyoruva rillāp paṭiyār pōlum (tēvā. 44 : 7).
paṭi = otta vaṭivam (True copy). kiḻitta vōlai paṭiyōlai (periyapu. taṭuttāṭ. 56).
mūlamum paṭiyum eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
paṭi - paṭivu - paṭivam. paṭi - paṭimai - paṭimam.
paṭi - vaṭi (koccai). paṭivu - vaṭivu. paṭivam - vaṭivam.  (va.va : 197).
paṭivam 
paṭimai - va. pratimā
paṭi - paṭimai = 1. vaṭivam kaṭṭaḷaip paṭimaiyiṟ paṭiyātu (cīvaka. 2752). 2. pāvai. 3. vaḻipaṭu teyva vuruvam. 4. nōṉpu, tavam. palpukaḻ niṟutta paṭimai yōṉē  (tol. ciṟappup.)
pratimā - patumai - pommai.  (va.va : 197-198.)
paṭivam : paṭutal = viḻutal. paṭu - paṭi. paṭital = oṉṟiṉmēl iṉṉoṉṟu viḻutal, viḻuntu patital, patintu uruva vaṭivam amaital.
paṭi = uruvam, uṭampu, oppu. appaṭi = atupōl.
paṭi - paṭivu - paṭivam. paṭi - paṭimai - paṭimam.
paṭi - vaṭi, paṭivu - vaṭivu. paṭivam - vaṭivam.
paṭi - prati (va.). paṭimai - pratimā (va.).  (ti.ma. 747.)
paṭu 
paṭu eṉṉum col perumai allatu mikutip poruḷ taruvataip paṭukuḻi, paṭutaṇṭam, paṭupoy, paṭumōcam eṉṉum vaḻakku nōkki yuṇarka.  (kuṟaḷ. 676).
paṭaiñaṉ iyalpu 
paṭaiñar pōril orukāl tam uyiriḻakkumāṟu pakaivaraik kollun toḻilai allatu vellun toḻilai mēṟkoṇṭiruppataṉālum avarkaḷiṉ nāṭṭut toṇṭum nilaiyāmaic ciṟappum nōkki avarkaṭkuk koṭukkap paṭṭirukkum urimai allatu oḻukkavitit taḷarcciyiṉālum, avarkaḷ piṟarporuḷai virumpiyaviṭattu avaraik kēḷāmalē eṭuttuk koḷvatu vaḻakkam. itaṟkut tārāḷam eṉṟu peyar. tār eṉpatu cēṉai. āḷam eṉpatu āḷiṉ taṉmai. tārāḷ, paṭaiñaṉ. tārāḷam oruvaraik kēḷāmalē avar poruḷai eṭuttuk koḷḷum paṭaiñaṉ iyalpu. oruvarkkuriya iṭattil avaraik kēḷāmaṟ pukuvatum tārāḷam eṉappaṭum. poruḷai vauvuvatum urimaiyai vauvuvatum oṉṟē.  (col : 112.)
paṭaiyum pātukāppum
1. paṭai
oru nāṭṭu araciyaṟkup paṭaiyē aṭippaṭai. paṭaiyiruppiṉ, nāṭillātavaṉum nāṭṭaip peṟalām; paṭaiyillāviṭiṉ, nāṭuṭaiyavaṉum atai iḻappāṉ. ātaliṉ, paṭaiyāṉatu aracaṉukku iṉṟiyamaiyāta vuṟuppākum. itaṉālēyē aracarkkuriyavaṟṟaik kūṟum tolkāppiyac cūttirattilum (1571). aracuṟuppukkaḷaik kūṟum kuṟaḷilum (381). ciṟappuṭaip poruḷai muṟpaṭak kiḷattal eṉṉum uttipaṟṟip paṭai muṟkūṟap peṟṟatu. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ cēṉaiyai aracaṉ tirumēṉi eṉṟaṉar (cilap 25 : 191). aracar toḻilākiya kāval pōr eṉṉumiraṇṭum paṭaiyiṉṟi yamaiyāmaiyiṉ,
uṟuppamain tūṟañcā velpaṭai vēntaṉ
veṟukkaiyu ḷellān talai  (kuṟaḷ. 761).
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
paṭai vakaikaḷ : paṭaiyāṉatu. porum iṭampaṟṟi,
(1)	nilappaṭai (Military)
(2)	kaṭaṟpaṭai (Navy),
eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ nilappaṭai, ūrtipaṟṟi.
(1)	karippaṭai.
(2)	parippaṭai.
(3)	tērppaṭai.
(4)	kāṟpaṭai allatu kālāṭpaṭai,
eṉa nālvakaippaṭum. ivaṟṟiṉ, kālāṭpaṭai mīṇṭum karuvipaṟṟi,
(1)	viṟpaṭai.
(2)	vāṭpaṭai.
(3)	vēṟpaṭai.
eṉa  mūvakaippaṭum. eppaṭaiyarāyiṉum. pōrmaṟavarkkup porunar eṉṟum paṭaiyāṭkaḷ eṉṟum paṭaiñar eṉṟum peyar.
mūvēntariṭattum toṉṟutoṭṭuk kalappaṭai (kaṭaṟpaṭai) yiruntu vantatu.
vāṉiyainta irumunnīrp
pēe nilai iya irumpavvattuk
koṭum puṇari vilaṅku pōḻak
kaṭuṅkāloṭu karaicēra
neṭuṅkoṭimicai yitaiyeṭut
tiṉṉicaiya muracu muḻaṅkap
poṉmalinta viḻuppaṇṭam
nāṭāra naṉ kiḻitarum
āṭiyaṟ perunāvāy
maḻaimuṟṟiya malaipuraiyat
tuṟaimuṟṟiya tuḷaṅkirukkait
teṉkaṭaṟ kuṇṭakaḻic
cīrcāṉṟa vuyarnelliṉ
ūrkoṇṭa vuyarkoṟṟava
eṉṟu, varalāṟṟuk kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭa vaṭimpalampaniṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ, kalappaṭai koṇṭu cāli eṉṉum cāvakat tīvai (java) veṉṟa ceytiyai, avaṉ vaḻiyiṉaṉāṉa talaiyālaṅ kāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñceḻiyaṉ mēlēṟṟi maturaik kāñci (75-88) kūṟuvataiyum;
ciṉamiku tāṉai vāṉavaṉ kuṭakaṭaṟ
polantaru nāvā yōṭṭiya ñāṉṟaip
piṟarkalañ celkalā taṉaiyēm
eṉṟu, ki.pi.2ām nūṟṟāṇṭilirunta cēramāṉ kaṭalōṭṭiya vēlkeḻu kuṭṭuvaṉ ceyta kaṭaṟpōraip paṟṟip puṟac ceyyuḷ (126) kūṟuvataiyum; ki.pi.10ām 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷi liruntē mutalām irācarācac cōḻaṉ, īḻattaiyum munnīrp paḻantīvu paṉṉīrāyirattaiyum  (Maldive islands) kalappaṭai koṇṭu veṉṟu, cēranāṭṭuk kāntaḷūrccālai kalamaṟut taruḷiyataiyum, avaṉ makaṉ irācēntiraṉ nakkavāram (Nicobar). malaiyā (Malaya), cumaturā (Sumatra) mutaliyavaṟṟai veṉṟa taiyum nōkkuka.
muttamiḻaracariṭamum yāṉaippaṭai mikutiyāyiruntatu. cōḻa vēntaṉiṭam aṟupatiṉāyiram yāṉaikaḷ iruntatāka oru cīṉa vaḻippōkkaṉ kūṟiyiruppatāl, yāṉai iyalpākak kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāy vāḻum kuṭamalait toṭaraiyuṭaiya cēraṉiṭattum pāṇṭiyaṉiṭattum, avai ettuṇaip peruntokai yiṉavāy iruntiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpatai ūkittuṇarntu koḷḷalām.
yāṉai maṟavarkku, āṉaiyāṭkaḷ āṉaippākar kuñcaramallar attimallar eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. aracaṉ aṭikkaṭi yāṉaippaṭaiyai aṇivakuppittuk kaṇṭu kaḷippaṉ. yāṉaippaṭai akkālattuc ciṟanta paṭaiyākak karutappaṭṭatu.
yāṉaiyuṭaiya paṭaikāṇṭal mikaviṉitē
eṉṟār pūtañcēntaṉārum.
kutirai tamiḻakattiṟku allatu intu tēyattiṟku uriya vilaṅkaṉṟātaliṉ, atu ataṉ iyaṟkai vāḻakamāṉa arapi nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu ērāḷamāka māperuñ celavil varuvikkap paṭṭatu.
parañcōti muṉivar tiruviḷaiyāṭaṟpurāṇam kutiraikaḷaip puravi, pāṭalam, kōṭakam, ivuḷi, vaṉṉi, kutirai, pari, kantukam eṉa eṭṭuvakaiyāka vakuttuk kūṟum. (naripariyākkiya paṭalam, 87-94)
kutiraimaṟavar, ivuḷimaṟavar eṉṟum kutiraic cēvakar eṉṟuṅ kūṟappaṭuvar.
paṭaikku eṉṟum cēṅkutiraikaḷum kaḷiṟṟiyāṉaikaḷumē payaṉpaṭuttap peṟum. viṉaiyiṟciṟanta kutiraikaṭkum yāṉaikaṭkum ciṟappup peyariṭap paṭuvatuṇṭu.
tērppaṭai caṅkakālattiṟkup piṉ payaṉpaṭuttap peṟavillai.
iṉi, paṭaiyai, mēṟkūṟiya muṟaiyilaṉṟi, ūḻiyak kāla aḷavu paṟṟi, mūlappaṭai kūlippaṭai yeṉṟum; tokukkum iṭam paṟṟi. nāṭṭuppaṭai kāṭṭuppaṭai eṉṟum; cērntuḷḷa pakkam paṟṟi, tuṇaippaṭai pakaippaṭai eṉṟum; etirnilai vakaiyil ivviraṇṭāka vakuppatumuṇṭu.
mūlappaṭaiyai vaḷḷuvar tolpaṭai eṉpar. vāḻaiyaṭi vāḻaiyāka iruntu varuvatum, evvakai ūṟṟaiyum poruṭpaṭuttātatum, iṟappiṟku añcātatum, pōraiyē virumpuvatum, aracaṉaik kākka eṉṟum uyiruvantīvatum, ekkāraṇattaiyiṭṭum aṟaipōkātatum, veṉṟē mīḷvatum tolpaṭait tiṟaṅkaḷām.
uṭpakai orutiṟam paṭṭeṉap puṭpakaik
kēvā ṉākaliṟ cāvēm yāmeṉa
nīṅkā maṟavar vīṅkutōḷ puṭaippa  (puṟam - 68)
eṉṟu kōvūr kiḻārum.
uṟiṉuyi rañcā maṟavar iṟaivaṉ
ceṟiṉuñcīr kuṉṟa lilar
eṉṟu vaḷḷuvaṉārum, kūṟiyiruttalāl;  nīṇṭa nāḷākap pōr peṟāta tolpaṭai taṉ maṟamikaiyāl tāṉē pōrukku muṉaivatum, aracaṉ taṭuppiṉum mīṟuvatum uṇṭeṉpataṟiyap paṭum.
kūlippaṭaiyāvatu, pōrkkālattil putuvatākak kūlik
kamarttap paṭuvatu. nāṭṭuppaṭai marutanilamakkaḷ cērntatu. kāṭṭuppaṭai, pālainilavācikaḷum kuṟiñci nilamāntarum cērntatu. aracapattiyālum nāṭṭup paṟṟālum, kūlikkaṉṟit toṇṭu kāraṇamākat tāmāka vantutavum ūrmakkaḷ paṭai kuṭippaṭai eṉappaṭum.
pōrkkālattup perumpaṭai amaitikkālattu vēṇṭā vātaliṉ. pōr muṭinta piṉ mūlappaṭaiyoḻinta piṟa vellām kalaikkappaṭuvatu marapu. aṉṟu mūlappaṭaip pakutikaḷ talainakarilum, piṟa kōnakarkaḷilum ellaip puṟaṅkaḷilum, putitāy vellappaṭṭa pakaivar nāṭṭilum niṟuttap peṟum, aṅṅaṉam niṟuttap peṟṟavai nilaippaṭai eṉap peyar peṟum, mutaṟ kulōttuṅkaṉ kōṭṭāṟṟil oru nilaippaṭaiyai niṟuttiyiruntāṉ, kalaikkappaṭṭa paṭaiyiṉar tam maraput toḻilaic ceytuvaruvar.
nilaippaṭai niṟuttappaṭṭa nakar paṭaivīṭu eṉṟum atu niṟuttappaṭṭa nilappakuti paṭaippaṟṟu eṉṟum, atu kuṭiyirukkum vīṭṭut tokuti paṭainilai eṉṟum, peyar peṟum. nilaippaṭaiyiṉar maṉaivi makkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭi vāḻvar.
paṭaippayiṟci: aracarum paṭaimaṟavarum maṟpōr paṭaikkalap pōr ākiya iruvakaip pōrum payiṉṟu vantaṉar.
maṟpōr payilvataṟku āṅkāṅku pōravai allatu muraṇkaḷari eṉappaṭum payiṟcik kūṭaṅkaḷiruntaṉa. kōp perunaṟkaḷḷi eṉṉuñ cōḻaṉ oru pōravai naṭatti vantatāl pōravaik kōpperunaṟkiḷḷi eṉappaṭṭāṉ.

viyantaṉṟum iḻintaṉṟum ilaṉē avarai
aḻuntap paṟṟi akal vicum pārppeḻak
kaviḻntunilañ cēra aṭṭatai
makiḻntaṉṟu malintaṉṟum ataṉiṉu milaṉē  (puṟam-77).

maintuṭai mallaṉ matavali murukki
orukāl mārpotuṅ kiṉṟē orukāl
varutār tāṅkip piṉṉotuṅ kiṉṟē
