oruvaṉiṭattil oṉṟaik kēṭkum pōtu, tāḻntavaṉ, ī eṉṟum, ottavaṉ, tā eṉṟum, uyarntavaṉ, koṭu eṉṟum, collavēṇṭumeṉpatu tamiḻ marapu.
oṉṟupaṭuttal
yāṉaikkum tēṉ vaṇṭiṟkum mukattiṉ muṉ ōr uṟiñci (Proboscis) uḷḷatu. inta oppumaiyaik kaṇṭa mutaṟkālat tamiḻar, iraṇṭiṟkum tumpi eṉap potup peyar iṭṭaṉar. tūmpu - tumpu - tumpi = tūmpu (kuḻāy) pōṉṟa uṟuppaiyuṭaiyatu.
iṉappaṭuttal
oru poruḷai, uṭampiṉ allatu uṟuppiṉ vaṭivil, otta vēṟu poruḷkaḷaik kaṇṭa paṇṭait tamiḻar, muṉṉataṉ peyarkku aṭai koṭuttup piṉṉavaṟṟiṟkiṭṭu, avaṟṟai yellām ōriṉappaṭuttiyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
e.ṭu : vēmpu, kaṟivēmpu, nāyvēmpu, nilavēmpu, nīrvēmpu.
vēmpaip piṟavaṟṟōṭu oppunōkki nalla vēmpu eṉṟaṉar.
iṭukuṟiyiṉmai
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē eṉpatu tolkāppiyam (640). āṉāl, pala coṟkaṭkup pārtta maṭṭil poruḷ tōṉṟātu. avaṟṟai āḻntu ārāya vēṇṭum. itaṉaiyē, moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (877) kuṟippiṭukiṉṟatu.
palā, paṉai, poṉ, maram mutaliyavaṟṟai moḻi nūlaṟivum collā rāycciyumillā ilakkaṇavuraiyāciriyar, iṭukuṟi yeṉṟu kuṟippatu vaḻakkam.
palā parutta paḻattaiyuṭaiyatu. pal - paru - parumai.
o. nō : cil - ciṉ - ciṟu. ciṉmai = ciṟumai.
ciṟukōṭṭup perumpaḻam tūṅki yāṅku.  (kuṟun. 18).
paṉai karukkumaṭṭaiyuṭaiyatu. pal - paṉ = aṟuvāṭ pal, paṉṉaṟuvāḷ, paṉvaittal eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷai nōkkuka. paṉ - paṉai = kūriya paṟpōṉṟa karukkuḷḷatu.
poṉ poṟpu allatu polivuḷḷatu. pol - poli - polivu. pol - poṟpu = aḻaku. pol - polam - polaṉ. pol - poṉ. poṟṟa = aḻakiya (cīvaka. 270). pollā = aḻakillāta (auvaiyār). poṟṟatu = polivuṟṟatu 
(cīvaka. 649).
maram uṇarcciyaṟṟatu. kāl uṇarcciyaṟṟāl, marattup pōyviṭṭatu eṉpatu vaḻakkam. uṇarcciyaṟṟavaṉai maram pōlirukkiṉṟāṉ eṉpar. matamatappu = uṇarcciyiṉmai, timir. matam - maram. matattal = marattal.
o. nō : vitai - virai.
iṭukuṟiyillāmalē oru perun tāymoḻiyai ākkiyatu, paṇṭait tamiḻariṉ nuṇmāṇ nuḻaipulattaic ciṟappak kāṭṭum.
iyaṟkaic collākkam
tamiḻil ellāc coṟkaḷum iyaṟkaiyāṉa muṟaiyil amaintavai. vaṭamoḻiyil ā eṉṉum muṉṉoṭṭāl etirpporuḷ viṉaiccoṟkaḷai amaittuk koḷvar.
e.ṭu : kaccati = celkiṟāṉ. ākaccati = varukiṟāṉ. tattē = koṭukkiṟāṉ, ātattē = eṭukkiṟāṉ. ittakaiya ceyaṟkaiyamaippu tamiḻil illai.
tūymai
kumarik kaṇṭattil tamiḻ tavira vēṟoru moḻiyum vaḻaṅkavillai. veḷināṭṭiliruntu poruḷkaḷ variṉ, avaṟṟiṟku uṭaṉuṭaṉ tūya tamiḻp peyarkaḷ amaikkap paṭṭaṉa.
karumpu cīṉa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu atikamāṉiṉ muṉṉōrāṟ koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu.
amararp pēṇiyum āvuti yaruttiyum
 aruppeṟaṉ marapiṟ karumpivaṭ ṭantum
 nīraka virukkai yāḻi cūṭṭiya
 toṉṉilai marapiṉniṉ muṉṉōr pōla
 eṉṟu auvaiyār pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka (puṟam. 99) cīṉa nāṭṭai vāṉavar nāṭeṉpatu paṇṭai vaḻakku. karuppu niṟamāṉatu karumpu.
miḷakāy amerikkāviṉiṉṟu vantatākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. miḷaku + kāy = miḷakāy. miḷakupōṟ kāramuḷḷa kāy miḷakāy. ivvaḻakkaip piṉpaṟṟiyē, uruḷaikkiḻaṅku, pukaiyilai, nilakkaṭalai, vāṉkōḻi mutaliya peyarkaḷum piṟkālattil ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ilakkaṇa amaippu
irutiṇai : poruḷkaḷaiyellām, pakuttaṟivuḷḷatu, pakuttaṟivillatu eṉap pakuttatu tamiḻap potumakkaḷē. kāḷaiyum āvum aḵṟiṇaiyil āṇpālum peṇpālu māyiṉum, kāḷai vantāṉ, āvu vantāḷ eṉṟu yārum collum vaḻakkamillai. kāḷai vantatu, āvu vantatu eṉṟu iraṇṭaiyum oṉṟaṉpāliṟ kūṟuvatē marapu.
murukaṉ vantāṉ, vaḷḷi vantāḷ eṉṟu āṇpāl peṇpāl (pakuttaṟivuḷḷa) makkaḷukkē vakukkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. ivvulakiṟ pakuttaṟivuḷḷa makkaḷum iṟaivaṉumē uyarnta vakuppeṉṟum, piṟavellām uyiruḷḷavaiyāyiṉum illavaiyāyiṉum tāḻnta vakuppē yeṉṟum, ēṟkeṉavē potumakkaḷ vakutta iru vakuppiṟkum, ilakkaṇiyar muṟaiyē uyartiṇai, aḵṟiṇai yeṉṟu peyar maṭṭum iṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar. ilakkaṇam eṉpatu, ēṟkeṉavē ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷa moḻiyamaitikaḷuḷ nallavaṟṟai maṭṭum koṇṭu, avaṟṟiṟkup peyariṭṭuk kāṭṭum muṟaiyēyaṉṟi, putitāka ēṟpaṭṭa pulavar paṭaippaṉṟu. pakuttaṟivai aḷavaiyākak koṇṭu poruḷkaḷaip pakutta iḵtoṉṟē, paṇṭait tamiḻariṉ akak karaṇa vaḷarcciyaik kāṭṭap pōtiya cāṉṟākum.
ōriyaloḻuṅku
yāṉ, yām, yāṅkaḷ; nāṉ, nām, nāṅkaḷ; nīṉ, nīm, nīṅkaḷ; tāṉ, tām, tāṅkaḷ eṉṉum mūviṭap pakarap peyarkaḷum;
avaṉ	ivaṉ	uvaṉ	evaṉ
avaḷ	ivaḷ	uvaḷ	evaḷ
avar	ivar	uvar	evar
atu	itu	utu	etu
avai	ivai	uvai	evai
eṉṉum cuṭṭup peyar viṉāp peyarkaḷum; oṉṟumutal toḷḷāyirattut toṇṇūṟṟoṉpatu varaippaṭṭa eṇṇup peyarkaḷum, koṇṭuḷḷa ōriyalamaippai vēṟemmoḻiyilum kāṇamuṭiyātu.
poruṭpāl
āriya moḻikaḷiṟpōl īṟupaṟṟiya pālamaippiṉṟi, poruḷkaḷiṉ āṇmai peṇmaiyum orumai paṉmaiyum paṟṟiya pālamaippē tamiḻiluḷḷatu.
vaṭamoḻiyil, maṉaiviyaik kuṟikkum tāra, pāryā, kaḷattira eṉṉum muccoṟkaḷuḷ, mutalatu āṇpāl; iṭaiyatu peṇpāl; kaṭaiyatu alippāl. pottakattaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷuḷ, kiranta āṇpāl; cruti peṇpāl; putaka alippāl. itiṉiṉṟu ammoḻiyiṉ oḻuṅkai aṟintu koḷka.
pālicaivu
tamiḻc coṟṟoṭarkaḷil, eḻuvāyum payaṉilaiyum perumpālum tiṇaipāl eṇ iṭam ottēyirukkum. tiṇaipāl eṇ iṭa mayakkam iruppiṉum teḷivāyt teriyum.
vaṭamoḻiyil, tat kaccati eṉpatu avaṉ pōkiṟāṉ, avaḷ pōkiṟāḷ, atu pōkiṟatu, eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu pōṉṟa coṟṟoṭaramaippu tamiḻil illai.
pulamakkaḷ amaippu
urainaṭai, ceyyuḷ eṉa moḻinaṭai iruvakaippaṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ ceyyuḷ pulamakkaḷ amaippākum. atu pāṭṭum pāvum eṉa iru vakaiyām. urainaṭai paṇpaṭṭu icaippāṭṭākavum, icaip pāṭṭu paṇpaṭṭuc ceyyuḷāka vum, tiruntum, ākavē, ceyyuḷē moḻiyiṉ ucca nilaiyām.
kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻar, ceyyuṭ kalaiyiṉ koṭumuṭi yēṟi, aṟuvakai veṇpākkaḷaiyum, nālvakai akavaṟpākkaḷai yum, nālum aintum āṟumāṉa uṟuppukkaḷaiyuṭaiya nāl vakaik kalippākkaḷaiyum, iruvakai vañcippākkaḷaiyum, yāttirun taṉar. yāttal = kaṭṭutal.
veṇpāvum kalippāvum pōṉṟa ceyyuḷ vakaikaḷai, vēṟemmoḻiyilum kāṇṭalaritu.
paṇṭait tamiḻar ceyyuṭkalaiyiṟ ciṟantiruntataṉāl, ceyyuḷukkum nūṟpāviṟkum poruḷkūṟum urai uṭpaṭa, ellā ilakkiyattaiyum ceyyuḷi lēyē iyaṟṟiyiruntaṉar.
pāṭṭurai nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē
aṅkatam mutucolō ṭavvēḻ nilattum
...............................................................................................................
yāppiṉ vaḻiya teṉmaṉār pulavar.
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam (cey.78) kūṟutal kāṇka.

2. tuppuravu
paṇṭait tamiḻ makkaḷ viṭikālaiyil eḻuntavuṭaṉ, ūrukku veḷiyē ceṉṟu kālaikkaṭaṉ kaḻittu, āṟṟil allatu kālvāyil allatu kuḷattil kāl kaḻuvuvar. āṟṟiṟkup pōtal, kālvāykkup pōtal, kuḷattiṟkup pōtal, kollaikkup pōtal, veḷikkup pōtal eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakku itai yuṇarttum. ittakaiya vaḻakkum oṉṟukkup pōtal iraṇṭukkup pōtal eṉṉum iṭakka raṭakkalum tamiḻar nākarikattaik kāṭṭum.
kalvit toḻilāḷar, kāvalar, vaṇikar mutaliya uṭaluḻaip pillā vakuppār kālaiyilum; uḻavar, kaittoḻilāḷar, kūlikkārar mutaliya uḻaippāḷi vakuppār mālaiyilum; nāḷtōṟum kuḷittu vantaṉar. aḻukkait tēyppataṟku āṭavar pīrkkaṅ kūṭṭaip payaṉpaṭuttuvatuṇṭu. peṇṭir mañcaḷ, cuṇṇam mutaliyavaṟṟait tēyttuk kuḷippar.
cuṇṇam eṉpatu palavakai naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷaic cērttu iṭikkum poṭi. atai iṭittu vaippatu paṇṭaik kālattu iḷamakaḷirkkup peruvaḻakka māyiruntatu. cuṇṇamiṭikkum pōtu pāṭṭup pāṭuvar. icceyal māṇikkavācakar maṉattaik kavarntatiṉāl, tiruppoṟcuṇṇam eṉṉum tiruvācakap pāṭal tillaiyil aruḷic ceyyappeṟṟatu.
araṇmaṉaiyil vāḻum araca makaḷirum, māḷikaikaḷil vāḻum celvap peṇṭirum, mātavi pōlum nāṭakak kaṇikaiyarum, kōṭṭam, turukkam, takaram, akil, cantaṉam eṉṉum aivakai viraiyum (naṟumaṇap poruḷum); nāval allatu pūvanti, kaṭu, nelli, tāṉṟi, āl, aracu, atti, itti, muttakkācu allatu karuṅkāli, māntaḷir eṉṉum pattu vakait tuvarum; ilavaṅkam, paccilai, kaccōlam, ēlam, nākaṇam, kōṭṭam, nākam, matāvarici, takkōlam, naṉṉāri, veṇkōṭṭam, kācaṟai (kattūri), vēri, ilāmiccam, kaṇṭil veṇṇey, kaṭu, nelli, tāṉṟi, tuttam, vaṇṇak kaccōlam, arēṇukam, māñci, cayilēkam, puḻuku, puṉṉai naṟuntātu, puliyukir, caraḷam, tamālam, vakuḷam, patumukam, nuṇṇēlam, koṭuvēri eṉṉum muppattiruvakai ōmālikaiyum; ūṟavaitta naṉṉīriṟ kuḷittu vantaṉar.
āṭavar aṟivaṉ (putaṉ) kāriyum (caṉiyum), peṇṭir cevvāy veḷḷiyum, oḻuṅkāy eṇṇey tēyttut talai muḻukiṉar. kuḷippu nīrāṭṭu eṉṟum, talaimuḻukku neyyāṭṭu eṉṟum, collappeṟum. neyyāṭṭil eṇṇeyaiyum uṭam paḻukkaiyum pōkka, cīyaṟkāy araiyal, araippu araiyal, ucilait tūḷ, pāci (paccai)p payaṟṟumā, kaḷimaṇ mutaliya vaṟṟaip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
cīyaṟkāy allatu cīkkāy eṉpatu, aḻukkaip pōkkuṅ kāy eṉṟu poruḷpaṭuvatu; cikaikkāy eṉpataṉ marūu aṉṟu.
cīttal = 1. tuṭaittal. meṉpūñ cemmaloṭu naṉkalañ cīppa  (maturaik. 685).
2. pōkkutal. iruḷcīkkuñ cuṭarēpōl  (kalit. 100 : 24).
3. tuppuravākkutal (cūṭā).
ūrkkum aṇittē poykai poykaikkuc
cēyttum aṉṟē ciṟukāṉ yāṟē
kūḻaik kērmaṇ koṇarkañ cēṟum
eṉṉum kuṟuntokaic ceyyuḷ (113), paṇṭait tamiḻa makaḷir ūrukkaṇittāṉa kuṭinīrnilaiyiṟ kuḷikkāmal caṟṟu appā luḷḷa kāṭṭāṟṟiṟkuc ceṉṟu kuḷittataiyum, kūntalaḻukkup pōkkak kaḷimaṇ eṭuttuc ceṉṟataiyum, kūṟutal kāṇka.
iru pālārum kuḷittavuṭaṉum talaimuḻukiyavuṭaṉum, veḷutta tuvarttu muṇṭiṉāl talaiyaiyum uṭampaiyum tuvartti, veḷuttāṭai allatu puttāṭai yuṭuppar. māṟṟāṭai yillāviṭiṉ, muṉpu aṇintirunta āṭaiyait tuvaittuk koḷvar.
peṇṭir kārakiṟ pukaiyālum cantaṉak kaṭṭaip pukaiyālum tam kūntaliṉ īram pularttuvar.
kālaiyiṟ kuḷippiṉum kuḷiyāviṭiṉum, pal tulakkāmal oruvarum uṇpatillai.
cōṟuṇpavar, vāyum mukamum kaikālum kaḻuviya piṉ, tuppuravāṉa iṭattil taṭukkil allatu palakaiyil amarntu cappaḷittiruntu (cammaṇaṅkūṭṭi uṭkārntu), kaḻuviya vāḻai ilaiyil allatu veṇkala vaṭṭilil valakkaiyālēyē eṭuttu uṇpar. aracar poṟkalattilum celvar veḷḷik kalattilum uṇpatu vaḻakkam. peṟṟōrum peṟṟa ciṟu piḷḷai kaḷumāyiruntāloḻiya, orē kalattil allatu ilaiyiṟ palar uṇpatillai. uṇṭeḻuntapiṉ, uṇṭa iṭam ilaiyakaṟṟit taṇṇīr allatu cāṇa nīr teḷittut tuppuravākkappeṟum. ilaiyilviṭṭa miccilaip peṟ ṟōrum maṉaiviyarum irappōrum tavirap piṟar uṇṇār. cila uṇavu vakaiyāl ēṟpaṭum vāy nāṟṟattaip pōkkutaṟku, naṟumaṇac carakkai vāyiliṭutal, veṟṟilai tiṉṉutal, mārpiṟ cantaṉam pūcutal mutaliyavaṟṟaik kaiyāḷvar. ivaṟṟaic cerimāṉattiṉ poruṭṭeṉṟu colvatu muṇṭu.
viṭintavuṭaṉ peṇṭir muṟṟaṅkaḷiṟ cāṇanteḷittu vīṭu vācalaip perukkik kōlamiṭuvar. cevvāyum veḷḷiyum tappātu vīṭu muḻutum āviṉ cāṇattāl meḻukuvar. āṇṭiṟ koru muṟaiyum tirumaṇam nikaḻumpōtum, vīṭu muḻutum oṭṭaṭai pōkki veḷḷaiyaṭittuc cemmaṇ kōlamiṭuvatu vaḻakkam. āṇṭiṟkoru muṟaiyeṉṟatu poṅkaṟ paṇṭikai. poṅkaṟku muntiṉa nāḷ, iṉṟupōl aṉṟum vīṭṭiluḷḷa aḻukkuk kantalkaḷaiyum utavāp poruḷkaḷaiyum koḷutti viṭuvar. atōṭu taṅkaḷaip piṭitta pīḻai pōy viṭṭatu eṉṉuṅ karuttil, ataip pōki eṉṟaṉar. pōkutal pōtal, pōkiyatu pōki. ac collai vēntaṉ (intiraṉ) peyarākak koṇṭu, avaṉai nōkkic ceyyum viḻā eṉpatu poruntātu. tai piṟantāl vaḻi piṟakkum eṉṉum paḻamoḻi, pōki eṉṉuñ coṟporuḷai valiyuṟuttum.
vāṭakai vīṭukaḷiṟ putuk kuṭi pukumpōtum, oṭṭaṭai pōkki veḷḷaiyaṭikkappeṟum.
veḷic ceṉṟu vanta vīṭṭārum veḷiyārum, vīṭṭiṟkuḷ pukumuṉ pātattaik kaḻuviviṭa vēṇṭum eṉpatu oḻukka neṟi.
veppamillāta nāḷilum vēḷaiyilum muḷḷillāta iṭattilum kūṭa, vīṭṭai viṭṭu veḷic celliṉ, aṭiyil maṇṇum mācum paṭātapaṭi ceruppaṇintē celvar uyarn tōr. ceruppu vācaṟku veḷiyē allatu vācalaṇṭaitāṉ viṭappeṟum. ek karaṇiyam paṟṟiyum uḷḷē koṇṭu cellappaṭuvatillai.
piḷḷai peṟṟa vīṭṭiṟku ēḻu nāḷum, mātaviṭāy vantava ḷukkup paṉṉiru nāḷum, iḻavu vīṭṭiṟkup patiṉaintu nāḷum tīṭṭām.
tīṭṭuḷḷa peṇṭir kalantoṭā makaḷir (puṟam.299) eṉappaṭuvar.

3. ūṇ
ulakil mutaṉ mutal uṇavai nākarikamāyc camaittuṇṭavaṉ tamiḻaṉē. ēṉaiya nāṭṭārellām, tam nāṭṭil viḷainta uṇavup poruḷkaḷaip paccaiyākavum cuṭṭum veṟumaiyāy avittum uṇṭuvanta kālattil uṇavaic cōṟum kaṟiyum eṉa iraṇṭāka vakuttu, nellariciyaic cōṟākac camaittum, kaṟi allatu kuḻampu vakaikaḷai, cuvaiyūṭṭuvaṉavum uṭampai valuppaṭuttuvaṉavum nōyvarātu taṭuppaṉavumāṉa palavakai maruntuc carakkukaḷai ucilai (macālai) yākac cērttu ākkiyum, uyarvāka uṇṭu vanta perumai tamiḻaṉatākum.
kāy vakaikaḷaik kāmpu kaḷaintu cīva vēṇṭupavaṟṟaic cīvik kaḻuviyum; pittamuḷḷavaṟṟai avittiṟuttum; mañcaḷ, miḷaku allatu miḷakāy vaṟṟal, cīrakam, kottumalli, tēṅkāy mutaliya vaṟṟai maipōl araittuk kalantum; iñci, veṅkāyam, veḷḷaippūṇṭu, peruṅkāyam, karāmpū (karuvāppū), karuvāppaṭṭai, ēlam, cōmpu mutaliyavaṟṟai vēṇṭumaḷavu cērttum; ventapiṉ puḷi allatu elumiccañcāṟu viṭavēṇṭiyavaṟṟiṟku viṭṭum; kaṭuku, kaṟivēppilai mutaliyaṉa tūvi āviṉ neyyil tāḷittum, mutaṉ mutaṟ kuḻampu kāyccappeṟṟatu tamiḻakattilēyē. tāḷippu ilakkiya vaḻakkil kuy eṉappaṭum. kuyyuṭai yaṭicil (puṟam. 127).
ulakil ariciyuḷḷa tavacaṅkaḷiṟ palavakaiyuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ nellaricic cōṟupōl vēṟoṉṟum cuvaiyāyk kuḻam poṭu poruntuvatillai. tamiḻakattilēyē, kāṭaik kaṇṇi yarici, kutiraivāliyarici, cāmaiyarici, tiṉaiyarici, varakarici eṉa aivakai yariciyuḷa. avai nellaricikkīṭākā. kampum cōḷamum kañciyum kūḻumē camaittaṟkutavum. kēḻvarakāl kaḷiyum kūḻumtāṉ ākka muṭiyum. kōtumai, vāṟkōtumai pōṉṟa piṟa nāṭṭut tavacaṅkaḷ (tāṉiyaṅkaḷ), appamum (roṭṭiyum) ciṟṟuṇṭiyumtāṉ aṭavum cuṭavum ēṟkum.
nellariciyuḷḷum palavakai yuḷa. avaṟṟuḷ talai ciṟantatu campā. atuvum aṟupatu vakaippaṭṭatām. avaṟṟuṭ ciṟantaṉa cīrakac campāvum ciṟumaṇic campāvum.
paccarici, puḻuṅkalarici eṉṉum iru vakaiyuḷ, tamiḻar virum piyatu piṉṉatē. atu cūṭṭait taṇippataṉāl veppanāṭṭiṟ kēṟṟatākum; kuḻampiṉ cuvaiyaiyum mikuttuk kāṭṭum. ataṟkākavē, ataṟkuriya taṉi muyaṟciyai mēṟ koṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl ēṟpaṭum valuk kuṟaiviṟkuk kaṟi vakaikaḷālum neyyālum īṭu ceyyap peṟum.
iruṅkā ḻulakkai yirumpumukan tēytta
avaippumāṇ arici amalaiveṇ cōṟu  (ciṟupāṇ. 193-4).
eṉpatu, tūya veḷḷaiyāy arici tīṭṭappaṭṭatait terivikkum.
potuvāka, kālaic ciṟṟuṇṭiyum naṇpakal mālaip pēruṇṭiyum āka mūvuṇṭikaḷē paṇṭait tamiḻar uṇṭu vantaṉar.
kālaiyuṇavum naṇpakaluṇavum, uḻavarkkum kūliyāḷarkkum paḻañcōṟum paḻaṅkaṟiyum ūṟukāyumākum. putuk kaṟiyāyiṉ neruppil vāṭṭiyatum tuvaiyalumāyirukkum. irāvuṇavē avarukkup putuc camaiyalākum.
meṉpulattu vayaluḻavar
vaṉpulattup pakaṭuviṭṭuk
kuṟumuyaliṉ kuḻaiccūṭṭoṭu
neṭuvāḷaip palluviyaṟ
paḻañcōṟṟup pukavarunti
 eṉpatu puṟam (395).
uḻavar maṅkala viṉaināḷum viḻā nāḷum viruntu nāḷum pōṉṟa ciṟappu nāḷilaṉṟi, mūvēḷaiyum nellaricic cōṟuṇpatillai. varaku, cāmai, kampu, cōḷam, kēḻvaraku mutaliya avvakkālattu viḷainta tavacattaiyē avvak kālattup pakaluṇavāy uṇpar.
illattilum niḻalilumiruntu vēlai ceyvōrellām, perumpālum, kālaiyiṟ paḻaiyatum naṇpakal mālaiyiṟ cuṭucōṟum, uṇpar. aracarum celvarum ellā nāḷum, piṟarellām ciṟappunāḷum, kālaiyiṟ palakāramum poṅkalum pōṉṟa cuṭu ciṟṟuṇṭi vakaikaḷai uṇpar.
iruvēḷaip pēruṇṭikaḷuḷ, tamiḻarkkuc ciṟantatu naṇ pakalatē. kuḻampuc cōṟum miḷaku nīrc cōṟum mōr allatu tayirc cōṟum eṉa, mukkaṭavaiyuḷḷatu pēruṇṭi. atai aṟucuvaiyuṇṭiyeṉpatu vaḻakku. mukkaṉikaḷuḷ ētēṉu moṉṟu iṉippum, pākaṟkāy allatu cuṇṭaivaṟṟal kacappum, kāravaṭai uṟaippum, puḷikkaṟiyum tayirum ūṟukāyum puḷippum, mātuḷaṅkāy allatu kaccal tuvarppum, uppēṟi uvarppum, ākum. ittakaiya uṇṭi, tamiḻariṉ cuvai mutircciyaiyum talaiciṟanta nākarikattaiyum maruttuva aṟivaiyum uṭalnala vuṇarcciyaiyum oruṅkē kāṭṭum. aṟucuvaiyuḷḷum tamiḻarkkuc ciṟantavai iṉippum puḷippum ākum. itu veppa nāṭṭiyalpu.
muḷitayir picainta kāntaḷ melviral
kaḻuvuṟu kaliṅkam kaḻāa tuṭīik
kuvaḷai yuṇkaṇ kuyppukai kamaḻat
tāṉtuḻan taṭṭa tīmpuḷip pākar
iṉiteṉak kaṇavaṉ uṇṭaliṉ
nuṇṇitiṉ makiḻntaṉ ṟoṇṇutaṉ mukaṉē.
eṉṉum kuṟuntokaic ceyyuḷum (164).
vēḷai veṇpū veṇṭayirk koḷīi
āymakaḷ aṭṭa ampuḷi mitavai  (215)
puṟavukkaru vaṉṉa puṉpula varakiṉ
pāṟpey puṉkam tēṉoṭu mayakkik
kuṟumuyaṟ koḻuñcūṭu kiḻitta vokkaloṭu
.....................................................................................................................
amalaik koḻuñcō ṟārnta pāṇarkku  (34)
pāliṟ peytavum pākiṟ koṇṭavum
aḷavupu kalantu mellitu paruki   (381)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭaṭikaḷum iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkaṉa. puṉkam = cōṟu. mitavai = kūḻ.
tamiḻar viruntuḷ talaiciṟantatu tirumaṇa viruntu. atiṟ patiṉeṇvakaik kaṟiyum kaṉṉalum (pāyacam) paṭaikkap peṟum. patiṉeṇ vakaik kaṟikaḷ : aviyal (uviyal), kaṭaiyal, kummāyam, kūṭṭu (ventāṇam), tuvaṭṭal, puraṭṭal, poriyal, vaṟuval, puḷikkaṟi, paccaṭi (āṇam), appaḷam, tuvaiyal, ūṟukāy, vaṟṟal, uḻuntuvaṭai, kāravaṭai, tēṅkuḻal, mukkaṉikaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉpaṉa.
kummāyam kuḻaiya venta paruppu, allatu payaṟu. ventāṇam (venta āṇam) eṉpatu iṉṟu veñcaṇam eṉa maruvi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
tiṇmai nilaipaṟṟi nīr, cāṟu, tīyal, iḷaṅkuḻampu, kuḻampu, kūṭṭu eṉak kuḻampu palavakaippaṭum. cōṟum, carukkaraip poṅkal (akkāraṭalai, akkāravaṭicil), veṇpoṅkal, miḷakuppoṅkal, neyppoṅkal, tēṅkāyccōṟu, pāṟpoṅkal, kaṭumpuccōṟu, ūṉcōṟu (pulavu), ūṉṟu vaiyaṭicil eṉap palavakaippaṭum.
ūṉcōṟu allatu pulavu paṇṭaik kālattil tamiḻakatti lēyē tōṉṟip piṉpu vaḻakkaṟṟatu; mukammatiyar vantapiṉ avariṭamiruntu putitāyk kaṟṟuk koṇṭataṉṟu.
maṭṭuvāy tiṟappavum maiviṭai vīḻppavum
aṭṭāṉ ṟāṉāk koḻuntuvai yūṉcōṟum
peṭṭāṅ kīyum peruvaḷam paḻuṉi
eṉṟum (113),
pularāp paccilai piṭaiyiṭupu toṭutta
malarā mālaip pantukaṇ ṭaṉṉa

cemmaṟ ṟammaniṉ vemmuṉai yirukkai
eṉṟum (33), varum puṟappāṭṭaṭikaḷaik kāṇka.
amiḻtaṉa marapiṉ ūṉṟuvai yaṭicil
eṉpatu (puṟam. 390 : 17), pulaviṉ ciṟappu vakaiyākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
iṟaicci vakaikaḷuḷ uṭumpiṟaicciyait talaiciṟantatākat tamiḻar koṇṭamai, muḻuvuṭumpu, mukkāṟkāṭai, araik kōḻi, kālāṭu eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyāl teriya varukiṉṟatu.
uṭumpiḻu taṟutta oṭuṅkāḻp paṭalaic

cerumiku cēeyniṟ pāṭunar kaiyē.
eṉṟu kūṟiya viṭaiyāl (puṟam. 14 : 12-19), kaṭaik kaḻakak kālattup pirāmaṇaṉum ūṉuṇṭamai aṟiyappaṭum.
neykaṉi kuṟumpūḻ kāya māka
ārpatam peṟuka tōḻi yattai
peruṅkal nāṭaṉ varainteṉa avaṉetir
naṉṟō makaṉē yeṉṟaṉaṉ
naṉṟē pōlum eṉṟurait tōṉē.  (kuṟun. 389).
eṉpatil, neyyiṟ poritta kuṟumpūḻk kaṟi kūṟappaṭṭatu.
kuṟumpūḻ vēṭṭuvaṉ 214ām puṟappāṭṭiṟ kuṟikkap paṭṭāṉ. kuṟumpūḻ = kāṭai. maṉaikkōḻi 395ām puṟappāṭṭiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. āṭṭil veḷḷāṭṭuk kaṭāvaiyē paṇṭaittamiḻar cuvaiyuḷḷateṉa virumpiyuṇṭaṉar.
māṭan tōṟum maiviṭai vīḻppa  (puṟam. 33).
maṭṭuvāy tiṟappavum maiviṭai vīḻppavum  (puṟam. 113).
naṟavun toṭumiṉ viṭaiyum vīḻmiṉ  (puṟam. 262).
maiviṭai yirumpōttuc centīc cērtti  (puṟam. 364).
viṭaivīḻttuc cūṭukiḻippa  (puṟam. 366).
marantōṟum maivīḻppa  (maturaik kāñci, 754).
mallaṉ maṉṟattu mataviṭai keṇṭi  (perumpāṇ. 143).
ceṅkaṇ maḻaviṭai keṇṭi  (perumporuḷ viḷakkam).
mai = kārāṭu, veḷḷāṭu. viṭai = kaṭā, veḷḷāṭṭuk kaṭā.
ikkālattil marakkaṟi vakaiyaic cērnta mōrk kuḻampu, akkālattil ūṉkaṟi vakaiyākavum iruntatu.
cempuṟ ṟīyaliṉ iṉṉaḷaip puḷittu
....................................................................................................................
iravalark kīyum vaḷḷiyōṉ nāṭē.
eṉṟu kapilar pāriyiṉ paṟampunāṭṭaip paṟṟip pāṭiyiruttal (puṟam. 119) kāṇka. aḷai = mōr.
viṭai eṉpatu muḻu vaḷarcciyaṭainta iḷa vilaṅkiṉ allatu paṟavaiyiṉ peyar. viṭaittal = paruttal, viṟaittal muṟukkāyiruttal. vilaṅkiṉattil āṇaiyum paṟavaiyiṉattil irupālaiyum viṭaiyeṉpatu marapu.
neyyiṟ poritta kaṟiyait tamiḻarellārum virumpi yuṇṭaṉar. veḷḷarip piñcaik kuṟukkēyaṟuttāl tōṉṟum vitai pōṉṟa veḷiṟiya naṟuneyyaiyē avar payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
aṇilvarik koṭuṅkāy vāḷpōḻn tiṭṭa
kāḻpōl nalviḷar naṟuney  (puṟam. 246)
eṉṟu pūtapāṇṭiyaṉ tēvi kūṟutal kāṇka.
poritta kaṟiyōṭu uṇpataṟkup pulavaricic cōṟupōl nīṇṭa parukkaic cōṟṟaiyē virumpiṉar. nīṇṭa parukkai kokkiṉ viral pōliruttalāl, kokkukir nimiral eṉappaṭṭatu.
maṇṭaiya kaṇṭa māṉvaṟaik karuṉai
kokkukir nimiral okkal āra  (puṟam. 398 : 24 - 25).
tamiḻakattil, mutalil, tuṟaviyarē pulāluṇavai nīkki vantaṉar. tiruvaḷḷuvar, aruḷuṭaimai pulāṉ maṟuttal kol lāmai eṉṉum mūvatikāraṅkaḷaiyum, tuṟavaṟaviyalil vait tiruppatum kaṇṇappanāyaṉār paṭaiyalum iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkaṉa. camaṇam tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟku vanta piṉṉarē, illaṟat tārum, ciṟappākac civaneṟiyar, pulāluṇavai nīkkat talaip paṭṭaṉar. ataṉāl, marakkaṟiyuṇavu caivam eṉappaṭṭatu.
marakkaṟi yuṇaviṉarāyiṉum ūṉuṇaviṉarāyiṉum, pāl, tayir, mōr, veṇṇey, ney eṉṉum āṉaintai mikap payaṉpaṭutti vantaṉar.
naṟuneyk kaṭalai vicaippa  (puṟam. 120 : 14).
kaṟi porikkak kāycciya neyyulaiyōcaikku matayāṉaiyiṉ perumūccaiyum, kotikkiṉṟa neyyil tuḷḷi vēkum kaṟiyōcaikku āḻnta niṟai kuḷattil perumaḻait tuḷikaḷ viḻum ōcaiyaiyum paṇṭaippulavar uvamai kūṟiyiruppatu mika makiḻntu pārāṭṭattakkatu.
maiyal yāṉai ayāvuyirt taṉṉa
neyyulai  (puṟam. 261 : 8-9).
neṭunīra niṟaikayattup
paṭumārita tuḷipōla
neytuḷḷiya vaṟai  (puṟam. 386 : 13).
yāṉaikkuk koṭukkum kavaḷamum neyyil mitittut tiraṭṭappaṭṭatiṉāl, neymmiti eṉap peyar peṟṟatu. camaiyal vakaikkellām āviṉ neyyaiyē akkālattil payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. nalleṇṇey eṉṉum eḷ ney talai muḻukkiṟku maṭṭum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.
aracarum maṟavarum pāṇarum kūttarum porunarum pulavar cilarum uḻavarum uḻaippāḷiyarum, kuṭippu vakaikaḷuḷ kaḷḷaiyē ciṟantatākak koṇṭaṉar. kaḷ eṉṉuñ col, veṟinīr, patanīr (teḷivu allatu paṉañcāṟu), maṭṭu (carpattu), tēṉ eṉṉum nālvakaiyaiyuṅ kuṟikkum. inta nāṉkaiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar paṇṭait tamiḻar. ivaṟṟuḷ veṟinīr vakaiyē vaḷḷuvarāl kaḷḷuṇṇāmai eṉṉum atikārattiṟ kaṇṭikkappaṭṭatu.
veṟinīr iyaṟkaik kaḷḷum ceyaṟkaik kaḷḷum eṉa iruvakaittu. paṉaṅkaḷḷum teṉṉaṅkaḷḷum īccaṅkaḷḷum iyaṟkai; aricic cōṟṟu nīraip puḷikka vaittu arikkap paṭṭa ariyalum, mēṉāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu puṭṭikaḷil vanta matuk kaḷḷum ceyaṟkai.
yavaṉar, naṉkalan tanta taṇkamaḻ tēṟal  (puṟam. 56 : 18).
veppa nāṭākiya tamiḻakattil, kālaiyiliruntu katiravaṉ aṭaiyum varai kāṭṭil kaṭuveyilil varuntiyuḻaikkum uḻavarkku, uḻaippu nōvaip pōkkavum nīr vēṭkaiyait taṇikkavum, veṟippum kuḷircciyum puḷippāṉa kuṭippu vēṇṭiyatāyiruntatu. ataṉāl, avarkkeṉṟu taṉik kaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭatu.
kaḷamark karitta viḷaiyal veṅkaḷ eṉpatu (puṟam. 212 : 2) itaiyuṇarttum.
kaḷḷiṉ puḷippaiyum veṟippaiyum mikukka, ataik kaṇṇāṭik kalaṅkaḷilum mūṅkiṟ kuḻāykaḷilum iṭṭu mūṭip paṉṉāḷ maṇṇiṟ putaittu vaippatum vaḻakkam. attakaiya kaḷḷiṉ kaṭumaikkut tēṭkoṭṭum pāmpuk kaṭiyum uvamai kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
nilamputaip paḻuṉiya maṭṭiṉ tēṟal  (puṟam. 120).
tēṭkaṭup paṉṉa nāṭpaṭu tēṟal  (puṟam. 392 : 16).
pāppukkaṭup paṉṉa tōppi  (akam. 348 : 7).
tēṟal = teḷinta kaḷ. tōppi = neṟ kaḷ.
iṉkaṭuṅkaḷ (puṟam. 80) eṉpataṉālum, pūkkamaḻ tēṟal (porunar. 157) eṉpataṉālum, ceyaṟkaikkaḷ iṉimaiyum maṇamum ūṭṭap peṟṟiruntamai aṟiyalām.
nāṭpaṭāta iyaṟkaik kaḷḷai uṭaluḻaippāḷikaḷ veṟi yuṇṭākāvāṟu aḷavāka uṇpatāṟ kēṭillai yeṉpatum naṉṟeṉpatum, ārāycciyāḷar karuttu. vēṉiṟ kālattiṟkuk kuḷircciyāṉa paṉaṅkaḷḷaiyum mārik kālattiṟku veppamāṉa teṉṉaṅkaḷḷaiyum, iyaṟkai vakuttiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
4. uṭai
tamiḻakattiṟ paḻaṅkālattilēyē necavu tōṉṟiviṭṭateṉa ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. vayavar yōvāṉ mārcal (Sir John Marshall) tam mokoñcō-tārōvum cintu nākarikamum (Mohenjodaro and the Indus Civilization) eṉṉum nūlil ki.mu. 3000 āṇṭukaṭkumuṉ mēṉāṭukaṭku intiyāviṉiṉṟē tuṇi ēṟṟumatiyāṉa teṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar. akkālattintiyā tamiḻakamē.
parutti toṉṟu toṭṭut teṉṉāṭṭil viḷaintu varukiṉṟatu. mūtāṭṭiyarum kaimpeṇṭirum paṇṭaik kālattil mēṟkoṇṭa toḻilkaḷuḷ peruvaḻakkāṉatu nūlnūṟṟal. ataṉāl avar paruttip peṇṭir eṉappaṭṭār.
paruttip peṇṭir paṉuva laṉṉa  (puṟam. 125 : 1).
āḷil peṇṭir tāḷiṟ ceyta
nuṇaṅkunuṇ paṉuval  (naṟ. 353).
pañcitaṉ collāp paṉuval iḻaiyākac
ceñcoṟ pulavaṉē cēyiḻaiyā - eñcāta
kaiyēvā yākak katirē matiyāka
maiyilā nūṉmuṭiyu māṟu  (naṉ. pāyiram).
paṉuval = nūl.
nūṟpataṟkup pañcaic curuḷākat tiraṭṭi vaippataṉāl, nūlnūṟṟalaik koṭṭai nūṟṟal eṉpatu vaḻakku. nūṟkuṅ karuvi, cuṟṟum cakkara muṭaimaiyāl iṟāṭṭu allatu iṟāṭṭai eṉappaṭṭatu.
iṟattal = vaḷaital, iṟaku = vaḷainta tūvu.
iṟavu = kūraiyiṉ cāyppu, iṟa - iṟappu = kūraiyiṉ cāyppu. itu iṟavāṇam, iṟavāram eṉṟum collappaṭum.
iṟavu - iṟavuḷ = malaiccarivu.
iṟai = 1. vīṭṭiṟappu, kuṟiyiṟaik kurampai  (puṟam. 129). 2. peṇṭiriṉ vaḷainta muṉkai. elvaḷai yiṟaiyū rummē  (kalit. 7 : 16).
iṟaital = vaḷaital, vaṇaṅkutal. iṇaiyaṭi yiṟaimiṉ  (patiṉō, āḷu. tirukkalam. 48).
iṟai - iṟaiñcu. iṟaiñcutal = vaṇaṅkutal.
iṟa - iṟā = vaḷainta peruṅ kūṉi.
iṟā - iṟāl = 1. vaṭṭamāṉa tēṉkūṭu. 2. peruṅkūṉi.
iṟā - iṟavu = 1. peruṅkūṉi. kaṭaliṟaviṉ cūṭutiṉṟum (paṭṭiṉap. 63). 2. tēṉkūṭu (ñāṉavā.)
iṟā - iṟāṭṭu = 1. peruṅkūṉi. 2. tēṉkūṭu. 3. nūṟkum cakkaram.
iṟāṭṭu - iṟāṭṭai = nūṟkum cakkaram.
iṟāṭṭu - iṟāṭṭiṉam = 1. nūṟkum cakkaram. 2. nīriṟaikkum uruḷai. 3. ēṟiviḷaiyāṭum kuṭaiyiṟāṭṭiṉam allatu iṟāṭṭiṉa vūñcal.
nūṟkum cakkaram kaiyiṉāṟ cuṟṟappaṭuvataṉāl, atu kaiyi ṟāṭṭu allatu kaiyiṟāṭṭai, allatu kaiyiṟāṭṭiṉam eṉṟu collappaṭum. iṟāṭṭu eṉṉum collē, ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutaliyil illai. irāṭṭu eṉṉum vaḻūuc col maṭṭum, tēṉkūṭu eṉṉum poruḷiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
iṟāṭṭu, iṟāṭṭai, iṟāṭṭiṉam eṉṉum mūvaṭivum, intiyil rahaṭ allatu rahaṇṭṭā eṉṉum vaṭivil nūṟkuṅ karuviyaik kuṟittu vaḻaṅku kiṉṟaṉa. āyiṉ, intic collē tamiḻc coṟku mūlamākac ceṉṉai akaramutaliyiṟ kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
nūl eṉṉuñ collē nuṇṇiyatu eṉṉum vērp poru ḷuṭaiyatu. nuṇaṅku nuṇ paṉuval eṉpatu mika nuṇṇiya nūlaik kuṟikkum.
akkālattil, paṭṭu, paruttip pañcu, vilaṅku mayir ākiya mūvakaik karuvi yālum āṭai neytu vantaṉar. itu,
nūliṉu mayiriṉum nuḻainūṟ paṭṭiṉum
pālvakai teriyāp paṉṉū ṟaṭukkattu
naṟumaṭi ceṟinta aṟuvai vītiyum
eṉṉum cilappatikāra aṭikaḷāl (14 : 205 -7) aṟiyalām. mayiriṉum eṉpataṟku eli mayiriṉālum eṉṟu urai varain tuḷḷār aṭiyārkku nallār.
mayir niṟainta oruvakai malaiyeli paṇṭait tamiḻ nāṭṭi lirunta teṉpatum, ataṉ mayirāl ciṟanta tāvaḷi (kampaḷam) neyyappaṭṭa teṉpatum,
pukaḻvaraic ceṉṉimēṟ pūcaiyiṟ periyaṉa
 pavaḻamē yaṉaiyaṉa paṉmayirp pēreli.
cenne ruppuṇuñ cevve limmayi
 ranne ruppaḷa vāypoṟ kampalam.
eṉṟu cīvakacintāmaṇi (1898, 2686) kūṟuvatāl aṟiyappaṭum. āyiṉum, elimayirāl maṭṭumaṉṟi āṭṭumayirālum āṭai neyyap paṭṭamai.
elippūm pōrvaiyoṭu mayirppaṭam virittu
eṉṉum peruṅkatai yaṭiyāl (uñcaik 47 : 179) aṟiyalām.
paṇṭait tamiḻnāṭṭil neyyappaṭṭa āṭai vakaikaḷuḷ, kōcikam, pītakam, paccilai, arattam, nuṇṭukil, cuṇṇam, vaṭakam, pañcu, iraṭṭu, pāṭakam, kōṅkalar, kōpam, cittirak kammi, kuruti, kariyal, pēṭakam, pariyaṭṭakkācu, vētaṅkam, puṅkark kāḻakam, cillikai, tūriyam, paṅkam, tattiyam, vaṇṇaṭai, kavaṟṟumaṭi, nūlyāppu, tirukku, tēvāṅku, poṉṉeḻuttu, kuccari, tēvakiri, kāttūlam, iṟañci, veṇpotti, cempotti, paṇippotti eṉa 36 vakaikaḷ aṭiyārkku nallārāṟ kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷa. ivaiyallāmal, kampalam, kaliṅkam, kāḻakam, cīrai, tukil, tūcu, paṭam mutaliya palavuḷa.
aṟukkappaṭuvataṉāl aṟuvai eṉṟum, tuṇikkappaṭuvataṉāl tuṇi eṉṟum, cavaṇṭiruppataṉāl cavaḷi yeṉṟum, āṭai pala potup peyar peṟum. cavaḷutal tuvaḷutal. meṉ kāṟṟilum āṭuvatu (acaivatu) āṭai.
cavaḷi eṉṉum tamiḻccol, tjavaḷi eṉṟu teluṅkilum javaḷi eṉṟu kaṉṉaṭattilum eṭuppoliyuṭaṉ olikkappaṭuva tālum, tamiḻilum aṅṅaṉam iṟṟait tamiḻar olippatālum, vaṭacolleṉṟu tavaṟākak karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. vaṭamoḻiyil iccol illai.
caricamaḻppuc caṭṭi caruku cavaṭi
caḷicakaṭu caṭṭai cavaḷi - cavicaraṭu
cantu cataṅkai caḻakkāti īriṭattum
vantaṉavāṟ cammutalum vai.
eṉṉum mayilainātar eṭuttukkāṭṭuc ceyyuḷāl, cavaḷi eṉpatu tūya teṉcollātal aṟiyappaṭum.
akkālattu mellāṭai, pāmpuc caṭṭai pōṉṟum, mūṅki liṉ uṭpuṟa mīntōl pōṉṟum, pukai virintāṟ pōṉṟum, nīrāvi paṭarntāṟ pōṉṟum, iḻai yōṭiyatu teriyāmalum, pūttoḻiluṭaṉ nuṇṇitāy neyyap paṭṭiruntateṉṟu paṇṭait tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
nōkku nuḻaikallā nuṇmaiya pūkkaṉintu
 aravuri yaṉṉa aṟuvai  (porunar. 82-83)
pāmpuri yaṉṉa vaṭiviṉa kāmpiṉ
 kaḻaipaṭu coliyiṉ iḻaiyaṇi vārā
 oṇpūṅ kaliṅkam  (puṟam 383 : 9 : 11)
kāmpucolit taṉṉa aṟuvai  (ciṟupāṇ. 236)
pukaivirin taṉṉa poṅkutukil
 āvi yaṉṉa avirnūṟkaliṅkam  (perumpāṇ. 469)
kaṇṇuḻai kallā nuṇṇūṟ kaiviṉai
 vaṇṇa aṟuvaiyar  (maṇi. 28 : 52 - 3)
iḻaimaruṅ kaṟiyā nuḻainūṟ kaliṅkam  (malaipaṭu. 561)
 nīlak kaccaip pūvā rāṭai  (puṟam. 274)
pōtuviri pakaṉṟaip putumala raṉṉa
 akaṉṟumaṭi kaliṅkam  (puṟam. 393)
tirumala raṉṉa putumaṭik koḷīi  (puṟam. 390.)
ikkālak kāñcipurap paṭṭuc cēlai pōṉṟē, akkālap paṭṭāṭaiyum muṉṟāṉai yōrattil aḻakiya maṇi pōṉṟanūṉ muṭiccukkaḷai yuṭaiyatā yiruntatu.
koṭṭaik karaiya paṭṭuṭai nalki  (porunar. 155).
akkālattil, uyarkuṭi āṭavar vēṭṭiyum mēlāṭaiyum, uyarkuṭip peṇṭir cēlaiyum kaccum (iravikkaiyum), aṇintiruntaṉar. tāḻkuṭi yāṭavar vēṭṭiyoṭu talai muṇṭum, tāḻkuṭip peṇṭir kacciṉṟiccēlai muṉṟāṉai yālamainta māryāppum, koṇṭiruntaṉar. ūrāṭcit talaivarum periyōrum mēlāṭaiyoṭu vaṭṭat talaip pākaiyum aṇintiruntaṉar.
aracarkkum araciyal atikārikaṭkum araṇmaṉai aluvalarkkumē, caṭṭaiyaṇiyum urimaiyiruntatu. caṭṭai uṭampiṟkup pai pōṉṟiruppatāl, meyppai eṉṟum peyar peṟum.
nuṇviṉaik kollar nūṟṟuvar piṉvara
meyppai pukku viyaṅkunaṭaic celaviṟ
kaikkōṟ kollaṉai  (cilap. 16 : 106 - 8).
īcaṉuṅ kaṟṟuccolvōr piṉvara vikaliṟ kūṭit
tēcuṭaic caṭṭai cātti
eṉṉum perum paṟṟap puliyūr nampi kūṟṟu piṟkālattatēṉum, caṭṭai eṉṉum col muṟkālattatē. kañcuka mākkaḷ eṉṉum cilappatikārac coṟku caṭṭaiyiṭṭa piratāṉikaḷ eṉṟu aruñcolluraikārar varaintiruttal kāṇka. pēṭik kōlat tārum aracakulap peṇṭirum, vaṭṭuṭai eṉṉum oru vakaic ciṟappuṭaiyai, araiyiṉiṉṟu muḻantāḷaḷavāka akkālattaṇin tiruntaṉar. (maṇi. 3 : 122; peruṅ 3. 4 : 122).
vēṭar, araiyiṉiṉṟu aṭit toṭaiyaḷavāka, kāḻam eṉṉum oru vakai yuṭaiyai aṇintiruntamai kāḻamiṭṭa kuṟaṅ kiṉaṉ eṉṉum kampar kūṟṟāl (kamparā. kaṅkaip. 32) teriya varukiṉṟatu. atu callaṭam pōṟ kuṟukiya araikkāṟ caṭṭai pōlum!
veyiṟ kālattilum vaṟaṇṭa iṭattilum cellum veḷip pōkkarum vaḻip pōkkarum, illaṟattārāyiṉ toṭutōl eṉṉum ceruppum, tuṟaviyarāyiṉ mitiyaṭi eṉṉum pātak kuṟaṭum, aṇintu ceṉṟaṉar.
caṭṭaiyiṭṭum mēṉāṭṭāṟpōṟ pātakkūṭu (Boots) aṇintum kāṭṭuvaḻiyiṟ ceṉṟatākap perumpāṇāṟṟup paṭaiyiṟ kūṟappaṭum vaṇikar, veḷināṭṭār pōl terikiṉṟatu.
aṭiputai yaraṇam eytip paṭampukku
................................................................................................
uṭampiṭit taṭakkai ōṭā vampalar  perumpāṇ. (69 - 76).
aṭiputaiyaraṇam = aṭiyai maṟaikkiṉṟa pātakkāppu. paṭam = caṭṭai.
5. aṇi
paṇṭait tamiḻakattil, ciṟanta vakaiyil virumpiya aḷavu aṇikalam ceyvataṟkēṟṟa poṉ, veḷḷi, muttu, maṇi mutaliya karuvikaḷum tērnta poṟkollarum, aṇipavarum, iruntaṉar.
ikkālattum, ulakil mikutiyāyp poṉ kiṭaikkumiṭam āttirēliyāvum teṉṉāppirikkāvumākum. ivai muḻukip pōṉa paḻantamiḻakamāṉa kumarik kaṇṭattaic cērntavai. ātalāl, kumari nāṭṭilum tamiḻarkku ērāḷamāyp poṉ kiṭaittirukka vēṇṭum.
kumarik kaṇṭam muḻukiya piṉpum, koṅku nāṭṭil mikutiyāyp poṉ kiṭaittatu. atu koṅkup poṉ eṉak kalveṭṭiṟ ciṟappāyk kuṟikkap peṟṟuḷḷatu. iṉṟu taṅkac curaṅkamuḷḷa kuvaḷālapuram (kōlār) koṅku nāṭṭaic cērntatē. annāṭṭil tōṉṟum kāviriyāṟu, paṇṭaināḷiṟ poṉ maṇalaik koḻittataṉāl poṉṉi yeṉappeṟṟatu. koṅku nāṭu paṇṭait tamiḻakattiṉ oru pakutiyākum.
maturaiyilirunta veḷḷiyampalamum tillaiyilirunta poṉṉampalamum, paṇṭait tamiḻakattiṉ veḷḷi poṉ vaḷattaik kāṭṭum.
mutaṟkālattil ērāḷamāyp poṉ kiṭaittataṉāl poṉṉālēyē kācaṭikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉāl, kāciṟku maṭṭumaṉṟi, tātu (ulōka) vakaikaṭkum poṉ eṉpatu potup peyarāyiṟṟu veṇpoṉ = veḷḷi, cempoṉ = cempu, karumpoṉ = irumpu. poṉ = tātu (Metal). itaṉāl, tamiḻakattil irumpuk kālattiṟku muṉ poṟkālam oṉṟu iruntatō eṉa aiyam eḻukiṉṟatu.
kaṭaikkaḻakak kālap pulavar cilarkku aḷikkappaṭṭa poṟparicilē, akkālattup poṉvaḷattaik kāṭṭap pōtumāṉatu.
kāppiyāṟṟuk kāppiyaṉār, kaḷaṅkāykkaṇṇi nārmuṭic cēral mītu patiṟṟuppattiṉ 4ām pattaip pāṭi, nāṟpatu nūṟāyiram poṉṉum avaṉ āṇṭatiṟ pākamum peṟṟār. poṉ eṉpatu poṟkācu.
kākkaipāṭiṉiyār nacceḷḷaiyār, āṭu kōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉ mītu patiṟ. 6ām pattaip pāṭi, aṇikalaṉuk keṉṟu oṉpatu tulām poṉṉum nūṟāyiram poṟkācum peṟṟār.
kapilar, celvakkaṭuṅkō vāḻiyātaṉ mītu patiṟ. 7ām pattaip pāṭi, nūṟāyiram poṟkācum avaṉ malai mītēṟik kaṇṭu koṭutta nāṭum peṟṟār.
aricil kiḻār, takaṭūreṟinta peruñcēralirumpoṟai mītu patiṟ. 8ām pattaip pāṭi, oṉpatu nūṟāyiram poṟkācum āṭciyurimaiyai maṟuttu amaiccurimaiyum peṟṟār.
peruṅkuṉṟūr kiḻār, kuṭakkō iḷañcēra lirumpoṟai mītu patiṟ. 9ām pattaip pāṭi, muppattīrāyiram poṟkācum pavavakaip paricilum peṟṟār.
kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅkaṇṇaṉār, karikāṟ peruvaḷavaṉ mītu paṭṭiṉappālaiyaip pāṭi, patiṉāṟu nūṟāyiram poṉ peṟṟār.
patiṟṟup pattil mutaṟpattum 10ām pattum iṉṟiṉmaiyāl, avaṟṟaip pāṭiya pulavar peṟṟa paricum teriyavillai.
mātavipōl āṭalpāṭal araṅkēṟiya nāṭakak kaṇikaiyiṉ mālai vilai āyirat teṇ kaḻañcu poṉṉāka matikkap paṭṭiruntatu.
kaṇṇuḷ viṉaiñar kaiviṉai muṟṟiya
nuṇviḷaik kollar  (cilap. 16 : 105 -6)
mattaka maṇiyoṭu vayiram kaṭṭiya
pattik kēvaṇap pacumpoṟ kuṭaiccūl
cittirac cilampiṉ ceyviṉai yellām
poyttoḻiṟ kollaṉ purintuṭaṉ nōkkik
kōpperun tēvik kallatai yiccilampu
yāppuṟa villai yeṉa  (cilap. 16 : 117 - 122).
palavuṟu kaṇṇuḷ cilakōl avirtoṭi  (kalit. 85 : 7)
eṉṉum pakutikaḷ akkālattu aṇikala viṉaittiṟattaik kāṭṭum.
malaipayanta maṇiyum kaṭaṟupayanta poṉṉum
kaṭalpayanta katirmuttamum  (puṟam. 377 : 16 - 7).
eṉpatu tamiḻakattiṉ poṉ maṇi muttu vaḷattaik kāṭṭum.
pāṇṭi nāṭṭaic cērnta kīḻaikkaṭal muttu, toṉṟu toṭṭu ulakap pukaḻ peṟṟatu.
viḷaintu mutirnta veṇmuttiṉ
ilaṅkuvaḷai iruñcērik
kaṭkoṇṭik kuṭippākkattu
naṟkoṟkaiyōr nacaipporuna  (maturaik. 135 - 138)
maṟappōrp pāṇṭiyar aṟattiṟ kākkum
koṟkaiyam peruntuṟai muttiṉ aṉṉa  (akam. 27 : 8-9).
ivartirai tanta īrṅkatir muttam
kavarnaṭaip puravik kālvaṭut tapukkum
naṟṟēr vaḻuti koṟkai muṉṟuṟai  (akam. 130 : 9 - 11)
viṉainavil yāṉai viṟaṟpōrp pāṇṭiyaṉ
pukaḻmali ciṟappiṟ koṟkai muṉṟuṟai
avirkatir muttamoṭu valampuri corintu  (akam. 201 : 3-5).
ilaṅkirum parappiṉ eṟicuṟā nīkki
valampuri mūḻkiya vāṉtimiṟ paratavar
olitalaip paṇilam ārppak kalleṉak
kalikeḻu koṟkai etirkoḷa iḻitarum  (akam. 350 : 10-13).
kāṇṭoṟuṅ kaluḻta laṉṟiyum īṇṭunīr
muttuppaṭu parappiṟ koṟkai muṉṟuṟai.  (naṟ. 23 : 5 - 6)
teṉkaṭal muttum kuṇakaṭal tukirum  (paṭṭiṉap. 189).
aṇikalattiṟkuriya poṉ, iyaṟkaiyākat tūyatum, puṭamiṭap paṭṭut tūyatākkap peṟṟatum, eṉa iruvakai. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu ōṭṭaṟṟa poṉ eṉappaṭum.
ōṭṭaṟṟa cempoṉ pōlē  (īṭu, 1, 10, 9)
māṟṟaṟiyāta ceḻum pacum poṉ
eṉṟu irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ kūṟiyatum ituvē.
puṭamiṭappaṭṭatu māṟṟuyarnta poṉ eṉappaṭum. pattarai māṟṟut taṅkam ciṟantateṉak kūṟuvatu ulakavaḻakku. tāyumāṉa aṭikaḷ itaip pattumāṟṟut taṅkam eṉpar.
pattumāṟṟut taṅka mākkiyē paṇikoṇṭa  (ciṉmayā. 7)
aparañci eṉṉum āyiratteṭṭu māṟṟup poṉṉum uḷḷatāka nūlkaḷ kūṟum,
āyiratteṭṭu māṟṟiṉ aparañci  (maccapu. tārakācurava. 26).
ayōkkiyar aḻaku aparañcic cimiḻil nañcu.
eṉṟoru piṟkālap paḻamoḻiyum uḷatu.
poṉṉiṉ māṟṟuraikkuṅ kal kaṭṭaḷaikkal eṉṟum, uraikkum kampi uraiyāṇi eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
mātavi, viralaṇi (kāṉ mōtiram), pariyakam (kāṟcavaṭi), nūpuram (cilampu), pāṭakam, cataṅkai, ariyakam (pāta cālam), kuṟaṅkuceṟi (kavāṉceṟi), viricikai, kaṇṭikai (māṇikka vaḷai), tōḷ vaḷai, cūṭakam, kaivaḷai (poṉvaḷai), pariyakam (pācittā maṇi, kaiccari), vālvaḷai (caṅka vaḷai, veḷḷi vaḷai), pavaḻavaḷai, vāḷaip pakuvāy mōtiram, maṇi mōtiram, marakatattāḷ ceṟi (marakatak kaṭai ceṟi), caṅkili (maṟat toṭari), nuṇñāṇ, āram, kayiṟkaṭai yoḻukiya kōvai (piṉṟāli), intiranīlak kaṭippiṇai (nīlak kutampai), teyvavutti (tiruttēvi), valampuri, toyyakam (talaippāḷai, pūrappāḷai), pullakam (teṉpalliyum vaṭapalliyum) eṉa 27 vakai aṇikaḷ aṇintiruntatākac cilappatikārak kaṭalāṭu kātai kūṟukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟuḷ aṭaṅkāta vēṟu cila aṇikalam akkālattiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu cūṭai allatu cūṭāmaṇi. peṇṭir araippaṭṭikai maṭṭum mēkalai, kāñci, kalāpam, parumam, viricikai eṉa aivakaip paṭṭiruntatu.
eṇkōvai kāñci eḻukōvai mēkalai
paṇkoḷ kalāpam patiṉāṟu - kaṇkoḷ
parumam patiṉeṭṭu muppat tiraṇṭu
viricikai yeṉṟuṇaraṟ pāṟṟu.
eṉpatu paḻañ ceyyuḷ.
ikkāla aṇikaḷē ēṟattāḻa aimpatu vakaippaṭṭiruppatāl, mutaṉmaiyāṉa aṇiyiṉaiyē iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kuṟit tiruttal vēṇṭum. ikkālattu araippaṭṭikai oṭṭiyāṇa mākum. iṅṅaṉamē cila aṇikaḷ vaṭivum  peyarum māṟiyuḷa.
ikkālattup pāṇṭi nāṭṭup paḻa nākarika makaḷir pōl, akkālattil ellāt tamiḻap peṇṭirum kātu vaḷarttiruntaṉar. kātu vaḷarkkumpōtu aṇivatu kutampaiyum, vaḷarttapiṉ aṇivatu kuḻaiyum kaṭippiṇaiyu mākum. kutampai iṉṟu kuṇukku eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
koṭuṅkuḻai tuṟantu vaṭintuvīḻ kātiṉaḷ  (cilap. 4 : 50)
vaḷḷait tāḷpōl vaṭikā tivaikāṇ
 uḷḷūṉ vāṭiya uṇaṅkal pōṉṟaṉa  (maṇi. 20 : 53-4).
eṉpaṉa kāṇka.
akkālattil āṭavarum aṇikalaṉ aṇivatu peruvaḻakku. vaṇikar kātiṟ kuṇṭalamum tōḷil (puyattil) kaṭakamum, mārpil maṇik kaṇṭikaiyum, aṇintiruntaṉar. piṟa vakuppār kātiṟ kaṭukkaṉum, kaiyiṟ kāppum aṇintiruntaṉar. kaṭukkaṉaip piṉpaṟṟiyē piṟkālak kamalam (kammal) eṉṉum makaḷir kātaṇi eḻuntatu. maṟavar tōḷiṟ kaṭakamum kāliṟ kaḻalum aṇintiruntaṉar. kaḻal piṟkālattil veṇṭaiyam eṉappaṭṭatu. kai mōtiram iru pālārkkum potuvām.
aracar, pāṇaṉukkup poṟṟāmaraiyum, avaṉ maṉaiviyākiya pāṭiṉikku allatu viṟalikkup poṉṉaṇikalamum, paricilāka aḷittu vantaṉar. viṟali viṟal (cattuvam)paṭa āṭupavaḷ. viṟal uḷḷak kuṟippāl uṭampil tōṉṟum vēṟupāṭu. avai kaṇṇīr vārtal, meymmayir cilirttal mutaliyaṉa.
13ām nūṟṟāṇṭil pāṇṭi nāṭṭaip pārkka vanta mārkkō pōlō eṉṉum veṉīcu nakara vaṇikar, cuntara pāṇṭiyaṉ taṉ kaḻuttil vilaimatikka voṇṇāta paṉmaṇi mālaiyum, mārpil vilaiyēṟap peṟṟa irumaṇiyāramum, tōḷil mūṉṟu paṉmuttak kaṭikaiyum, kāliṟ kaḻalum, kāl viralkaḷil mōtiramum aṇintirunta tākak kūṟiyuḷḷār.
maitīr pacumpoṉ māṇṭa maṇiyaḻuttic
ceyta teṉiṉum cerupputtaṉ kāṟkēyām
eytiya celvatta rāyiṉum kīḻkaḷaic
ceytoḻilāṟ kāṇap paṭum.  (nālaṭi. 347)
eṉṉum nālaṭic ceyyuḷāl, paṇṭai mūvēntariṉ ceruppu ettakaiya tāyirukkum eṉpatai uyttuṇaralām.
mayilukkut tōkaipōlap peṇṭirkkuk kūntal aḻaku taruvatāl, akkālattup peṇṭir tam kūntalai naṉṟāyp pēṇi, kuḻal, koṇṭai, paṉiccai, curuḷ, muṭi eṉṉum aivakaiyil aḻakupeṟa muṭittu vantaṉar. avai aimpāl eṉappaṭum. avaṟṟaik kuḻal, aḷakam, koṇṭai, paṉiccai, tuñcai eṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum; curuḷ, kural, aḷakam, tuñcu kuḻal, koṇṭai eṉac cāmuṇṭi tēvanāyakarum; ciṟitu vēṟupaṭak kūṟuvar. kūntalukku maṇam iyaṟkai eṉṉum aḷaviṟku naṟumaṇam ūṭṭuvatu oru taṉik kalaiyāyiruntatu. nāṟaiṅ kūntal eṉṟār iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷum (cilap. 10 : 43).
peṇṭir tam kaṇṇiṟku maiyūṭṭuvatu peru vaḻakkamā yiruntatu.
eḻutuṅkāṟ kōlkāṇāk kaṇṇēpōṟ koṇkaṉ
paḻikāṇēṉ kaṇṭa viṭattu.
eṉṉum kuṟaḷ (1285) itait terivikkum. maiyūṭṭuvataṉ peruvaḻakkiṉāl, uṇkaṇ eṉṉun toṭarē maiyuṇṭa kaṇṇaik kuṟikkum.
irunōk kivaḷuṇkaṇ ṇuḷḷatu.  (kuṟaḷ. 1091).
maiyūṭṭal kaṇṇiṟku aḻaku maṭṭumaṉṟik kuḷircciyum tarumeṉpatu, aṟiñar karuttu.
ikkālattu mēṉāṭṭup peṇṭir pōl, akkālat tamiḻap peṇṭirum tam utaṭukaṭkuc ceñcāyam ūṭṭi vantaṉar. atu avaraḻakaic ciṟappittataṉāl peṇṭiraḻakai vaṇṇikkum iṭamellām ilavitaḻc cevvāy (cilap. 14 : 136) eṉṟum, kovvaic cevvāy (tiruvāca. 6 : 2) eṉṟum, tuppuṟaḻ toṇṭaic cevvāy (cīvaka. 550) eṉṟum, piṟavāṟum, kūṟuvatu pulavar vaḻakkamāyiṟṟu.
iṉi, uḷḷaṅkāṟkum cenniṟam peṟac cempañcuk kuḻampu ūṭṭi vantamai. alattaka mūṭṭiya aṭi (maṇi. 6 : 110) eṉpatāl aṟiyalām. alattakam, cempañcuk kuḻampu.
makaḷir mārpilum tōḷilum kātalar cantaṉak kuḻampāl varaiyum ōviyaṅkaḷum, irupālārum neṟṟiyilum kaiyilum kuttuvikkum paccaik kōlamum, ōviya vuṇarcciyaiyaṉṟi nākarikattaik kāṭṭā.
6. 	uṟaiyuḷ
	uṟaiyuḷ eṉpatu kuṭiyirukkum vīṭu.
kuṟiñcinila vāṇarāṉa kuṟavar kuṉṟavar iṟavuḷar mutaliya vakuppārum, pālainilavāṇarāṉa maṟavar eyiṉar vēṭar mutaliya vakuppārum, ilai vēynta kuṭicaikaḷilum kuṟṟil kaḷilum; mullainilavāṇarāṉa iṭaiyar kūrai vēynta ciṟṟilkaḷilum maruta nilac ciṟṟūrvāṇarāṉa uḻavar. maṇcuvark kūrai vīṭukaḷilum; vatintārēṉum; marutanilap pērūrvāṇar ēntāṉa (vacatiyāṉa) paccaic ceṅkaṟcuvark kūrai vīṭukaḷilum cuṭṭa ceṅkaṟcuvark kārai vīṭukaḷilum vāḻntuvantaṉar. cuṭṭa ceṅkal, cuṭumaṇ eṉṟum cuṭumaṭ palakai eṉṟum collappaṭṭatu. paccaic ceṅkal maṭpalakai yeṉappaṭṭatu.
cuṭumaṇ ōṅkiya neṭunakar varaippil  (perumpāṇ. 405).
cuṭumaṭ palakaipala koṇarvittu  (periyapu. ēyarkōṉ. 49).
ciṟu ceṅkal iṭṭikai eṉappaṭṭatu.
kaṇcorīi iṭṭikai tīṟṟu pavar  (paḻa. 108).
iṭṭitu = ciṟitu (kuṟaḷ. 478). iṭṭimai = ciṟumai (tivā,). iṭṭiya = ciṟiya (aiṅkuṟu. 215).
iṭṭikai eṉpatu, vaṭamoḻiyil iṣṭikā eṉṟu tirintu taṉ ciṟappup poruḷaiyiḻantu, ceṅkal eṉṟu maṭṭum poruḷpaṭum.
ciṟiyatum periyatum uyarntatum tāḻntatum allātu naṭu nikarttāṉa uṟaiyuḷ, kuṭi, maṉai, il, vīṭu eṉṉum coṟkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṟ kuṟikkappeṟum. illam, vaḷamaṉai, māḷikai, nakar eṉpaṉa, peruñcelvar vāḻum ciṟanta uṟaiyuḷaik kuṟikkum. aracar vāḻum māḷikai araṇ peṟṟirukkumātalāl, araṇmaṉai yeṉap peṟum; aracaṉ maṉai eṉṉum poruḷiṟ kōyil eṉṟum collappeṟum.
kuṭi, nakar, māḷikai eṉṉum tūya tamiḻc coṟkaḷ vaṭamoḻic ceṉṟum vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
mēṉilaiyuḷḷa vīṭu māṭam eṉappaṭṭatu. atu ulaka vaḻakkil māṭivīṭu eṉavum mettaivīṭu eṉavum vaḻaṅkum. irunilai mutal eḻunilai varai akkālattu māṭaṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉakil āvivimmum eḻunilaimāṭañ cērntum (cīvaka. 2840).
ovvoru māṭamum allatu māḷikaiyum, cuṟṟuc cuvar, mukamaṇṭapam, talaivācal, iṭaikaḻi, (naṭai), muṉkaṭṭu, uḷmuṟṟam, piṉkaṭṭu, kūṭam, aṭukkaḷai (camaiyalaṟai), puḻaikkaṭai (kollaippuṟam), maṉaikkiṇaṟu, kuḷippaṟai, calakkappurai (kakkūcu), cālakam allatu aṅkaṇam eṉṉum pakutikaḷaiyuṭaiyatāyiruntatu. mēṉilaiyil nilāmuṟṟa miruntatu.
vakaipeṟa eḻuntu vāṉam mūḻkic
 cilkāṟ ṟicaikkum palpuḻai nalil
eṉṉum maturaikkāñci aṭikaṭku (357 -8).
maṇṭapam kūṭam tāykkaṭṭu aṭukkaḷai eṉṟāṟ pōlum peyarkaḷaip peṟutaliṉ, vakaipeṟa veḻunteṉṟār eṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ciṟappurai varaintiruttal kāṇka:
vaṇaṅku - vāṅku - vaṅku - aṅku. o.nō : vaḷai - aḷai. aṅkutal = vaḷaital, cāytal, aṅku - aṅkaṇam. o. nō : cāykaṭai - cākkaṭai.
kāṟṟu varumvaḻi, kālatar, cāḷaram, palakaṇi eṉṉum peyarkaḷaip peṟṟiruntatu.
kārai vīṭukaḷ ucciyil ōṭu vēyntatum maccup pāviyum iruvakaiyā yiruntaṉa. ōṭum, ilakkiya vaḻakkil cuṭumaṇ eṉappaṭṭatu.
cuṭumaṇ ēṟā vaṭunīṅku ciṟappiṉ
 muṭiyara coṭuṅkum kaṭimaṉai
eṉpataṟku (cilap. 14 : 146-7), ōṭu vēyātu poṟṟakaṭu vēynta maṉai eṉṟu aruñcolluraikārar ōr urai varaintiruppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. muṭivēntarum virumpum nāṭakak kaṇikaiyar maṉaikaḷātaliṉ, poṟṟakaṭu vēyntatākavum iruntirukkalām. kāñci nakariṟ palar kūṭutaṟkuriya potu ampalamum poṉṉāl vēyap paṭṭiruntatāka maṇimēkalai kūṟutal kāṇka.
cālaiyuṅ kūṭamum tamaṉiyap potiyilum  (maṇi. 28 : 66)
maḻaitōyum uyarmāṭattu
eṉṉum paṭṭiṉappālai (145) yaṭiyum,
māṭam aḻintakkāl maṟṟum eṭuppatōr
 kūṭam marattiṟkut tuppākum.
eṉṉum paḻamoḻic ceyyuḷum (71) māṭattiṉ perumaiyai uṇarttum.
7. 	ūrtiyum pōkkuvarattum
	ūrtal ēṟic cellutal, ūrti ēṟic celluṅ karuvi.
	nilavūrtikaḷ aṇikam, vaṇṭi, vilaṅku eṉa mūvakaippaṭum.
	aṇikam, civikai, pallakku, mēṉā, capparam eṉpaṉa.
civikai, iruvar kāvic cellum kūṇṭup pallakku. pallakku eṇmarukkuk kuṟaiyātu tōḷil tāṅkic cellum tiṟanta taṇṭaiyap paṭaip pallakku. capparam uruḷiyiṉṟi oṟṟait taṭṭuḷḷa cappai (moṭṭai)t tēr.
civikai eṉpatu cipikkā eṉṟum, pallakku eṉpatu paryaṅka eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum. capparam iṉṟu teyvap paṭimaiyaik koṇṭu cellavē payaṉpaṭuttap peṟum.
vaṇṭi : cakaṭam, kūṭārappaṇṭi, kollāppaṇṭi, vaiyam, pāṇṭil, vaṅkam, tēr eṉpaṉa. cakaṭam potuvakaiyāṉa māṭṭu vaṇṭi, kūṭārap paṇṭi kūṇṭu vaṇṭi. kollāp paṇṭi ciṟanta kāḷaikaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟatum perumakkaḷ ēṟic celvatumāṉa nākarikak kūṇṭu vaṇṭi, vaiyam iru kutirai pūṇṭiḻukkum tēr pōṉṟa vaṇṭi. pāṇṭil īruruḷiyuḷḷa kutirai vaṇṭi. vaṅkam paḷḷiyōṭa vaṇṭi : atu paḷḷi yōṭam pōṉṟatu. tēr ikkālat tēr pōṟ ciṟiyatu.
vilaṅku : kāḷai, kutirai, kōvēṟu kaḻutai, yāṉai, oṭṭakam eṉpaṉa.
nīrūrtikaḷ : puṇai, parical, kaṭṭumaram, ōṭam, ampi, timil, paḵṟi, tōṇi. paṭaku, nīrmāṭam (paḷḷiyōṭam) nāvāy (kappal) eṉpaṉa. nāvāy, vaṅkam, kappal eṉpaṉa ciṟitu vēṟu paṭṭavaiyāyumirukkalām.
tamiḻ nāṭṭilum vaṭa nāṭukaḷilumuḷḷa pērūrkaṭkum kō nakar kaṭkum, taṭivaḻi eṉṉum peruñcālaikaḷ ceṉṟaṉa. iccālaikaḷ kāṭukaḷiṟ kūṭum kavartta iṭaṅkaḷil, avvan nāṭṭu vēntaṉiṉ viṟpaṭaikaḷ, vaṇikac cāttukaṭku vaḻippaṟikkum kaḷvarālum koḷḷaikkārarālum poruṭ cētamum āṭcētamum nērāvāṟu iravum pakalum kāttu niṉṟaṉa.
uṭampiṭit taṭakkai yōṭā vampalar

aṇarccevik kaḻutaic cāttoṭu vaḻaṅkum
ulkuṭaip peruvaḻik kavalai kākkum
villuṭai vaippiṉ viyaṉkāṭ ṭiyaviṉ  (perumpāṇ. 76-82).
eṉṟu kaṭiyalūr uruttiraṅ kaṇṇaṉār toṇṭai nāṭṭaip paṟṟik kūṟutal kāṇka.
nāṭu kaippaṟṟaṟkum vāṇikattiṟkum ayalnāṭu pārttaṟkum, aracarum vaṇikarum potu makkaḷum cellak kūṭiya nāvāy eṉṉum peruṅkalaṅkaḷ, kīḻkaṭalilum mēlkaṭalilum aṭikkaṭi ceṉṟu koṇṭiruntaṉa.
vāṉiyainta irumunnīrp
pēenilaiiya irum pavvattuk
koṭumpuṇari vilaṅkupōḻak
kaṭuṅkāloṭu karaicēra
neṭuṅkoṭi micaiyitaiyeṭut
tiṉṉicaiya muracamuḻaṅkap
poṉmalinta viḻuppaṇṭam
nāṭāra naṉkiḻitarum
āṭiyaṟ perunāvāy
maḻaimuṟṟiya malaipuraiyat
tuṟaimuṟṟiya tuḷaṅkirukkait
teṇkaṭaṟ kuṇṭakaḻic
cīrcāṉṟa vuyarnelliṉ
ūrkoṇṭa vuyarkoṟṟava.
eṉṟu maturaikkāñci (75-88), vaṭimpalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ cāli (cāvaka)t tīvaik kaippaṟṟiyatai, avaṉ vaḻit tōṉṟalāṉa talaiyālaṅkāṉattuc ceruveṉṟa neṭuñ ceḻiyaṉ mītu ēṟṟikkūṟutal kāṇka.
kalantanta poṟparicam
kaḻittōṇiyāṟ karaicērkkuntu
malaittāramum kaṭaṟṟāramum
talaippeytu varunarkkīyum
puṉalaṅ kaḷḷiṉ polantārk kuṭṭuvaṉ
muḻaṅkukaṭal muḻaviṉ muciṟi yaṉṉa  (puṟam. 343 : 5-10).
eṉpatu vaṇikam paṟṟiyatu.
cātuvaṉ eṉpōṉ takavila ṉāki
