vaṅkam pōkum vaṇikar tammuṭaṉ
taṅkā vēṭkaiyiṉ tāṉum celvuḻi
eṉpatu, (maṇi. 16 : 4-12) ayal nāṭu kāṇac ceṉṟamai paṟṟiyatu.
8. vāḻkkai vakai
ulaka vāḻkkai, illaṟam tuṟavaṟam taṉi vāḻkkai eṉa mūvakaippaṭum.
oru kaṟpuṭaip peṇṇai maṇantu illattiliruntu aṟañ ceytu vāḻum vāḻkkai illaṟamākum. ulakap paṟṟai yoḻittu vīṭupēṟṟu muyaṟciyil īṭupaṭuvatu tuṟavaṟamākum. maṇañ ceyyātu ulakap paṟṟōṭu taṉiyāyiruppatu taṉi vāḻkkai (Celibacy) ākum. ivaṟṟuḷ illaṟattaiyē ciṟantatākak koṇṭaṉar tamiḻar. iṟaivaṉ makkaḷai āṇum peṇṇu māy paṭaittiruppatē, avar kūṭi vāḻtaṟ poruṭṭē. illaṟattālum oruvar vīṭupēṟṟai aṭaiya muṭiyum.
aṟattāṟṟiṉ ilvāḻkkai yāṟṟiṉ puṟattāṟṟiṟ
pōoyp peṟuva tevaṉ  (kuṟaḷ. 46).
aṟaṉeṉap paṭṭatē yilvāḻkkai aḵtum
piṟaṉpaḻippa tillāyiṉ naṉṟu.  (kuṟaḷ. 49).
eṉṟār tamiḻ aṟanūlāciriyar tiruvaḷḷuvar. ivaṟṟaiyē,
illaṟa mallatu nallaṟa maṉṟu
eṉac curuṅkac coṉṉār piṟkālattu auvaiyār. āṭavarum peṇṭirum kūṭātu vāḻvatu aritāyiruppatuṭaṉ, tuṟavaṟat tiṟkum illaṟamē iṉṟiya maiyāta tuṇaiyākiṉṟatu.
mēṟkūṟiya mūvakai vāḻkkaiyuḷ, ovvoṉṟum nallatum tīyatum eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. illaṟattil orē maṉaiviyuṭaṉ vāḻvatu nallatu; pala maṉaiviyaruṭaṉ allatu pala peṇ ṭiraik kātalittu vāḻvatu tīyatu. tuṟavaṟattil uṇmaiyā yiruppatu nallatu; uḷḷoṉṟum puṟam poṉṟumāyiruppatu tīyatu; atu kūṭāvoḻukkam eṉappaṭum. taṉi vāḻkkaiyil oru peṇṇaiyum kātaliyā tiruppatu nallatu; maṟaivāyk kātalittoḻukuvatu tīyatu. ivaṟṟuḷ nalla vaṟṟaiyē tamiḻ mēlōr pōṟṟiṉar.
ulaka vāḻkkaikku iṉṟiyamaiyāt tuṇaiyāyiruppatāl maṉaivikku vāḻkkait tuṇaivi eṉṟu peyar.
illaṟam
maṇap paruvam vanta piṉpē, palavakaiyilum otta ōr iḷaiña ṉum iḷaiñaiyum, tāmākak kūṭiyō tam peṟṟōrāṟ kūṭṭappaṭṭō, kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyumāki vāḻvatu, āriya varukaikku muṟpaṭṭa paṇṭait tamiḻa marapu. maṇamāṉamaikku aṭaiyāḷamāka, maṉaiviyiṉ kaḻuttil tāli eṉṉum maṅkalavaṇi aṇiyap peṟum.
kaṇavaṉum maṉaiviyum eṅṅaṉam kūṭiyiruppiṉum kātal avarkku iṉṟiyamaiyātatākum kātal eṉpatu iṟakkum varaiyum oruvarai yoruvar iṉṟiyamaiyāmai. kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyariṭaip paṭṭa kātal, kāmam eṉṉum ciṟappup peyar peṟum. accol iṉṟu peṇṇācai eṉṉum tīya poru ḷil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
kāmattai orutalaik kāmam, irutalaik kāmam, poruntāk kāmam eṉa mūvakaiyāy vakuttaṉar muṉṉōr. ōr āṭavaṉum peṇṭumākiya iruvaruḷ, oruvar maṭṭum kātalippatu oru talaik kāmam; yārēṉum oruvar neṟi tavaṟik kātalippatu poruntāk kāmam. ivaṟṟuḷ irutalaik kāmamē ciṟantatākavum neṟippaṭṭatākavum koḷḷappaṭṭatu. peṟṟōrum maṟṟōrumiṉṟit tāmākak kūṭuvatellām, perumpālum irutalaik kāmamākavē yirukkum.
kāmattai akapporuḷ eṉṟum, orutalaik kāmattaik kaikkiḷai eṉṟum, irutalaik kāmattai aṉpiṉ aintiṇai eṉṟum; poruntāk kāmattaip peruntiṇai eṉṟum ilakkaṇam kūṟum. aṉpiṉ aintiṇaiyē neṟippaṭṭatākak koḷḷappaṭṭatiṉāl ataiyē akam eṉṟu ciṟappittum, ēṉaiyiraṇṭaiyum akappuṟam eṉṟu iḻittum, kūṟuvar ilakkaṇiyar.
kaikkiḷai, kuṟippu eṉṟum maṇam eṉṟum iru vakaiyāyc collappaṭum. oruvaṉ kāmavuṇarcciyillāta oru ciṟumiyiṭam allatu kātalillāta oru peṇṇiṭam, cila kātaṟkuṟippuc coṟkaḷai maṭṭum tāṉē colli yiṉpuṟutal kaikkiḷaik kuṟippām. peṟṟōrāṟ kūṭṭap peṟṟa kaṇavaṉ maṉaivi yaruḷ, yārēṉum oruvar kātalillāmalē icaintiruppiṉ atu kaikkiḷai maṇamām. kaikkiḷai orupakkak kātal. kai pakkam. kiḷai kātal.
oru peṇṇai maṇavuṟavu muṟai tappiyō, valintō, ēmāṟṟiyō, tūkka nilaiyilō, nōy nilaiyilō, iṟanta piṉpō pūppu niṉṟa piṉpō kūṭuvatum, taṉpāloṭum vilaṅkoṭum kūṭuvatum, peruntiṇaiyām. itu iṅṅaṉam palavakaiyāyp perukiyiruppatāṟ peruntiṇai yeṉap paṭṭatu. tamiḻukkuc ciṟappāṉa poruḷilakkaṇam āriya varukaikku eṇṇāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpē amaintu viṭṭataṉāl, āriya voḻukka nūlkaḷiṟ collappaṭṭa eṇvakai maṇattuḷ tāṉ oṉṟē nālvakai peṟṟatiṉāl peruntiṇai yeṉap paṭṭateṉpatu, kālamalaivum nūṉ malaivuṅ kalanta peruvaḻuvām.
peṟṟōr ceytu vaikkum maṇam, pēccu maṇamum aruñceyal maṇamum eṉa irutiṟappaṭum. maṇavāḷap piḷḷai vīṭṭār pōyk kēṭka, peṇ vīṭṭār icaintu peṇ koṭuppatu. pēccu maṇam; peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr kuṟitta ōr aṟaviṉaiyō maṟaviṉaiyō ceytu peṇṇaik koḷvatu aruñceyal maṇam. paṇṭait tamiḻakattil kollēṟu taḻuvi ataṟkuriya peṇṇai maṇappatu; mullai nila vaḻakkamāyiruntatu. maṇamāṉa aṉṟē maṇamakkaḷ kūṭuvar.
koṭuppārum aṭuppārumiṉṟik kātalar tāmākak kūṭum kūṭṭam, maṟaivākak toṭaṅkuvatum veḷippaṭaiyāyt toṭaṅkuvatum eṉa iruvakaippaṭum. maṟaivāṉa kūṭṭam kaḷavu eṉṟum, veḷippaṭaiyāṉa kūṭṭam kaṟpu eṉṟum, collappeṟum. kaḷavu perumpālum irumātattiṟkuṭ paṭṭēyirukkum atu veḷippaṭṭapiṉ kaṟpām. kaṟpellām karaṇam eṉṉum tālikaṭṭuc caṭaṅkōṭum pantalaṇi, maṇamuḻā, vāḻttu, varicai uṟṟāruṭaṉ uṇṇum uṇṭāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟōṭum kūṭiya maṇaviḻāvoṭu toṭaṅkum. kaḷavuk kālattil kūṭṭam taṭaippaṭiṉum, peṇṇiṉ peṟṟōr piṟarkkup peṇ koṭukka icaiyiṉum, kātalaṉ kātaliyaik kūṭṭik koṇṭu vēṟṟūr ceṉṟuviṭum uṭaṉ pōkkum uṇṭu. avar tirumpi vantapiṉ, kātalaṉ vīṭṭilēṉum kātali vīṭṭilēṉum vatuvai eṉṉum maṇaviḻā nikaḻum.
iṉi, kaḷavukkālattil kūṭṭam taṭaippaṭuvatāl, kātali taṉ kātalaṉaik kāṇappeṟāmal maṉam varunti mēṉi vēṟupaṭumpōtu, peṟṟōr vēlaṉ eṉṉum mantirakkāraṉai varavaḻaittu tam makaḷ nōykkuk karaṇiyam (kāraṇam) viṉavuvatum, avaṉ atu murukaṉāl nērntateṉṟu kūṟi, veḷḷāṭṭuk kaṟiyum kaḷḷum vilāppuṭaikka vuṇṭu veṟiyāṭṭu eṉṉum kūttai nikaḻtti annōyaip pōkku vatāka naṭippatum uṇṭu. aṉṟu kātali nērākavō taṉ tōḻi vāyilākavō, taṉ peṟṟōriṭam uḷḷataic colli viṭuvāḷ. atu aṟattoṭu niṟṟal eṉappaṭum. taṉ kātalaṉaṉṟi vēṟu yārkkum taṉṉaip pēciṉum, kātali aṟattoṭu niṟpāḷ; ataṉpiṉ kātalaṉukku maṇañ ceytuvaikkap peṟu vāḷ. maṇamakaṉ allatu avaṉ vīṭṭār maṇamakaḷukkup paricam koṭuppar. maṇamakaṉ paricam peṟum anākarika māṉaṅkeṭṭa āriya iḻivaḻakku akkālattillai.
tamiḻap peṇṭir kaṟpiṟ ciṟantavarātaliṉ, oruvarai maṇantapiṉ allatu kātalitta piṉ vēṟoruvaraiyum kaṉavilum karutuvatillai; vēṟu evaraiyēṉum maṇakka nēriṉ, uṭaṉē uyirai viṭṭu viṭuvar.
kātalar kūṭum kūṭṭam, uṭampāṟ kūṭuvatum uḷḷattāṟ kūṭuvatum eṉa iruvakai. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu meyyuṟu puṇarcci eṉṟum, piṉṉatu uḷḷappuṇarcci eṉṟum, collappeṟum. kaṟpuṭaip peṇṭirkku iraṇṭum oṉṟē. itaṉālaṉṟō, tilakavatiyammaiyār tamakkup pēcap peṟṟirunta kalippakaiyār pōrk kaḷattiṟ paṭṭapiṉ iṟakkat tuṇintatum, ataṉ piṉ tam oṟṟaik koru tampiyār tirunāvukkaracariṉ poruṭṭu uyir tāṅkiyatum, iṟuti varai maṇavātiruntatum, eṉka.
(āriyar varumuṉ) karaṇam eṉṉum tāli kaṭṭuc caṭaṅkai, ūrt talaivaṉ, kuṭi mutiyōṉ, maṅkala mutu peṇṭir, kulap pūcāri mutaliyōr naṭatti vaittaṉar.
paṇṭaiyaracarum peruñcelvarum perumpālum ciṟṟiṉpa vuṇarcci ciṟantu, pala tēviyaraiyum kāmak kiḻatti iṟparattai kātaṟparattai mutaliyōraiyum koṇṭiruntamai yāl, ōruyirum īruṭalumāṉa irutalaik kāma iṉpa vāḻkkai, pūtapāṇṭiyaṉum avaṉ tēviyum pōṉṟa oru cila aracak kuṭumpaṅkaḷiṭaiyum, uḻavarum iṭaiyarum pōṉṟa potu makkaḷiṭaiyum. pula makkaḷiṭaiyumtāṉ perumpālum iruntu vantatu.
maṭaṅkaliṟ ciṉaii maṭaṅkā vuḷḷat
taṭaṅkāt tāṉai vēntar uṭaṅkiyain
teṉṉoṭu porutum eṉpa avarai
āramar alaṟat tākkit tēro
ṭavarppuṟaṅ kāṇē ṉāyiṉ ciṟanta
pērama ruṇkaṇ ivaḷiṉum pirika
eṉṟu pūtapāṇṭiyaṉ taṉ pakaivarai nōkkik kūṟiya vañciṉamum (puṟam. 71), avaṉ iṟantapiṉ uṭaṉkaṭṭaiyēṟiya avaṉ tēvi yāṭiya,
palcāṉ ṟīrē palcāṉ ṟīrē
celkeṉac collā toḻikeṉa vilakkum
pollāc cūḻccip palcāṉ ṟīrē
.........................................................................................................
peruṅkāṭṭup paṇṇiya karuṅkōṭ ṭīmam
numakkari tākuka tilla emakkem
peruntōṭ kaṇavaṉ māynteṉa arumpaṟa
vaḷḷitaḻ aviḻnta tāmarai
naḷḷirum poykaiyum tīyum ōraṟṟē.
eṉṉum pāṭṭum (puṟam. 246), aṟiñar uḷḷattai eṉṟum urukkun taṉmaiya.
oruvaṉuṭaiya maṉaivi urimaip peṇṇāyiṉum perumaip peṇṇāyiṉum uḻuvaṟ peṇṇāyiṉum, mūvakaiyum ūḻiṉ payaṉē eṉṟu muṉṉōr karutiṉar. tāramum kuruvum talaiviti (āciriyaṉum maṉaiviyum amaivatu ūḻmuṟai) eṉṉum piṟkālap paḻamoḻiyum, eṅkē muṭipōṭṭu vaittiruk kiṟatō aṅkētāṉ muṭiyum eṉṟu kūṟum vaḻakkac collum, “Marriage are made in heaven,” “Marriage and hanging go by destiny” eṉṉum āṅkilap paḻamoḻikaḷum iṅkuk karutattakkaṉa. vāḻkkait tuṇaiyākiya maṉaivikku ūḻt tuṇai eṉṟum peyar.
ammāṉ makaḷum akkai makaḷumpōl, maṇakkakkūṭiya uṟavumuṟaip peṇ urimaip peṇ; uṟavumuṟai yiṉṟic celvak kuṭumpattiṉiṉṟu eṭukkum peṇ perumaip peṇ; iraṇṭumiṉṟi oruvaṉ tāṉē kaṇṭavuṭaṉ kātalittu maṇakkum peṇ uḻuvaṟ peṇ pala piṟappākat toṭarntu maṉaiviyāy varupavaḷ uḻuvaṟ peṇ. eṉpatu, piṟavit toṭar nampikkaiyāḷar karuttu. uḻuvalaṉpaip payiliyatu keḻīiya naṭpu eṉpar iṟaiyaṉār (kuṟun. 2). ūḻāl ēṟpaṭṭa āvalai uḻuval eṉṟaṉar. itait teyvappuṇarcciyeṉṟum, iyaṟkaip puṇarcciyeṉṟum, nūlkaḷ kūṟum.
mūvakaip peṇkaḷuḷḷum uḻuvaṟ peṇṇai maṇappatē kulamata nilaccārpu kaṭantatākaliṉ, teyvattāl nērntatāka vitantu kūṟappeṟum.
kātalaṉ kaḷavuk kālattil taṉ kātaliyai nōkki, ulaka muḻuvataiyum peṟṟālum nāṉ uṉṉaik kaiviṭēṉ eṉṟu (kuṟun.300) uṟuti kūṟiyataṟku ēṟpa, kaṟpuk kālattil, nī toṭṭatu nañcāyiruntālum eṉakkuk tēvar amutamākum. (tol, kaṟpiyal, 5) eṉṟum, nī eṉakku vēppaṅkāyait tantālum atu tīñcuvaik kaṟkaṇṭu pōl iṉikkum (kuṟun.166) eṉṟum, uṉ kūntalaippōl naṟumaṇamuḷḷa malarai nāṉ ulakil eṅkuṅ kaṇṭatillaiyeṉṟum, (kuṟun.2) palapaṭap pārāṭṭi avaḷai mēṉmēlum ūkki iṉpuṟuttu vatu vaḻakkam.
kātal maṉaiviyum, taṉ kaṇavaṉait teyvam pōṟ pēṇi, avaṉ iṭṭa cūḷai (āṇaiyai) niṟaivēṟṟāviṭattu ataṉāl avaṉukkut tīṅku nērātavāṟu teyvattai vēṇṭik koḷvatum, avaṉ cūḷ tappavillai yeṉpatum (kuṟun.87), taṉ talaivaṉ kuṟṟattaip piṟar eṭutturaippiṉ atai maṟuttu avaṉaip pukaḻvatum, (kuṟun. 3), taṉ kaṇavaṉum tāṉum oruṅkē iṟakka vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpuvatum (kuṟun. 57), vaḻakkam.
aracarum maṟavarum pōr ceyvataṟkum, muṉivarum pulavarum tūtupaṟṟiyum, vaṇikar poruḷīṭṭaṟkum, vēṟṟūrum vēṟṟu nāṭum cella nēriṉ, avar tirumpi varumvarai avar maṉaiviyar āṟṟiyiruppatum, cuvariṟ kōṭiṭṭu nāḷeṇṇi varuvatum, avar kuṟitta kālattil varāviṭiṉ viraintu varumāṟu teyvattai vēṇṭuvatum, iyalpām.
kaṇavaṉukkuk kaṟpuṭai maṉaiviyum, peṟṟōrukku aṟivuṭai makkaḷum, ciṟanta pēṟākak karutappaṭṭaṉar.
eṉṉoṭu porutum eṉpa avarai
āramar alaṟat tākkit tēroṭu
avarppuṟaṅ kāṇē ṉāyiṉ ciṟanta
pēramar uṇkaṇ ivaḷiṉum pirika.
eṉṟu pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ vañciṉaṅ kūṟutalum, cēṇāṟu nallicaic cēyiḻai kaṇava eṉṟu (patiṟ. 88), kuṭakkō iḷañcēral irumpoṟaiyum, ceyirtīr kaṟpiṟ cēyiḻai kaṇava eṉṟu (puṟam. 3), pāṇṭiyaṉ karuṅkai yoḷvāṭ perum peyar vaḻutiyum, pārāṭṭap peṟutalum kāṇka.
peṇṇiṟ peruntakka yāvuḷa kaṟpeṉṉum
tiṇmaiyuṇ ṭākap peṟiṉ.
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar (kuṟaḷ. 54).
iṉi makkaṭ pēṟupaṟṟi,
paṭaippuppala paṭaittup palarō ṭuṇṇum
uṭaipperuñ celva rāyiṉum iṭaippaṭak
kuṟukuṟu naṭantu ciṟukai nīṭṭi
iṭṭun toṭṭuṅ kavviyun tuḻantum
neyyuṭai yaṭicil meypaṭa vitirttum
mayakkuṟu makkaḷai yillōrkkup
payakkuṟai yillaittām vāḻum nāḷē.
eṉṟu (puṟam. 188) pāṇṭiyaṉ aṟivuṭai nampiyum,
poṉṉuṭaiya rēṉum pukaḻuṭaiya rēṉumaṟ
ṟeṉṉuṭaiya rēṉum uṭaiyarō - iṉṉaṭicil
pukkaḷaiyum tāmaraikkaip pūnāṟum ceyyavāy
makkaḷaiyīṅ killā tavar.
eṉṟu pukaḻēntip pulavarum (naḷaveṇpā, kalitoṭar. 63).
peṟumavaṟṟuḷ yāmaṟinta tillai yaṟivaṟinta
makkaṭpē ṟalla piṟa.
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvarum (kuṟaḷ. 61), kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
kaṇavaṉ tavaṟṟālō maṉaiviyiṉ pētaimaiyālō, cila camaiyaṅkaḷil avariṭaip piṇakku nērvatuṇṭu. aṉṟu maṉaivi ūṭik kaṇavaṉoṭu pēcātiruppāḷ. ūṭutal caṭaivu koḷḷutal. atu kaṇavaṉāl eḷitāyt tīrkkappaṭum. atu caṟṟuk kaṭumaiyāṉāl pulavi eṉappaṭum. atu kuḻantaiyaik kaṇavaṉ eṭuttuk koṇṭu pōyk koṭuppataṉālum, vīṭṭiṟku viruntiṉar vantiruppatālum, uṟaviṉarum naṇparum talaiyiṭuvatālum, tīrkkappaṭum. pulavi muṟṟi viṭṭāl tuṉi eṉappaṭum. atai oruvarālum tīrkka muṭiyātu. nīṇṭa nāṭ ceṉṟu atu tāṉē taṇiyum. paḻakap paḻakap pālum puḷippatu pōl, kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyariṭaippaṭṭa kāmaviṉpam caṟṟuc cuvai kuṟaiyumpōtu atai niṟaittaṟkup pulaviyum vēṇṭu meṉṟum, atu uṇaviṟku uppiṭuvatu pōṉṟa teṉṟum, ūṭal uppuk kuṟaivatum tuṉi uppu mikuvatum pōṉṟavai yeṉṟum, uppu mikaiyāṟ cuvai keṭuvatupōl tuṉiyāl iṉpaṅ keṭumātalāl annilaiyai aṭaiyātavāṟu pulaviyait taṭuttuviṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟuvar.
uppamain taṟṟāṟ pulavi atu ciṟitu
mikkaṟṟāl nīḷa viṭal.  (kuṟaḷ. 1302).
peṇṭir ettuṇaik kalvi kaṟṟavarāyiruppiṉum, uḻattiyarum iṭaicciyarum maṟattiyarum kuṟattiyarum paṇṭamāṟṟup peṇṭirum kūliyāṭṭi yarum vēlaikkāriyarum vaṟiyavarumā yiruntāloḻiya, maṇamāṉapiṉ, mūppaṭaiyu maṭṭum, kaṇavarōṭum peṟṟōrōṭum aṇṇaṉ akkai mārōṭum māmiyārōṭum pāṭṭaṉ pāṭṭimārōṭumaṉṟi, vīṭṭai viṭṭu veḷiyē taṉiyē cellap peṟār.
kaṇavaṉaip pēṇutalum camaiyal ceytalum piḷḷai vaḷarttalum kaṇavaṉillātapōtu vīṭṭaik kāttalumē, peṇṭirkku iyaṟkaiyāl allatu iṟaivaṉāl vakukkap paṭṭa paṇiyeṉpatu, paṇṭait tamiḻar karuttu. maṇamāṉa peṇ vīṭṭilēyēyiruntu vēlai ceyva taṉālēyē, avaḷukku illāḷ, illak kiḻatti, maṉaivi, maṉaiyāḷ, maṉaiyāṭṭi, maṉaikkiḻatti, vīṭṭukkāri eṉṉum iṭam paṟṟiya peyarkaḷum, il, maṉai, kuṭi eṉṉum iṭavāku peyar kaḷum ēṟpaṭṭaṉa.
vīṭṭiṟku allatu illattiṟku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷai yellām kaṇavaṉē īṭṭa vēṇṭumeṉpatum, ataṉāl maṉaiviyum iḷa makkaḷum iṉpamāy vāḻavēṇṭumeṉ patum, paṇṭaiyōr karuttu.
viṉaiyē āṭavark kuyirē vāḷnutal
maṉaiyuṟai makaḷirk kāṭavar uyir.
eṉṉum kuṟuntokaic ceyyuḷaṭikaḷ (135 : 1-2), itaṉaip pulappaṭuttum.
ikkālattiṟ kālaimutal mālai varai āṭavar kaṭumaiyāy uḻaittum, kuṭumpattiṟkup pōtiya aḷavu poruḷ tēṭavō uṇavup poruḷ koḷḷavō muṭiyavillai. itaṉālēyē, peṇṭir veḷiyēṟi āciriyap paṇiyum araciyalaluvaṟ paṇiyum āṟṟa vēṇṭiyatākiṉṟatu. ākavē, iṉṟu avar kaṭamai iru maṭaṅkāyp perukiyuḷḷatu. innilaimai makkaṭ perukkaiyum uṇavut taṭṭaiyum kāṭṭumēṉum, itaṟku aṭippaṭaik karaṇiyam araciyal tavaṟē.
peṇṭirait taṉiyē vīṭṭaiviṭṭu veḷip pōkkāmaikku iṉṉoru karaṇiyamuṇṭu. avar potuvāka āṭavarāl, ciṟappākak kāmukarāl, nukarccip poruḷākak karutappaṭum nilaimai iṉṉum māṟavillai. āṭavarai nōkka, avar meṉmaiyar, valuvaṟṟavar. itaṉāl avarkku melliyal, acaiyiyal, taḷariyal eṉṉum peyarkaḷ ilakkiyattil ēṟpaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. tīyōrāl avarkkum avaruṟaviṉarkkum cētamum māṉak kēṭum nērāvaṇṇamē, avar tuṇaiyō ṭaṉṟi veḷiyē aṉuppappaṭuvatillai. āṭavar nāṉmuḻa vēṭṭiyuṭukkum pōtu, peṇṭir patiṉeṇ muḻac cēlai yaṇivatum, ik karaṇiyam paṟṟiyē.
ākavē, peṇṭirai veḷiviṭātiruppatu, avaruṭaiya nalam pēṇalēyaṉṟi avaraic ciṟaippaṭuttalākātu. kaṭaikaṭkum kōyiṟkum tiruviḻāviṟkum uṟaviṉar vīṭṭu maṅkala amaṅkala nikaḻccikaṭkum piṟa iṭaṅkaṭkum, tuṇaiyoṭu pōy vara avarkku nirampa vāyppuṇṭu.
pūppaṭainta kaṉṉip peṇkaḷaiyum takka tuṇaiyiṉṟi veḷiyē viṭuva tillai.
kaṇavaṉ, poruḷīṭṭal paṟṟiyō tīyoḻukkam paṟṟiyō pirintiruk kumpōtu, kaṟpuṭai maṉaivi taṉṉai aṇi ceytu koḷvatillai; maṅkalavaṇi tavira maṟṟavaṟṟai yellām kaḻaṟṟi viṭuvāḷ.
akkālattil, ilvāḻkkaikkuriya aṟaṅkaḷaiyum ceyvatillai.
aṟavōrk kaḷittalum antaṇa rōmpalum
tuṟavōrk ketirtalum tollōr ciṟappiṉ
viruntetir kōṭalum iḻanta eṉṉai.
eṉṟu (cilap. 16 : 71-73), kaṇṇaki maturaiyil taṉ kaṇavaṉai nōkkik kūṟutal kāṇka.
maṟumukam pārāta kaṟpeṉpatu kaṇavaṉ maṉaivi yiruvarkkum potuvēṉum, pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ pōṉṟa oru cilarē avvaṟattaik kaṭaippiṭitta āṭavarāvar; peṇṭirō paṟpallāyiravar. kātalilum āṭavar peṇṭirkku īṭākār. uṭaṉkaṭṭaiyēṟutalum uṭaṉuyir viṭutalumē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟākum. maṟumaṇañ ceyyāta kaimmai nilaiyum peṇṭir ciṟappaik kāṭṭum.
pulavar, aracar, vaṇikar, veḷḷāḷar ākiya vakuppukkaḷaic cērnta peṇṭir, kaṇavaṉ iṟanta piṉ, eṭṭām nāḷ iṟutic caṭaṅkil maṅkalavaṇiyum piṟavaṇikaḷum nīkkappeṟuvar. atu tāliyaṟuppu eṉappaṭum. ataṉ piṉ veḷḷāṭaiyaṇintu vēṟevaraiyum maṇavāmal tam eñciya kālattaik kaḻippar. avar uyarkuṭip piṟantavar eṉappaṭuvar.
vīṭṭaiviṭṭu veḷiyēṟi uḻavum kaittoḻilum aṅkāṭi viṟpaṉaiyum kūli vēlaiyum teruppaṇṭamāṟṟum ceyyum piṟa vakuppup peṇṭirellām, kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ tāliyaṟuppiṉum maṟumaṇam ceytu koḷvar. atu aṟuttuk kaṭṭutal eṉappaṭum. iṉi, cila vakuppuppeṇṭir, kaṇavaṉ uyirōṭirukkum pōtē tīrvai eṉṉum kaṭṭaṇattaik koṭuttut tīrttuviṭṭu vēṟoruvaṉai maṇantu koḷvatum uṇṭu. atu, tīrttuk kaṭṭutal eṉappaṭum. atu ettaṉai muṟaiyum nikaḻum.
aracar pōrkkaḷattil tōṟṟut taṟkolai ceytu koṇ ṭālum, pakaivarāṟ kollappaṭṭālum, avar tēviyarum makaḷirum pakaiyaracarkku aṭimaiyākātavāṟu tīkkuḷittu iṟappatumuṇṭu.
kaṇavaṉ uyirōṭirukkum varai avaṉukku uṇmaiyāṉa maṉaiviyā yiruntu, avaṉ iṟantapiṉ vēṟoruvaṉai maṇappatum, kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ maṟumaṇam ceyyā tiruppatum, kaṟpiṉ pāṟpaṭumēṉum, avaṟṟait tamiḻakam kaṟpeṉak koḷḷavillai. tamiḻakak kaṟpu ulakattilēyē talai ciṟantatākum.
kuṭumpat talaivaṉ iṟantapiṉ, īmak kaṭaṉum iṟutic caṭaṅkum avaṉ putalvarāl, putalvaṉ illāviṭṭāl avaṉ uṭamaikku urimai pūṇum uṟaviṉaṉāl, naṭattappeṟum. īmam eṉpatu cuṭalai.
tuṟavaṟam
paṭṭiṉattup piḷḷaiyār pōl ulaka vāḻkkaiyiṉ nilaiyāmaiyai uṇarntō, tāyumāṉavar pōl kuruviṉāl aṟivuṇarttap peṟṟō, civappirakācar pōl iyalpākavē illaṟattil veṟuppuk koṇṭō, irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ pōl iḷamaiyilēyē kaṭanta aṟivaṭaintō, iḷaṅkō vaṭikaḷ pōl uṭaṉ piṟantārkkuk kēṭuvarātu taṭuttaṟ poruṭṭō, kōvala kaṇṇakiyar tantaiyarum mātavi maṇi mēkalaiyarum pōl kaṇṇaṉṉa uṟaviṉar neṭum pirivait tāṅka muṭiyāmalō, tuṟavu pūṇuvatu toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkamākum.
kuṭumpac caṇṭaiyāṟ caṭaivu koṇṭum, kuṭumpap poṟuppai nīkkik koḷḷavum, tuṟavu pūṇuvatumuṇṭu. ivaṟṟuḷ muṉṉatu ciṟappuṭaiyataṉṟu; piṉṉatu peruṅkuṟṟa mākum.
āṇṭi, paṇṭāram, aṭikaḷ, muṉivaṉ, cittaṉ eṉat tuṟaviyar palavakaiyar. āṇṭi irappōṉ; paṇṭāram aṟivu nūlkaḷai nirampak kaṟṟa paṇṭitaṉ; aṭikaḷ uḷḷat tūymaiyum āvik kuriya (Spiritual) ceytikaḷiṟ paṭṭaṟivum vāyntavar, paṭṭiṉat tārum tāyumāṉavarum irāmaliṅkarum pōl. immūvarum nāṭṭiliruppavar. muṉivarum cittarum malai yiliruppavar.
muṉivar allatu muṉaivar ulakappaṟṟai muṟṟum veṟuttavar. muṉital, veṟuttal. muṉaital, veṟuttal.
illaṟattāruḷ oruvaṉukku aracaṉ, āciriyaṉ, tantai, tāy, tamaiyaṉ eṉṟu aivakaip periyōriruppatāl, avar aiṅkuravar eṉṟum; avaruṭ ciṟanta peṟṟōriruvarum irumutu kuravar eṉṟum; aḻaikkappeṟuvar. kuravaṉ periyōṉ.
tamiḻar illaṟattil veṟṟi kaṇṭatu pōṉṟē tuṟavaṟattilum, aruḷuṭaimai, pulāṉ maṟuttal, kaḷḷāmai, vāymai, vekuḷāmai, iṉṉāceyyāmai, kollāmai, avāvaṟuttal eṉṉum aṟuvakai yaṟaṅkaḷaiyum; nōṉpu (tavam), meyyuṇartal (ōkam) eṉṉum iruvakai vaḻikaḷaiyum, kaṭaippiṭittu veṟṟi kaṇṭaṉar.
tamiḻat tuṟaviyaruḷ talaiciṟantaovar cittar. avar vāṉvaḻi iyaṅkutal, kūṭuviṭṭuk kūṭu pāytal, tōṉṟi maṟaital, maṇṇuḷ iruttal, uṇṇātu vāḻtal mutaliya pala āṟṟal kaḷai aṭaintavar. tirumūlar oru cittar. paṭṭiṉat tārum irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷum, iṟutiyil cittanilai aṭaintatāka avar varalāṟu kūṟum.
nōṉpu (tavam) eṉpatu illaṟattārkkum tuṟavaṟattārkkum potuvām.

9. camayavoḻukkam
camayam eṉpatu matam. atu oruvakaic camaivaik kuṟittalāl camayam eṉappaṭṭatu. camaital nukarccikkup patamātal. arici cōṟākac camaivatu uṇpataṟkup patamātal. peṇpiḷḷai maṅkaiyākac camaivatu maṇa nukarccikkup patamātal. ātaṉ (āṉmā) iṟaiyaṭimaiyākac camaivatu, vīṭupēṟṟiṟku allatu iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷai yaṭaivataṟkup patamātal.
camaiyam eṉpatu vēḷaiyaik kuṟikkumpōtum, oru viṉaikkup patamāṉa allatu takka kālanilai eṉṉum poruḷatē. nalla camaiyam, takka camaiyam, ēṟṟa camaiyam eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷai nōkkuka. palavakaic camaivukaḷuḷḷum ātaṉ iṟaiyaṭimai yākac camaivatu talaiciṟantatātalāl, camaivu eṉṉum viṉai, ataṉaiyē ciṟappākak kuṟikkum. āyiṉum, poruḷ mayakkamillāvāṟu, cōṟu camaivatu camaiyal eṉṟum, peṇ camaivatu camaital eṉṟum, nēram camaivatu camaiyam eṉṟum, ātaṉ camaivatu camayam eṉṟum, vēṟupaṭuttic collap peṟum.
camaiyam eṉṉum col amaiyam eṉṉum colliṉ mutaṉmikai (Prothesis). amaital poruntutal allatu takutiyātal. nallatoṉṟu vāyppiṉ, amaintu viṭṭatu eṉpar. amaiyum nēram amaiyam.
uyir mutaṟcoṟkaḷ coṉmutal meykaḷuḷ oṉṟum palavum peṟṟuc coṟkaḷaip piṟappittal, collākka neṟi muṟaimai yām.
e-ṭu : uruḷ - curuḷ, ēṇ - cēṇ.
amai eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyum am eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu tirintatākum. itaṉāl, camayam eṉṉum col tūya teṉ collātal teḷika. atu vaṭa colleṉṟu vaṭamoḻic coṟ kaḷañciyaṅkaḷilum ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutaliyilum kuṟittiruppatu koṇṭu mayaṅkaṟka.
āriyar varumuṉpē, tamiḻar camayat tuṟaiyilum talai ciṟantiruntaṉar. āyiṉum, ellārum annilaiyaṭaiyavillai. enta nākarika nāṭṭilum, talaiyāyār iṭaiyāyār kaṭaiyāyār eṉṉum muttiṟattār irukkavē ceyvar.
ākavē, tamiḻar matamum, ciṟu teyva vaṇakkam, perun teyva vaḻipāṭu, kaṭavuḷ neṟi eṉa munnilaippaṭṭiruntatu.
ciṟu teyvaṅkaḷ
(1)	teṉpulattār (iṟanta muṉṉōr).
(2)	naṭukal teyvaṅkaḷ.
(3)	pēykaḷ (pēy, pūtam, muṉi, caṭaimuṉi, aṇaṅku (mōkiṉi), cūraramakaḷir mutaliyaṉa).
(4)	tīya vuyirikaḷ (pāmpu, cuṟā, mutalai mutaliyaṉa).
(5)	iṭat teyvaṅkaḷ (āṟṟut teyvam, malait teyvam, kāṭṭut teyvam. nakart teyvam, nāṭṭut teyvam).
(6)	iyaṟkaip pūtaṅkaḷ (kāṟṟum tīyum).
(7)	vāṉac cuṭarkaḷ (katiravaṉum tiṅkaḷum).
(8)	celvat teyvam (tirumakaḷ).
(9)	kalvit teyvam (nāmakaḷ allatu coṉmakaḷ).
(10)	pālvarai teyvam (ūḻ vakuppatu).
perun teyvaṅkaḷ
ivai aintiṇait teyvaṅkaḷākum.
kuṟiñci - cēyōṉ
mullai - māyōṉ.
pālai - kāḷi.
marutam - vēntaṉ.
neytal - vāraṇaṉ.
cēyōṉ civantavaṉ. cēntaṉ eṉṉum peyarum ap poruḷatē. murukaṉ, vēlaṉ, kumaraṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum ivaṉuk kuṇṭu. civaṉ eṉpatu cēyōṉ eṉpataṉ ulaka vaḻakku vaṭivam. ippeyar vaṭivu vēṟupāṭṭaik koṇṭu, piṟ kālattil āriyar orē teyvattai iraṇṭākkit tantaiyum makaṉumākak kūṟi viṭṭaṉar. kumaraṉ eṉpataṟkum cēy eṉṉum kuṟukkat tiṟkum makaṉ eṉṟu tavaṟākap poruḷ koṇṭatē itaṟku aṭippaṭai.
civaṉ eṉṟu āriyat teyvam oṉṟumillai. civa eṉṉum col, nalla allatu maṅkala eṉṉum poruḷil, urut tiraṉukkum intiraṉukkum akkiṉikkum potuvāṉa aṭaimoḻiyākavē  āriya vētattil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu.
puṟakkaṇ kāṇa muṭiyātavaṟṟaiyum neṭuntolaiviluḷḷa vaṟṟaiyum kaṇṭaṟiyum ōr aṟivukkaṇ pōṉṟa uṟuppu, kumari nāṭṭu makkaḷ neṟṟiyiliruntateṉṟum, ataṉālēyē avartam iṟaivaṉukkum (civaṉukkum) oru neṟṟik kaṇṇaip paṭaittuk kūṟiṉareṉṟum, ārāycciyāḷar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. veḷḷimalai (kailai) yirukkaiyum kāḷaiyūrtiyum civaṉaik kuṟiñci nilat teyvamākak kāṭṭuvatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉiṉ talaimaiyamaiccarum pirāmaṇarum ciṟanta civaṉaṭiyārum vaṭamoḻi teṉmoḻi vallunarumākiya māṇikkavācakar,
pāṇṭi nāṭē paḻampati yākavum  tiruvācakam, 2: 118
teṉṉā ṭuṭaiya civaṉē pōṟṟi  (4:164)
eṉṟu pāṇṭi nāṭṭaiyē civaneṟip piṟappiṭamākak kuṟittal kāṇka. iṉi, civaperumāṉ aṟupattu nāṉku tiruviḷaiyāṭal kaḷaiyum maturaiyil aruḷic ceytataiyum, muttoḻil allatu aintoḻil tirunaṭattait tillaiyil āṟṟuvataiyum, eṇmaṟac ceyalkaḷ ceyta iṭaṅkaḷ (aṭṭavīraṭṭam) tamiḻ nāṭṭiliruppataiyum , ūṉṟi nōkkuka.
māyōṉ kariyavaṉ. mā karuppu. māya kiruṣṇaṉ eṉṉum peyarkkum itaṟkum yātoru toṭarpumillai. kaṇṇa pirāṉ piṟakkumuṉpē, kiruṣṇa eṉṉum col karuppu eṉṉum poruḷil irukku vētattil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. kiruṣṇa pakṣa (karumpakkam), kiruṣṇa sarppa (karumpāmpu) eṉṉum peyarkaḷai nōkkuka. kiruṣṇa eṉṉum coṟku vēta moḻiyil vērillai; tamiḻilēyē uḷḷatu.
kaḷ-karu-karuḷ= 1. karumai karuḷ taru kaṇṭattu... kailaiyār   (tēvā.337, 4) 2. iruḷ (piṅ.) 3. kuṟṟam. karuḷ tīrvaliyāl  (cētupu. muttīrt. 5).
karuḷ = kruṣ. ḷakara meyyīṟu vētamoḻiyil ṣakara meyyīṟākat tiriyum.
e-ṭu: cuḷ-cuṣ (to dry) uḷ - uṣ, (to burn). 
māyōṉ, māl, viṇṭu eṉṉum peyarkaḷ oru poruṭ coṟkaḷ.
māl = karumai, mukil, tirumāl.
viṇṭu = mukil, vāṉam, tirumāl,
viṇ = vāṉam, mukil, mēlulakam.
viṇ - viṇṭu.
āriya vētattil viṣṇu eṉṉum peyar katiravaṉaik kuṟittatu. kālai eḻucciyaiyum naṇpakaṟ celavaiyum ēṟpāṭṭu (cāyuṅkāla) vīḻcciyaiyumē mūvaṭiyāl (mūṉṟu eṭṭāl) ulaka muḻuvataiyum viṣṇu (katiravaṉ) aḷappatāka mutaliṟ koṇṭaṉar vēta āriyar.
kuṟiñci nilattār tam teyvattai malaiyil tōṉṟum neruppiṉ kūṟākavum, mullai nilattār tam teyvattai vāṉattiṉiṉṟu poḻiyum mukiliṉ kūṟākavum, koṇṭaṉar. itaṉālēyē, civaṉukkut tīvaṇṇaṉ, antivaṇṇaṉ, māṇikkak kūttaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum; tirumālukkuk 
kārvaṇṇaṉ, maṇivaṇṇaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷum; tōṉṟiṉa.
kāḷi karuppi. kaḷ- kaḷvaṉ = kariyavaṉ. kaḷ-kāḷ- kāḻ = karumai. kāḷ- kāḷam = karumai. kāḷ- kāḷi. kāḷi kūḷikaṭkut talaivi. kūḷi pēy. pēy karuppeṉṟum iruḷ eṉṟum peyar peṟum. karuppi, karuppāy eṉṉum kāḷiyiṉ peyarkaḷai, iṉṟum peṇṭirkkiṭuvatu peruvaḻakku. tāy eṉṉum poruḷil ammai (ammaṉ) aiyai eṉṟum, iḷaiñai eṉṉum poruḷil kaṉṉi, kumari eṉṟum, veṟṟi tarupavaḷ eṉṉum poruḷil koṟṟavai (koṟṟavvai) eṉṟum, kāḷikkup peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkum. aṅkāḷammai eṉpatu aḻakiya kāḷi yammai eṉṉum poruḷatu.
āriyar varumuṉ vaṭa intiyā muḻutum tiraviṭar paraviyirunta tiṉāl, vaṅka nāṭṭil kāḷik kōṭṭam ēṟpaṭṭatu.
vēntaṉ = aracaṉ. mutaṟkālattil aracaṉum teyvamāka vaṇaṅkappaṭṭāṉ. ataṉāl, iṟaivaṉ eṉṉum peyar aracaṉukkum kaṭavuṭkum, kōyil eṉṉum peyar araṇmaṉaikkum teyvak kōṭṭattiṟkum, potuvāyiṉa. vēntaṉ iṟantapiṉ vāṉavarkkum aracaṉāki vāṉulakat tiṉiṉṟu maḻaiyaip poḻivikkiṉṟāṉ eṉṟu paṇṭai maruta nilamakkaḷ karutiyatāl, maḻait teyvattai vēntaṉ eṉṉum peyarāl vaṇaṅki vantaṉar.
āriyar varumuṉpē, moḻi vēṟupāṭṭiṉāl vēntaṉukku vaṭanāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vanta peyar intiraṉ eṉpatu. intiraṉ aracaṉ. ākavē iraṇṭum oru poruṭ coṟkaḷē. vēta āriyar intira vaṇakkattai, vaṭanāṭṭut tirāviṭaraip piṉ paṟṟiyē mēṟkoṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum. mēlaiyāriya nāṭukaḷil intira vaṇakkamē yiruntatillaiyeṉṟu, mākacu mullar (Max Muller) kūṟiyiruppatu, iṅkuk kavaṉikkattakkatu.
kaṭaik kaḻakak kālam varai kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattil naṭai peṟṟu vanta intira viḻāvum, tamiḻarvēntaṉ viḻāvē.
vāraṇaṉ = kaṭal teyvam. vāraṇam, kaṭal. kaṭalukku oru teyva miruppatākak karuti, atai neytal nilamakkaḷ vaṇaṅki vantaṉar.
mutaṟkālat tiraviṭar vaṭamēṟkāyc ceṉṟu kirēkka nāṭṭiṟ kuṭiyēṟiyapiṉ, vāraṇam eṉṉum col (Ouranos) eṉat tirintatu. appeyart teyvam mutaliṟ kirēkkarkkuk kaṭal teyvamākavēyiruntu, piṉpu, vāṉat teyvamāyiṟṟu. kirēkkat  tiṟku mika neruṅkiya moḻiyaip pēcivanta kīḻaiyāriyaruḷ oru pirivārāṉa vēta āriyar, kaṭalaiyaṟiyāmal neṭukavum nilavaḻiyākavē vantatiṉāl, maḻait teyvattaiyē varuṇā eṉa aḻaittaṉar. avar intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntu vaṭanāṭṭut tiraviṭarōṭum teṉṉāṭṭut tamiḻarōṭum toṭarpu koṇṭa piṉṉarē, varuṇaṉaik kaṭal teyvamākak karutat toṭaṅkiṉar. āyiṉum, iṉṉum maḻaikkāka varuṇaṉai vēṇṭuvatē pirāmaṇar vaḻakkamāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu.
tolkāppiyattil varuṇaṉ mēya perumaṇalulakamum (akat. 5) eṉṟu, vāraṇaṉ eṉṉum peyarai vaṭamoḻi vaṭiviṟ kuṟittiruppatu tavaṟākum. atu vāraṇaṉ mēya ērmaṇa lulakamum eṉṟiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
aintiṇait teyva vaḻipāṭukaḷuḷ, cēyōṉ vaḻipāṭum māyōṉ vaḻipāṭum piṟkālattil iruperuñ camayaṅkaḷāka vaḷarcciyaṭaintuḷḷaṉa.
kaṭavuḷ neṟi
ūrpēr kuṇaṅkuṟiyaṟṟu, maṉamoḻi meykaḷaik kaṭantu eṅkum niṟaintiruttal, ellām aṟintiruttal, ellām vallatātal, eṉṟumuṇmai, aruḷ vaṭivuṭaimai, iṉpa nilainiṟṟal, oppuyarviṉmai, mācumaṟuviṉmai ākiya eṇ kuṇaṅkaḷaiyuṭaiyatāy ellāvulakaṅkaḷaiyum paṭaittuk kāttaḻittuvarum oru param poruḷuṇṭeṉṟu nampi, ataṉai vaḻipaṭuvatē kaṭavuḷ neṟiyām. itu cittamatam eṉavum paṭum.
ellāvaṟṟaiyum kaṭantiruppataṉālēyē iṟaivaṉukkuk kaṭavuḷ eṉap peyariṭṭaṉar. āriyar vanta piṉ, iccol mutaliṟ perunteyvaṅkaṭkum, piṉpu ciṟuteyvaṅkaṭkum, iṟutiyil makkaḷāṉa muṉivarkkum vaḻaṅki iḻivaṭain tuḷḷatu.
tiruvaḷḷuvar tam nūṉmukattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatu uruvamaṟṟa kaṭavuḷ vaḻuttē. tamiḻariṉ uruvavaṇakka mallāk kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭṭai uḷiyiṭṭa kallaiyum  eṭṭut ticaiyum eṉṉum paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ pāṭalaiyum aṅkiṅkeṉātapaṭi. paṇṇēṉ uṉakkāṉa pūcai eṉṉum tāyumāṉavar pāṭalaiyum nōkkiyuṇarka.
kaṭavuḷaiyum maṟumaiyaiyum nampāta oru ciṟu kūṭṭattārum akkālat tiruntaṉar. āyiṉ, aṟiñar avaraik kaṇṭittaṉar.
ulakattār uṇṭeṉpa tilleṉpāṉ vaiyat
talakaiyā vaikkap paṭum.  (kuṟaḷ. 850)
nallataṉ nalaṉum tīyataṉ tīmaiyum
illai yeṉpōrk kiṉaṉā kiliyar  (puṟam. 29: 11-12)
maṟumaiyoṉ ṟuṇṭō maṉappaṭṭa ellām
peṟumāṟu ceymmiṉeṉ pārē- naṟuneyyuḷ
kaṭṭi yaṭaiyaik kaḷaivittuk kaṇcorīi
iṭṭikai tīṟṟu pavar.  (paḻamoḻi, 108).


10. toḻilkaḷ
1.  uḻavu
uṇṭi mutaṟṟē uṇaviṉ piṇṭam
uṇṭi koṭuttōr uyirkoṭut tōrē  (puṟam. 18)
uḻuvār ulakattiṟ kāṇiyaḵ tāṟṟā
teḻuvārai yellām poṟuttu  (kuṟaḷ. 1032)
irappōr cuṟṟamum purappōr koṟṟamum
uḻaviṭai viḷaippōr  (cilap. 10: 151-2)
uḻavar. ātalāl, oru nāṭṭu vāḻviṟkum muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum aṭippaṭai uḻavu eṉpataip paṇṭait tamiḻmakkaḷ uṇarntiruntaṉar.
uḻavut toḻiṟku iṉṟiyamaiyātavai nilam, nīr, vitai, erutu eṉṉum nāṉkām.
paṇṭait tamiḻar, aintiṇaikaḷuḷ marutattaiyum neytalai yum meṉpula meṉṟum, kuṟiñciyaiyum, mullaiyaiyum vaṉpula meṉṟum, vakuttiruntaṉar. marutanilattai nāṭu eṉṟum, maṟṟa nilaṅkaḷaik kāṭu eṉṟum aḻaittaṉar. kuṟiñci nilattil uḻattakka iṭattai ērkkāṭu eṉṟum, uḻat takātataik kottuk kāṭu eṉṟum pakuttaṉar. kuṟiñciyilum mullaiyilu muḷḷa viḷainilaṅkaḷ kollai allatu puṉam eṉṟum, marutattiluḷḷa viḷainilaṅkaḷ cey eṉṟum, putuk kollai itai eṉṟum, paḻaṅ kollai mutai eṉṟum, ciṟitu ceyyap peṟṟa cey puṉcey eṉṟum, naṉṟāyc ceyyappeṟṟa cey naṉcey eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
ceytal eṉpatu tiruttutal allatu paṇpaṭuttutal. puṉmai ciṟumai.
kollai eṉpatu vāṉavārik kāṭeṉṟum, puṉcey kiṇaṟṟup pāycca leṉṟum, naṉcey āṟṟu allatu ērip pāyccaleṉṟum, aṟital vēṇṭum. eḷ koḷ mutaliyaṉa kollaip payir;  kēḻvaraku cōḷam mutaliyaṉa puṉceyp payir; nel karumpu mutaliyaṉa naṉceyp payir.
naṉceykaḷuḷ, paḻamaiyāṉatu paḻaṉam eṉṟum, pōraṭikkum kaḷamuḷḷatu kaḻaṉi eṉṟum collap peṟum. paṇṇai eṉpatu paṇṇappaṭṭatu (paṇpaṭuttappaṭṭatu) eṉṉum poruḷatēṉum, vaḻakkil kaḷamar allatu ceṟumar eṉṉum paṇṇaiyāṭkaḷ kuṭiyiruntu vēlai ceyyum periya vayaṟ parappaiyē (Farm) kuṟikkum. cēṟuḷḷamaiyāl ceṟu eṉṟum, vaippup pōṉṟamaiyāl vayal eṉṟum, naṉceykkup peyarkaḷuṇṭu.
puṉamāyiṉum puṉceyāyiṉum, paṇṭait tamiḻar mēṭṭu nilattiṟ payir ceyya virumpavillai. mēṭu cuval eṉṟum paḷḷam aval eṉṟum peyar peṟum. mēṭṭup puṉceyai uḻutavaṉum keṭṭāṉ, mēṉi miṉukkiyai maṇantavaṉum keṭṭāṉ eṉpatu paḻamoḻi.
naṉceyp pācaṉattiṟku, āṟṟunīr illāviṭaṅkaṭkuk kaṇṇāṟukaḷum kālvāykaḷum veṭṭip pāycciṉar. atu iyalāviṭattu ērikaḷai veṭṭiṉar. ērt toḻiṟku utavuvatu ēri. kuḷippatu kuḷam. iṉṟu ēriyait tavaṟākak kuḷameṉpar oru cārār. iyaṟkaiyāka uṇṭāṉa ēri allatu kuḷam poykai eṉappaṭum. mullai nilattiṟ puṉceyp pācaṉattiṟkuk kiṇaṟukaḷai veṭṭiṉar.
erutukaḷaik koṇṭu kiṇaṟṟu nīrai iṟaikkum ēṟṟam kammālai eṉappaṭṭatu. am, nīr. am - kam = nīr. kam + ālai = kammālai. ālai cuṟṟi varuvatu. karumpālai eṉpatai nōkkuka. ālutal āṭutal. mutaṟ kālattil erutukaḷ oru marat tūṇaic cuṟṟi vantaṉa. kammālai eṉpatu iṉṟu kamalai eṉṟum kavalai eṉṟum tirintu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. iṉṟum kamalaiyāṭutal eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. iṉṟu erutukaḷ nērākac celvatāl kavalai yōṭṭutal eṉṟum kūṟuvar. kamalai yēṟṟattaik kapilai yēṟṟam eṉṟu ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutali kuṟittiruppatu tavaṟākum, kapilai (va) yeṉpatu kurāl eṉṉum āvakai. āvaik kaṭṭi nīriṟaippatu vaḻakkattiṟku māṟākum.
vitaikkeṉṟu mutaṟ kāyppaiyum ciṟanta maṇikaḷaiyum otukki vaittaṉar. akkālattu uḻavar poruḷīṭṭalaik kuṟik koḷḷātu, uṇavu viḷaittalaiyē kuṟikkoṇṭu patiṉeṇ kūlaṅkaḷaiyum avaṟṟiṉ vakaikaḷaiyum, āṇṭu tōṟum viḷaittu vantaṉar. nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa nelvakaikaḷ viḷaikkap peṟṟaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ perumpālaṉavaṟṟai iṉṟu kaṇṇālum kāṇa muṭiyavillai; kātālum kēṭka muṭiyavillai. poṉ tiṉai, centiṉai, karuntiṉai eṉṉum mūvakaiyuḷ, iṉṟu poṉṟiṉaiyē kāṇa muṭikiṉṟatu. avarai vakaikaḷuṭ pala āṇṭutōṟum ovvoṉṟāy maṟaintu varukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu āṭciyai naṭattupavarukkup pataviyaik kākkavēṇṭu meṉ patēyaṉṟi, vitai vakaikaḷaip pēṇa vēṇṭumeṉṉum kalai nōkkillai.
tamiḻakattil uḻavut toḻiṟku iṉṟiyamaiyāt tuṇaiyāka, toṉṟu toṭṭup payaṉpaṭṭuvarum vilaṅku erutākum. ērt toḻiṟku utavuvataṉāl kāḷai eruteṉap paṭṭatu. ēr-ērtu- erutu. kāṭṭu māṭṭaip piṭittup paḻakki vīṭṭu vilaṅkākki ērt toḻiṟkup payaṉpaṭuttiṉar.
erutiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmai nōkkiyē ērt toḻilaip pakaṭu eṉṟaṉar.
pakaṭupuṟan tarunar pāram ōmpi  (puṟam 35)
pakaṭu naṭanta kūḻ  (nālaṭi. 4)
uḻavut toḻilum pāṇṭiyam eṉappaṭṭatu.
pāṇṭiyañ ceyvāṉ poruḷiṉum  (kalit. 136).
pāṇṭi erutu.
erutukaḷai niṟam paṟṟiyum tiṟam paṟṟiyum pala vakaiyāka vakuttu, avaṟṟuḷ nālvakaiyaic ciṟappāka iṟakka varicaiyil eṭuttuk kūṟiṉar. atu muḻuppullai, mukkāl mayilai, araic civappu, kāl karuppu; eṉap paḻamoḻiyāy vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu.
paṇṭaiyuḻavar ellāp payir paccaikaḷaiyum paruvamaṟintē viḷaittu vantaṉar. (cittiraimāta vuḻavu pattarai māṟṟut taṅkam. paṭṭan tappiṉāl naṭṭam āṭip paṭṭam tēṭi vitai,  eṉpaṉa piṟkālattup paḻamoḻikaḷākum.) mārikkāla vēḷāṇmaiyaik kālam eṉṟum, vēṉiṟkāla vēḷāṇmai  yaik kōṭai eṉṟum kūṟiṉar.
iṉṟu naṭaipeṟṟuvarum vēḷāṇmai viṉaikaḷ pattāyiram āṇṭukaṭku muṉpu toṭṭē paṇṭāṭu paḻanaṭaiyāy varuvaṉa vākum.
kāṭu veṭṭik kaḷapputal, kal poṟukkutal, eruviṭutal, āḻa vuḻutal, kaṭṭiyaṭittal, parampaṭittal (tāḷiyaṭittal, palliyāṭu tal, ūṭṭittal, paṭaliḻuttal). puḻutiyuṇakkal, vitaittal, kaḷaiyeṭuttal, kāval kāttal, aṟuvaṭai ceytal, kaḷañ cērttal, cāṇaiyaṭaital (cūṭupōṭutal pōramaittal), cāṇai pirittal, kāyap pōṭutal, piṇaiyalaṭittal (kaṭā viṭutal, ataritirittal), vaikkōl allatu cakkai allatu kappi nīkkal, poli tūṟṟal, poliyaḷattal, vitaikkeṭuttal, kaḷañciyañ cērttal eṉpaṉa vāṉavārip putuk kollai vēḷāṇmai viṉaikaḷām, kāṭu veṭṭik kaḷapputal putuk kollaikkē nikaḻum.
paḻaṅkollaiyāyiṉ, urampōṭutaṟku eruviṭutalōṭu kiṭaiyamarttalum kuppaiyaṭittalum nikaḻum.
puṉcey vēḷāṇmaiyāyiṉ, puḻutiyuṇakkaṟkup piṉṉum kaḷaiyeṭuttaṟku muṉṉum, nāṟṟup pāval, pātti piṭittal, nīr pāycci naṭutal oṉṟaraivāṭam nīr pāyccal ākiya viṉaikaḷ nikaḻum.
naṉcey vēḷāṇmaiyāyiṉ, nāṟṟup pāval, nīr pāyccal, toḷi(cēṟu)k kalakkal, kuḻai mitittal, naṭutal eṉṉum viṉaikaḷum, avaṟṟiṟkuppiṉ kaḷaiyeṭuttal mutaliya vāṉavāri vēḷāṇmai viṉaikaḷum, nikaḻum,
akam - akaram = marutanilattār. o.nō: L.agros, field E.acre.
ēr - E. ear, to plough ME. eren, AS. erian, Ice. erja, Goth, arjan, L,arare, Gk. arow, I plough, Ir. araim. I plough. E. arable = ploughable; earth = that which is ploughed. Root - ar
ēr eṉṉum col vaṭamoḻiyiliṉmai kavaṉikkattakkatu.
kāṟu (koḻu)- AS. scear, ME. schar, Ger. schar, schaar, E. share.
toḷ (tōṇṭu) - E. till. A.S. tilian, ME. tilien, Du. telen, 
toḷ - toy. toyyāvulakam = toḻil allatu viṉai ceyyā viṇṇulakam.
toḷ- toḻu- toḻil. ulaka mutal toḻil uḻavē.
kal (tōṇṭu)- L. colere, to till. kal eṉṉum viṉaiyiṉiṉṟu kalvi eṉṉum col tōṉṟiyatu pōṉṟē, colo eṉṉum viṉaiyi ṉiṉṟu cultivation, culture eṉṉum coṟkaḷum, ar (uḻu) eṉṉum viṉaiyiṉiṉṟu ars, artis (art) eṉṉum coṟkaḷum, tōṉṟi yuḷḷaṉa. ulaka mutaṟ kalviyum uḻavē.
pullam (erutu) ME. bole, ON. boli MLG., MDu. bulle. E. bull. 
pullamēṟi taṉpūmpukaliyai  (tēvā. 76,11)
2. necavu
ney - neyavu - necavu.
maṭattakai māmayil paṉikkumeṉ ṟaruḷip
 paṭā am ītta keṭāa nallicaik
 kaṭāa yāṉaik kalimāṉ pēka.
eṉṟu paraṇar pāṭiyiruppatālum (puṟam. 145).
putaiyiruṭ paṭāam pōka nīkki
eṉṉum cilappatikāra aṭikku (5:4), allaṟkālaip pacantu vārap paṉittup pōrtta iruḷākiya paṭāttai eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār uraivarain tiruppataṉālum,
paṭṭiṉum mayiriṉum parutti nūliṉum
kaṭṭum nuṇviṉaik kāruka rirukkaiyum
eṉpatāl (cilap. 5:16-17), mayirnecavum kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattiruntamai aṟiyappaṭuvatālum, paṭām eṉpatu cālvai (shawl) eṉṉum pāracīkac collāṟ kuṟikkap peṟum meṉ mayirp pōrvaiyāyirukkalām.
kaṇṭaṅ kuttiya maṇṭapa eḻiṉiyum  (uñcaik. 37 : 103)
kaḻippaṭa māṭam kāloṭu tuḷaṅka  (uñcaik. 44:42)
eṉṉum peruṅkataiyaṭikaḷum pāṇṭiyaṉ kūṭakārattut tuñciya māṟaṉ vaḻutiyeṉṉum peyarum koṇṭu, kūṭāram amaittaṟkuriya muraṭṭut tuṇiyum neyyappaṭṭamaiyai uyttuṇaralām.
tiraiyāl amaikkappaṭṭa maṇṭapam maṇṭapa eḻiṉi.
paṭam-tuṇi, māṭama-māṭam pōl uyarnta kūṭam. paṭamāṭattaip paṭamāḷikai, paṭamaṇṭapam eṉpatumuṇṭu. kūṭāram, kūṭam pōl uḷḷa paṭamāṭam. kūṭa āram- kūṭavāram- kūṭakāram- kūṭāram. āram ōr īṟu.
e-ṭu:  koṭṭāram, vaṭṭāram, kaṭikaiyāram - kaṭikāram.
3. kammiyam
karuttal ceytal. itu vaḻakkaṟṟup pōṉa oru tamiḻ viṉaiccol.
tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ karuniṟamum pukar (brown) niṟamum poṉṉiṟamum uḷḷavar toṉṟutoṭṭu iruntu varukiṉṟaṉar. poṉ ṉiṟattaic civappeṉṟum veḷḷaiyeṉṟum colvatu vaḻakkam. poṉmai karumai ākiya iruniṟam paṟṟiyē, veṇkaḷamar karuṅkaḷamar, veḷḷāḷar, kārāḷar, veḷ ḷokkalar kārokkalar eṉṉum colliṇaikaḷ eḻuntaṉa. kāyp pēṟumāṟu viṉai ceyyiṉ karuppar akaṅkai karuppatum civappar akaṅkai civappatum iyalpu. itupaṟṟi viṉai ceytalaik kuṟikka, karuttal ceytal eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. kaikarukkumāṟu ceyvatu karuttal; civakkumāṟu ceyvatu ceytal.
karu-karumam-ceykai. karu-karuvi= ceykaikku vēṇṭum tuṇaip poruḷ. karu-karaṇam= ceykai, karuvi. aṇam or īṟu.
iṉi, karumaic coṟkup perumaip poruḷumiruttalāl, karuttal = mikuttal eṉṟumām. putitāyc ceyyap peṟum oru poruḷ ēṟkeṉavēyuḷḷa ataṉ vakaiyai mikuttal kāṇka. make eṉṉum āṅkilac collaiyum magnus (great) eṉṉum ilattīṉ colliṉiṉṟu tirippar āṅkilac collārāyc ciyāḷar. taṉviṉaiyum piṟaviṉaip poruḷ payappatu collākka marapiṟ kottatē.
o.nō : veḷuttal = veḷḷaiyātal (taṉviṉai), veḷḷaiyākkutal (piṟaviṉai). vaṇṇāṉ tuṇikaḷai veḷukkiṟāṉ eṉpatil, veḷuttal viṉai piṟaviṉaiyā yiruttal kāṇka.
karumam-kammam= ceykai. toḻil, kait toḻil, kol toḻil, kammiyat toḻil.
kammam = kammiyat toḻil.
kammam = kammiyat toḻil.
kammañcey mākkaḷ  (nālaṭi.393)
kammam- kamam.
ivviru vaṭivum mutalil toḻil eṉṟē poruḷpaṭṭaṉa. ulakil mutalil tōṉṟiya toḻil uḻavē. toḻil, kai eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyarum, cey, paṇṇai eṉṉum nilap peyarum itaṉai valiyuṟuttum.
toḷ- (toḷil) - toḻil. toḷḷutal tōṇṭutal.
kai= kaiyāṟ ceyyum ceykai allatu toḻil, mutal toḻilāṉa uḻavu.
iravār irappārkkoṉ ṟīvar karavātu
kaiceytūṇ mālai yavar.
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaḷai (1035) nōkkuka.
kai eṉpatu ceykaiyaik kuṟittalālēyē oru toḻiṟ peyarīṟāyiṟṟu. e-ṭu: ceykai, varukai.
uḻavu mutal toḻilāṉatiṉāl, kammam kamam eṉṉum iruvaṭivum mutalil uḻavaik kuṟittaṉa.
kammavāru (te.) - kammavār= teluṅka vuḻavar.
f«k¤j« - f«k¤jK(bj.)= paṇṇaip payirt toḻil.
kamam = payirt toḻil. kamakkāraṉ = uḻavaṉ.
kamam - kam = 1. toḻil īmum kammum urumeṉ kiḷaviyum  (tol, eḻut. 328) 2. kammiyat toḻil, (naṉ, 223, virutti.)
kam+āḷaṉ kammāḷaṉ. kammāḷar eṉpār kollaṉ, taccaṉ, kammiyaṉ (ciṟpi), taṭṭāṉ, kaṉṉāṉ eṉṉum aiṅkollar. kammiyaṉaik kaltaccaṉ eṉṟu kūṟuvatu vaḻakkam.
kam-kammiyam = 1. kaittoḻil. 2. kammāḷat toḻil.
kammāḷaṉ eṉṉum coṟ pōṉṟē, kammiyaṉ eṉṉum collum piṟa kollaraiyum kuṟikkum.
kammiyaṉ = 1. toḻilāḷi. kammiyarum ūrvar kaḷiṟu (cīvaka. 495) 2. kammāḷaṉ (ti.vā.). 3. poṟkollaṉ. ācil kammiyaṉ mācaṟap puṉainta (puṟam. 353)
kammiyanūl = kaṭṭaṭanūl (ciṟpa cāttiram) kammiyanūl tolvaram pellai kaṇṭu (tiruviḷai. tirunakaraṅ. 38)
karumakaṉ eṉpatu, karuṅkaik kollar  (cilap. 5: 29) eṉpatu pōl iruppuk kollaṉai maṭṭum kuṟikkum. karumakaṉ - karumāṉ.
o.nō:  perumakaṉ - perumāṉ, marumakaṉ - marumāṉ.
karu eṉṉum viṉaimutaṉilai vaṭamoḻiyil kru eṉat tiriyum. karaṇam eṉpataik kāraṇa eṉa nīṭṭiyum, kru eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyiṉiṉṟu kārya eṉṉum collai ākkiyum, karumam eṉpataik karmaṉ eṉat tirittum, uḷḷaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.
miṉṉum piṉṉum paṉṉum kaṉṉum
 annāṟ collum toḻiṟpeya riyala.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvil (eḻut. 345) kaṉṉārat toḻil kuṟikkap paṭṭuḷḷatu. tolkāppiyar pāratak kālattiṟkum kaṭaikkaḻakak kālattiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭavar;  pāṇiṉikku muntiyavar.
nellum cellum kollum collum
 allatu kiḷappiṉum vēṟṟumai yiyala.

o. nō : ai - aintu - añcu
mai - maiñcu - mañcu = mukil.

kaiccāttu - kaccāttu
āṅkilattilum, taṭṭumuṭṭukkaḷ ceyyum taccaṉai joiner eṉṟu kūṟutal kāṇka.
taccaṉ eṉpatu, vaṭamoḻiyil takṣa eṉṟum, kirēkkattil tekton eṉṟum tiriyum. uṇmai iṅṅaṉamiruppavum, ceṉṉaip palkalaikkaḻakat tamiḻ akaramutaliyait tokutta pirāmaṇat tamiḻp pulavar takṣa eṉṉum colliṉiṉṟu taccaṉ eṉpatu vantatākak kuṟittirukkiṉṟaṉar.
pāṇṭiyaṉ tēviyiṉ vaṭṭakkaccaik kaṭṭil, piṉvarumāṟu yāṉai maruppiṉāl (tantattiṉāl) ciṟanta vēlaippāṭṭuṭaṉ ceyyap peṟṟatāka, neṭunalvāṭai kūṟukiṉṟatu.
nāṟpatāṇṭu akavaiyuḷḷatum, muracupōṉṟa periya kālkaḷaiyum aḻakiya pukar niṟainta mattakattaiyum uṭaiyatum, pōrukkuc ciṟantateṉṟu peyar peṟṟatum, pōr ceytu iṟantatumāṉa, yāṉaiyiṉ tāṉē vīḻnta kompai irupuṟamum kaṉamum cemmaiyum oppac cetukki, kūriya ciṟṟuḷiyālē periya ilait toḻilai iṭaiyilē amaittu; niṟaicūli yiṉ pālkaṭṭiya mārpu pōlat tiraṇṭa kuṭattaiyum, uḷḷip pūṭu pōṉṟa kaṭaiccalaiyum, koṇṭa kālkaḷaip porutti; mūṭṭuvāy māṭcimaippaṭat takaṭukaḷai āṇikaḷāl taittu, aḻakākat toṭutta muttuk kuñcaṅkaḷaic cuṟṟivarat toṅka viṭṭu; puliyiṉ variyaik koṇṭa polivu peṟṟa kaccālē takaṭumaṟaiya naṭuviṭam muḻutum piṉṉi; kuṟṟamaṟṟup pēraḷavu koṇṭu perum peyarpeṟṟa vaṭṭakkaccaik kaṭṭil
tacanāṉ keytiya paṇaimaruḷ nōṉṟāḷ
ikalmīk kūṟum ēnteḻil varinutal
porutoḻi nākam oḻiyeyi ṟarukeṟintu
cīrum cemmaiyum oppa vallōṉ
kūruḷik kuyiṉṟa īrilai iṭaiyiṭupu
tūṅkiyal makaḷir vīṅkumulai kaṭuppap
puṭaitiraṇ ṭirunta kuṭatta iṭaitiraṇṭu
uḷḷi nōṉmutal porutti yaṭiyamaittup
pēraḷa veytiya perumpeyarp pāṇṭil
maṭaimāṇ nuṇṇiḻai poliyat toṭaimāṇṭu
muttuṭaic cālēkam nāṟṟik kuttuṟuttup
pulippoṟik koṇṭa pūṅkēḻt taṭṭattut
takaṭukaṇ putaiyak koḷīit tukaḷtīrntu  (neṭunal. 115-128).
nilattiṉ mēlōṭum vaṇṭiyum tērum pōṉṟē nīrmēlōṭum marakkalaṅkaḷum, mutaṉ mutal tamiḻnāṭṭiltāṉ ceyyap peṟṟaṉa. ōṭam ceypavaṉukku ōṭāvi (ōṭāḷvi) eṉṟum, nāvāy pōṉṟa peruṅkalam ceypavarukkuk kalañcey kammiyar (maṇi. 25 - 124); kalampuṇar kammiyar (maṇi. 7:70) eṉṟum, peyar. ivar kappal taccar.
kalattaiyuṭaiya neytal nila makkaḷ kalavar, kalattai ōṭṭum talaivaṉ nīkāṉ (nīkāmaṉ, mīkāmaṉ mīkāṉ (Captain)). kalattil viṉai ceypavar ōcunar (Sailors). kalattiṟ ceṉṟu vāṇikam ceypavaṉ kaṭalōṭi.
tiraikaṭa lōṭiyum tiraviyam tēṭu.
eṉṟu auvaiyār kūṟiyatu kāṇka.
koṭittēr vītiyum tēvar kōṭṭamum
eṉṟu maṇimēkalai (21:120) kūṟuvatāl, teyva uruvattai vaittirukkum perun tērum akkālat tiruntirukkalām.
ikkālattuḷḷa tērkaḷuḷ tiruvārūrt tēr mikap peritu. atuvum araiyē yaraikkāl tērēṉṟum, muṉpu erintu pōṉatu mukkāl tēreṉṟum, kūṟuvar. aṅṅaṉamāyiṉ, ataṟkum muṉpē muḻuttēr iruntiruttal vēṇṭum. muḻut tēr ēḻu taṭṭum mukkāltēr aintu taṭṭum araittēr mūṉṟutaṭṭum uḷḷaṉavākac collappaṭum. ivai muṟaiyē nūṟaṭiyum, eḻupattaintaṭiyum aṟupataṭiyum uyaramuḷḷaṉa eṉpar.
maṉṉārkuṭi matilaḻaku... maṉaiyaḻaku, tiruvārūrt tēraḻaku, tiruviṭaimarutūrt teruvaḻaku eṉṟoru paḻamoḻi vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
vāṉaḷāvum kōpuraṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭirunta paṇṭaik kālat til, māperun tērkaḷ ceyyappaṭṭiruntatil viyappillai.
teyvat tēriṟ pala aṇiyuṟuppukkaḷum tēvar muṉivar paṭimaikaḷum yāḷi kutirai yuruvaṅkaḷum ceytu vaikkap peṟutalāl, tērttaccar taccuk kalaiyil talaiciṟantavarāvar.
kaṭṭumaram mutal kappalvarai palvakaik kalaṅkaḷaiyum ceyyum taccar kalampuṇar kammiyar (kappal taccar) eṉappaṭṭār.
kāṭci kālkōḷ nīrppaṭai naṭukal
eṉṉum veṭcittiṇait tuṟaikaḷum (tol. puṟat. 5). ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ paṉimalaiyiṟ kalleṭuttuk kaṇṇakikkup paṭimañ camaippittatum, kaltaccu viṉaikkuc cāṉṟukaḷām.
maṇṭapaṅkaḷai yellām kallāṟ kaṭṭuvatē toṉṟu toṭṭa vaḻakkam. nūṟṟukkāl maṇṭapaṅkaḷum āyirakkāl maṇṭapaṅkaḷum viḻāppaṭi maṇṭapaṅkaḷum attakaiya. kaṟ kōyil kaṟṟaḷiyeṉappaṭṭatu. taḷikōyil, teyvappaṭimaikaḷ (cilaikaḷ) aivakaik kollarālum ceyyap paṭṭaṉa.

6. piṟa toḻilkaḷ
pāy necavum muṭaivum, taiyal, tōl pataṉiṭal, cerupput taittal, tōluṟai ceytal, peṭṭi cuḷaku (kūṭai muṟam) muṭaitalum piṉṉutalum, kaṭṭiṟ piṉṉutal, tuṇiyālum neṭṭiyālum palvēṟu kavarccip poruḷkaḷum viḷaiyāṭṭuk karuvikaḷum aṇikaḷum ceytal, vaṇṇam pūcutal, vaṇṇa ōviyam varaital, maṇpāvai ceytal, naṟu maṇap poruḷ kūṭṭutal, cuṇṇa miṭittal, cekkāṭṭutal, āṭaiveḷuttal, mālai kaṭṭutal, caṅkaṟuttal, pavaḻa maṟuttal, maṇi kōttal, mīṉ piṭittal, muttuk kuḷittal, uppu viḷaittal, kaḷḷiṟakkutal, kaḷ camaittal, āṭu mēyttal, māṭu mēyttal, tayir kaṭaital, vēṭṭaiyāṭutal, koṭikkāl vaittal. cāṇaikkal ceytal, palvakaic ciṟṟuṇṭi ceytal, kuyiluvak karuvikaḷ (palvēṟu icaik karuvikaḷ) ceytal mutaliyaṉa.



11. vāṇikam
nākarika makkaḷ vāḻkkaikku, uṇavu pōṉṟē uṭai, kalam, uṟaiyuḷ, ūrti mutaliya poruḷkaḷum, poṉ veḷḷi mutaliya celvamum, iṉṟiyamaiyātiruppatāl, poruḷāṭcit tuṟaiyil, oru nāṭṭiṉ nalvāḻviṟkum muṉṉēṟṟattiṟkum aṭippaṭaiyāṉatu uḻavut toḻileṉṟum, ataṟkaṭuttavai iyaṟkaiyum ceyaṟkaiyumāṉa viḷaiporuṭkaḷāṟ ceyyap paṭum palvēṟu kaittoḻilkaḷeṉṟum, avaṟṟiṟkaṭutta nilavaḻiyum nīrvaḻiyum naṭattappeṟum vaṇikameṉṟum, paṇṭait tamiḻar naṉku aṟintiruntaṉar. ataṉāl, mūvēntarum iruvakai vaṇikattaiyum ūkkutaṟkuc cālaikaḷum tuṟai mukaṅkaḷum amaittum, avaṟṟaip pātukāttum vantaṉar.
vaṇikam eṉpatu vaṇij eṉṟum, vāṇikam eṉpatu vāṇijya eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
nāṭṭiṟku uḷḷiruntu āṭciyaic cevvaiyāyc ceytaṟku oru marutanilat talainakarum, nāṭṭōrattiṟ kaṭaṟkaraiyi liruntu nīr vaṇikattai ūkkuvataṟku oru neytal talainakaru māka, iru talainakarai mūvēntarum toṉṟutoṭṭuk koṇṭiruntaṉar.
vēntaṉ  	maruttalaivar  	neytal talainakar
pāṇṭiyaṉ	maturai	koṟkai
cōḻaṉ	uṟaiyūr	pukār (kāvirippūm
			   paṭṭiṉam)
cēraṉ	karuvūr (karūr)	vañci
mutaliru kaḻaka nūlkaḷum muṟṟum aḻintu pōṉamaiyāl, pāṇṭiyariṉ iṭaikkaḻakakkāla marutanilat talainakarum talaikkaḻakakkāla neytal nilat talainakarum, aṟiyap paṭavillai. cērar koṅkunāṭṭai iḻanta piṉṉar, vañcikkē karuvūrp peyarai iṭṭuk koṇṭaṉar.
kōnakarkaḷilum tuṟainakarkaḷilum iruntu nila vaṇikamum nīr vaṇikamum ceytu vanta nakarattār allatu nakara māntar 
eṉṉum vakuppiṉarai, eṇpērāyaṅkaḷuḷ ōrāyamākak koṇṭu, avarai maṉṉar piṉṉōr eṉṟu ciṟappittum, avaruḷ talaimaiyāṉavarkku eṭṭip paṭṭam vaḻaṅkiyum, mūvēntarum ūkki vantaṉar.
eṭṭip paṭṭac ciṉṉamāka oru poṟpū aḷikkappeṟum.
eṭṭippūp peṟṟu  (maṇi. 22: 113)
eṭṭip paṭṭam, ataip peṟṟa vaṇikaṉiṉ maṉaivikkum, allatu makaṭkum matippuravup paṭṭamāka (Title of  Courtesy) vaḻaṅki vantatākap peruṅkatai kūṟum.
eṭṭi kāvitip paṭṭan tāṅkiya
mayiliyaṉ mātar   (peruṅ. ilāvā. 3: 144)
eṭṭip paṭṭattārkku, eṭṭippuravu eṉṉum nilamāṉiyamum aḷikkappaṭṭatāka mayilainātar urai kuṟikkum (naṉ. 158)
kōṭiyum tēṭik koṭimaramum naṭṭi
eṉṉum ulaka vaḻakkut toṭarmoḻiyāl, kōṭip poṉ tēṭiya celvākkuk koṭiyum oṉṟu koṭukkappaṭṭatākat terikiṉṟatu.
eṭṭutal = uyartal. eṭṭam = uyaram. eṭṭi = uyarntōṉ. eṭṭi-ceṭṭi (mutaṉmeyp pēṟu).
o.nō: ēṇ- cēṇ. ēmam- cēmam. ceṭṭiyiṉ taṉmai ceṭṭu.
cirēṣṭi eṉṉum vaṭacol, tiru eṉṉum tamiḻc colliṉ tiripāṉ crī (srī) eṉpataṉ uccattara (superlative degree) vaṭiviṉiṉṟu tirintatākum.

nila vāṇikam
nilavāṇikar vaṇikap paṇṭaṅkaḷaik kutiraikaḷ mēlum kōvēṟu kaḻutaikaḷ mēlum ēṟṟikkoṇṭu kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāyk kāṭṭu vaḻiyē tamiḻakattaiyaṭutta vaṭuka nāṭṭiṟkum neṭuntolaivāṉa vaṭa nāṭṭiṟkum, kāvaṟ paṭaiyuṭaṉ ceṉṟu ērāḷamāyp poruḷīṭṭi vantaṉar. avvaṇikak kūṭṭaṅkaṭkuc cāttu eṉṟu peyar. cāttu kūṭṭam, cārttu - cāttu. cārtal cērtal.
cāttu = 1. kūṭṭam. curivaṉaic cāttu niṟaimati tavaḻum (kallā. 63, 32) 2. vaṇikak kūṭṭam. cāttoṭu pōntu taṉittuya ruḻantēṉ (cilap. 11, 190)
cāttu eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil sārtta eṉṟu tiriyum.
vaṇikac cāttukkaḷiṉ kāval teyvamākiya aiyaṉārkkuc cāttaṉ eṉṟu peyar. ataṉāl, vaṇikarkkuc cāttaṉ cāttuvaṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷ iyaṟ peyarāy vaḻaṅkiṉa. aiyaṉār kōyilil, vaṇikac cāttaik kuṟittaṟku maṇkutirai yuruvaṅkaḷ ceytu vaittiruttalaik kāṇka. cāttaṉ eṉṉum teyvap peyar vaṭamoḻiyil cātā eṉat tiriyum.
iṉi, vaṇikac cāttiṉ talaivaṉum cāttaṉ eṉappaṭuvāṉ. ip peyarum sārtta eṉṟē vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum. itaṉāl, vaṭamoḻiyil cāttaik kuṟikkum coṟkum cāttiṉ talaivaṉaik kuṟikkum coṟkum vēṟupāṭiṉmaiyum, cāttaṉ eṉṉum teyvattaik kuṟikkum col vēṟupaṭṭiruppatum, kaṇṭu uṇmai teḷika.
kullaik kaṇṇi vaṭukar muṉaiyatu

nīrvāṇikam
ulakukiḷarn taṉṉa urukeḻu vaṅkam
 pulavuttiraip peruṅkaṭal nīriṭaip pōḻa
eṉpatāl (akam. 255 : 1-2), kaṭalaip piḷantu cellum māperuṅ kappalkaḷ tamiḻakattiṟ ceyyappaṭṭamai aṟiyappaṭum.
kappalkaḷ taṅkum tuṟaimukattaic cērppu, koṇku eṉpatu ilakkiya vaḻakku.
kīḻ kaṭaṟkaraiyil koṟkai, toṇṭi, pukār (kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉam) eṉṉum tuṟainakarkaḷum, mēl kaṭaṟkaraiyil vañci, muciṟi, toṇṭi eṉṉum tuṟainakarkaḷum iruntaṉa. iṭaik kaḻakak kālattil pāṇṭiyar tuṟainakar kumariyāṟṟiṉ kayavāyil (estuary) amaintiruntatu. ataṉ tamiḻp peyar (alaivāyil?) maṟaintu, ataṉ moḻipeyarppāṉa kapāṭa puram eṉṉum vaṭacollē iṉṟu ilakkiya vaḻakkiliruk kiṉṟatu.
iravil vaḻitappic cellum kalaṅkaṭku vaḻikāṭṭuvataṟku, ovvoru tuṟainakarilum kalaṅkarai viḷakkam (Light house) iruntatu.
ilaṅkunīr varaippiṟ kalaṅkarai viḷakkamum  (cilap. 6 : 141)
vāṉa mūṉṟiya matalai pōla
ēṇi cāttiya ēṟṟaruñ ceṉṉi

iravil māṭṭiya ilaṅkucuṭar ñekiḻi
uravunīr aḻuvat tōṭukalaṅ karaiyum
tuṟai  (perumpāṇ. 349- 51)
kaṭaṟkarai uḷnāṭṭai nōkka mikat tāḻnta maṭṭattiliruppa tāl, tuṟainakarkaḷellām paṭṭiṉam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
patital tāḻntiruttal. paḷḷamāṉa nilattaip patinta nilam eṉpar. pati + aṉam = pataṉam - pattaṉam - paṭṭaṉam - paṭṭiṉam.
takaram ṭakaramāvatu peruvaḻakku.
o.nō:	kottu maṇveṭṭi- koṭṭu maṇveṭṭi.
		kaḷaikkottu- kaḷaikkoṭṭu.
		pottu - poṭṭu - poruttu.
		vīrattāṉam (va.) - vīraṭṭāṉam.
		pataṉam - paṭaṉam = nōyāḷiyaip pātukāttal.
iṉi, pataṉam eṉpatu, kalaṅkaḷ kāṟṟiṉālum koḷḷaik kārarālum cētamiṉṟip pātukāppāyirukkumiṭam eṉṟumām. pataṉam, pātukāppu.
paṭṭiṉam eṉpataip paṭṭaṇam eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku. ik kālattiṟ paṭṭaṇam eṉpatu ceṉṉaiyaic ciṟappāyk kuṟittal pōl, akkālattil paṭṭiṉam eṉpatu pukāraic ciṟappāyk kuṟittatu. paṭṭiṉappālai, paṭṭiṉattup piḷḷaiyār eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷai nōkkuka.
oru tiṇaikkum ciṟappāyuriyataṉṟi nakarap potup peyarāy vaḻaṅkum pati eṉṉum col, makkaḷ pativāy (nilaiyāy) irukkum iṭattaik kuṟikkum. patital nilaiyāyk kuṭi yiruttal.
kappalkaḷ tuṟaimukattiṟku vantu cērumpōtum atai viṭṭup puṟappaṭumpōtum, muracaṅkaḷ muḻakkappaṭṭaṉa.
