nārantam (nārattai) - Ar. Pers. naranj, Fr., It. arancio, E. Orange.
kaṭṭumaram, kalikkō (Calico), tēkku (Teak),  paccilai (patc houli)   veṟṟilai (betel) mutaliya coṟkaḷ kiḻakkintiyak kuḻumpār kālattiṟ ceṉṟaṉavākum. kōḻikkōṭṭiliruntu  (Calicut) ēṟṟumatiyāṉa tuṇi kalikkō eṉappaṭṭatu.
kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻak kalavarum kaṭalōṭikaḷum ulaka muḻutum kalattiṟ cuṟṟiṉamai vaṭavai (Aurora Borealis) eṉṉum collālum, tamiḻar kaṭal vaṇikat toṉmai,
munnīr vaḻakkam makaṭūu vōṭillai
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvālum (980), arapi nāṭṭuk kutiraiyum oṭṭakamum tolkāppiyattiṟ kuṟikkappaṭu vatālum, uṇarappaṭum.
pērā. nīlakaṇṭa cāttiriyārum tām eḻutiya tiruvicaya (Sri Vijaya) nāṭṭu varalāṟṟut tōṟṟuvāyil, “The more we learn the further goes back the history of eastern navigation’  and so far as we know, the Indian Ocean was the first centre of the oceanic activity of man” eṉṟu, tamiḻar mutaṉ mutal intumā vāriyiṟ kalañ celuttiyataiyum avar kaṭal vāṇikat toṉmaiyaiyum kūṟāmaṟ kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
yavaṉar tanta viṉaimāṇ naṉkalam
 poṉṉoṭu vantu kaṟiyoṭu peyarum
eṉpatu (akam. 149: 9-10), kirēkkarum urōmarum poṉ koṇṭu vantu miḷaku vāṅkic ceṉṟataiyum,
viḻumiya nāvāy perunīr ōccunar
 naṉantalait tēettu naṉkalaṉ uymmār
 puṇarntuṭaṉ koṇarnta puraviyō ṭaṉaittum
eṉpatu (maturai. 321-23), arapiyarum piṟarum kutirai koṇṭu vantu aṇikalam vāṅkic ceṉṟataiyum, kūṟum.
12. araciyal
mutaṟ kālattil, muḻukip pōṉa kumarik kaṇṭam muḻuvatum pāṇṭi nāṭākavum, kumarimuṉai mutal paṉimalai varaiyum kuṭapāti cēra nāṭākavum kuṇapāti cōḻa nāṭākavum, iruntaṉa.
paḻam pāṇṭināṭu, teṉpāli nāṭu mutal kumariyāṟu varai eḻu nūṟu kātam (2001 kal) nīṇṭiruntatu. pāṇṭiyaṉ ataiyiḻanta piṉ, piṟkālattup pāṇṭi nāṭākiya nellai maturai mukavai māvaṭṭap pakutiyai, cōḻa nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum kaippaṟṟi yāṇṭāṉ. itaṉ uṇmaiyai,
malitirai yūrntutaṉ maṇkaṭal vauvaliṉ
meliviṉṟi mēṟceṉṟu mēvārnā ṭiṭampaṭap
puliyoṭu vilnīkkip pukaḻpoṟitta kiḷarkeṇṭai
valiyiṉāṉ vaṇakkiya vāṭāccīrt teṉṉavaṉ
eṉṉum mullaikkalit taravālum (4).
aṅṅaṉamākiya nilak kuṟaikkuc cōḻa nāṭṭellaiyilē muttūrk kūṟṟamum cēramāṉāṭṭuk kuṇṭūrk kūṟṟamumeṉṉu mivaṟṟai, iḻanta nāṭṭiṟkāka vāṇṭa teṉṉavaṉ eṉṉum aṭiyārkku nallār uraiyālum (cilap. pak. 303), aṟika.
kumarikkaṇṭam muḻuki, vaṭanāṭṭuk koṭuntamiḻ tiraviṭamākat tirintu vaṭanāṭu moḻipeyar tēyamāṉa piṉ tamiḻakam mikac curuṅkiviṭṭatu. mutaṟkālattil pāṇṭi nāṭu aimmaṇṭalaṅ kaḷākavum, cōḻa nāṭu puṉal maṇṭalam (puṉṉāṭu) toṇṭai maṇṭalam eṉa iru maṇṭalaṅkaḷākavum, cēranāṭu malai maṇṭalam koṅku maṇṭalam eṉa iru maṇṭalaṅkaḷākavum, pirikkappaṭṭiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
pāṇṭiyaṉ aintuṇaiyaracaraik koṇṭu āṇṭatāl, pañcavaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ.
paḻiyoṭu paṭarāp pañcava vāḻi (cilap 20:33).  añcavaṉ eṉṟa collē pañcavaṉ eṉṟu tirintiruttal vēṇṭum.
o.nō: appaḷam - appaḷamu - appaṭamu (te);  appaḷa - pappaḷa (ka.)  pappaṭam (ma.) parppaṭṭa (va); appā - E. papa. 
appaḷit turuṭṭuvatu appaḷam. appaḷittal - camaṉākat tēyttal.
pāṇṭiyaṉaik kuṟikkum pañcavaṉ eṉṉum peyar orumai yeṉṟum, pāṇṭavaraik kuṟikkum pañcavar eṉṉum peyar paṉmai yeṉṟum, vēṟupāṭaṟital vēṇṭum.
toṇṭai maṇṭalam cōḻar āṭciyiṉiṉṟu nīṅkiṉatu pōṉṟē, koṅku maṇṭalamum cērar āṭciyiṉiṉṟu piṟ kālattil nīṅki viṭṭatu. koṅku maṇṭalam mutaliṟ cēra nāṭṭiṉ pakutiyāyiruntamai, kollic cilampaṉ eṉṉum cēraṉ peyarālum, cērar kuṭiyiṉaṉāṉa atikamāṉ takaṭūrai āṇṭatiṉālum, cērar koṅkuvai kāvūrnaṉāṭatil  eṉṟu aruṇakirinātar pāṭiyiruppatālum, vañcikkuk karuvūr eṉṉum peyaruṇmaiyālum, piṟavaṟṟālum, aṟiyappaṭum.
ovvoru nāṭum pala kōṭṭam allatu vaḷanāṭu eṉṉum perum pirivukaḷākavum, ovvoru perum pirivum pala kūṟṟam allatu nāṭu eṉṉum ciṟu pirivukaḷākavum, pirikkappaṭṭi runtaṉa.  muttamiḻ nāṭum orē vēntaṉatu āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭa piṟkālattil, ovvoru tamiḻnāṭum oru peru maṇṭalamākak karutappaṭṭatu. innilaimai muṟkālattil illai. āṭci paṟṟi munnāṭum vēṟupaṭṭavēṉum, moḻi paṟṟit tamiḻakam eṉa oṉṟupaṭṭē yiruntu vantaṉa. ovvoru kūṟṟamum atai yotta nāṭum pala taṉiyūrkaḷākavum pala ciṟṟūrkaḷ cērnta paṟṟukkaḷākavum  pirikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. taṉiyūr ūrāṭciyum paṟṟu ūrāṭci yoṉṟiyamum ottaṉa.
ovvoru taṉiyūrilum allatu paṟṟilum, ūravai eṉṟa aṭippaṭai yāṭcikkuḻu iruntatu. atu āṇṭutōṟum tiruvuḷac cīṭṭup pōṉṟa kuṭavōlaiyāl tērnteṭukkap paṭṭatu. kuṟṟavāḷikaḷum poṟuppaṟṟavarkaḷum atil iṭam peṟavillai. ūrāṭciyiṉ ovvoru tuṟaiyaiyum kavaṉikka, vāriyam eṉṉum uṭkuḻu amaikkappaṭṭatu. av vāriyaṅkaḷ ēri vāriyam, kaliṅku vāriyam, kaḻaṉi vāriyam, tōṭṭa vāriyam, kuṭumpu vāriyam, poṉ vāriyam, kaṇakku vāriyam, kōyil vāriyam, taṭivaḻi (peruñcālai) vāriyam, pañcavāra vāriyam, āṭṭai vāriyam mutaliyaṉavākum. ūravai naṭavaṭikkai kaḷaiyum kaṇakkaiyum eḻuti vaikka ūrkkaṇakkaṉ eṉṉum aluvalaṉ iruntāṉ.
pañcavāra vāriyam varittaṇṭalaik kavaṉippateṉṟum, piṟa vāriyaṅkaḷai mēṟpārppateṉṟum. palavāṟākak kūṟuvar. atu iyaṟkaiyākavō ceyaṟkaiyākavō uṇṭākum uṇavup pañcattait taṭukka ēṟpaṭṭatākavum irukkalām. vaḻakkut tīrttu muṟai ceytal āṭṭai vāriyattiṉ kaṭamaiyākum. urimai. (Civil) vaḻakku, kuṟṟa (Criminal) vaḻakku ākiya iraṇṭaiyum kēṭṭu, kolait taṇṭam ceyyavum av vāriyattiṟku atikāramiruntatu. maṉṟupāṭu, taṇṭā, kuṟṟam eṉat taṇṭaṉai mūvakaip paṭṭiruntatu.
amai - avai, o.nō: ammai - avvai, kumi - kuvi. amaital = neruṅkutal, poruntutal, kūṭutal. amai= kūṭṭam (mutaṉilait toḻilāku peyar). o.nō: imai-kumai. (va.).
ovvoru kūṟṟattaiyum kavaṉikka nāṭāḷvāṉ eṉṟa atikāriyum, ovvoru kōṭṭattaiyum mēṟpārkka nāṭu kaṇkāṭci eṉṟa atikāriyum iruntaṉar.
ūramai ceyyum vāriyap perumakkaḷōm  (S.I.I.i, 117). 
avai - cavai - capā (va.). o.nō:  vaṭavai - vaṭavā (va.) - paṭapā(va.).
aracaṉ talainakariliruntu āṇṭu vantāṉ. aracaṉukkuk kāval toḻilē ciṟappātalālum, kāvalaṉ puravalaṉ eṉṉum peyaruṇmaiyālum, aracu eṉpatu araṇ eṉṉum colliṟku iṉamāy, pātukāppu allatu kāval eṉṉum karuttai aṭippaṭaiyāyk koṇṭatākat terikiṉṟatu. aracu- aracaṉ.
Gk. archon, L. rex, regis, Skt. rajan.
aracaṉ - araicaṉ- araiyaṉ - rāyaṉ (koccai) - rāyalu (te).
E. roy, king, as in Viceroy. ME. royal (adj.) f. OF. roial. 
aracar kuṟunila maṉṉarum perunila maṉṉarum eṉa iru tiṟattār. cilavūrt talaivarāṉa kiḻavarum palavūrt talaivarāṉa vēḷirum porunarum kuṟunila maṉṉar. perunāṭṭut talaiva rāṉa cēra cōḻa pāṇṭiyar mūvarum perunila maṉṉar. avar muṭi cūṭiyatāl vēntar eṉappaṭṭār. kuṟunila maṉṉar avaruk kaṭaṅkiya ciṟṟaracar.
vēyntōṉ- vēntaṉ. vēytal- muṭiyaṇital. koṉṟai vēyntōṉāṉa civaṉaik koṉṟai vēntaṉ eṉṟu auvaiyār kūṟutal kāṇka.
mūvēntarum avar kuṭumpattiṉarum potuvākak kōkkaḷ eṉappaṭuvar. kuṭavarkō, kōpperuñcōḻaṉ, kōyil, kōpperuntēvi, iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ eṉṉum peyarkaḷai nōkkuka.
kōvaṉ - kōṉ = iṭaiyaṉ, iṭaiyaṉ āṭukaḷaik kāppatu pōl makkaḷaik kākkum aracaṉ.
kōṉ - (Turk. Khan) (kāṉ) eṉpar kālṭuvel. kōṉ - kō.
mūvēntarum, aimperuṅkuḻu, eṇpērāyam, uṟutic cuṟṟam eṉa mūvakaip paṭṭa patiṉeṇkaṇat tuṇaivaruṭaṉ, āṭci ceytu vantaṉar.
amaiccar, pōṟṟiyar, paṭaittalaivar, tūtar, oṟṟar eṉpavar aimperuṅ kuḻuvār, amaiccar araciyal viṉaikaḷai amaippavar allatu aracaṉukku neruṅkiyiruppavar. amaital- neruṅkutal. pōṟṟiyar tamiḻap pūcāriyar. pirāmaṇar vantapiṉ, pōṟṟiyar iṭattil purōkitar amarttappaṭṭār. tūtaṉ eṉpatu muṉ celpavaṉ eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum teṉ col. tūtu - tūtaṉ. ivviru collum vaṭamoḻiyil tūta (duta) eṉṟu tiriyum.
kaṇakkar, karumat talaivar, poṉ cuṟṟattār allatu pokkaca cālaiyar, vāyiṟ kāvalar, nakara māntar, paṭaittalaivar, kutirai maṟavar, yāṉai maṟavar eṉpavar eṇpērāyattār. itiṟ paṭait talaivar eṉpār tērppaṭait talaivarāyirukkalām.
āruyir naṇpar, antaṇar, camaiyaṟkārar, maruttuvar, kaṇiyar eṉpavar uṟutic cuṟṟattār.
iṉi, aṟivālum akavaiyālum mūtta periyōrai aracar aṟivuraiyāḷarāka amarttik koḷvatumuṇṭu. avar mutukaṇ eṉappaṭuvar.
ovvōr araciyal tuṟaiyum tiṇaikkaḷam (department)  eṉappaṭṭatu. araciṟait tiṇaikkaḷat talaivaṉ puravuvarit tiṇaikkaḷa nāyakam eṉappaṭṭāṉ. nilavari, toḻilvari, vaṇikap pōkkuvarattu vari, nallā nallerutu mutaliya iyaṅku tiṇaivari, tirumaṇa vari (kaliyāṇak kāṇam) kāval vari mutaliya palavakai varikaḷ kuṭikaḷiṭam vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. nāṭukūṟu eṉpavaṉ nilat tīrvaiyait tiṭṭañ ceypavaṉ. variyilār eṉpavar varukiṉṟa varit tokaikaḷai vāṅkip pativu ceypavar. varik kūṟiṭuvār eṉpavar pativu ceyta varit tokai 
kaḷaip paṅkiṭupavar allatu pākupāṭu ceypavar. varip pottakam eṉpavaṉ nilavarik kaṇakkaṉ. puravu vari eṉpavaṉ piṟa varik kaṇakkaṉ. nilavari viḷaivil āṟiloru paṅku kūlamākavum, piṟa varikaḷ kuṟippiṭṭa paṇat tokaiyākavum, vāṅkappaṭṭaṉa. ūravaiyār avaṟṟai vāṅkit talainakari luḷḷa paṇṭārattiṟkum pokkaca cālaikkum aṉuppi vantaṉar. nilavariyait tiṭṭañ ceyya nilaṅkaḷ ellām tulliyamāy aḷakkap paṭṭaṉa.
vēntaṉ amaitik kālattil ovvoru nāḷum pakalil mutaṟ pattu nāḻikai koṭaikkum, iṭaippattu nāḻikai vaḻakkut tīrttu muṟai ceyvataṟkum, kaṭaip pattu nāḻikai taṉ illaṟa iṉpattiṟkum celaviṭṭāṉ. avaṉ kālaiyil eḻuntavuṭaṉ paḷḷiyeḻucci muracam aṟaiyappaṭum. eṭṭar eṉpavar, avaṉukku nāḻikai yaṟivippavar. akavar (cūtar) eṉpavar, niṉṟu koṇṭu avaṉ muṉṉōr perumaiyaik kūṟi avaṉaip pukaḻntu pāṭupavar. ōvar (mākatar) eṉpavar, iruntu koṇṭu aṅṅaṉam pāṭupavar. ēttāḷar piṟa camaiyaṅkaḷiṟ pukaḻntu pāṭupavar.
vēntaṉ taṉ kolu maṇṭapattiṟkuc cellumpōtum, aṅku amarntirukkum pōtum, atiṉiṉṟu mīḷumpōtum, mey kāvalar pakkat tuṇaiyāvar. piṟa nāṭṭaracarum avar tūtarum kuṟunila maṉṉarum patiṉeṇ kaṇattārum tiṇaikkaḷat talaivarum nāṭṭup perumakkaḷum kūṭiya kolu maṇṭapattil, aracu kaṭṭil eṉṉum ariyaṇaiyil vēntaṉ vīṟṟirukkum kolu ōlakkam eṉappaṭum. kālaik kolu nāḷōlakkam eṉṟum, aritāyk kūṭum mālaik kolu allōlakkam eṉṟum peyar peṟum. koṭai vēḷai koṭai muracum muṟai vēḷai muṟai muracum aṟaintu terivikkappeṟum.
vēntaṉ araciyaṟ paṇiyil īṭupaṭṭirukkum pōtellām, cila atikārikaḷ avaṉuṭaṉiruppar. avar uṭaṉ kūṭṭattār eṉappaṭuvar.
vēntaṉ avvappōtu iṭum kaṭṭaḷaikaḷait tiruvāyk kēḷvi eṉpavar kēṭṭu vantu, eḻuttāḷariṭam aṟivippār. vēntaṉ kaṭṭaḷaikaḷai eḻutupavar tirumantiravōlai eṉpār. avarukkut talaivarāyiruppavar tirumantiravōlai nāyakam eṉappaṭuvar. nāḷtōṟum naṭappavaṟṟai nikaḻccik kuṟippil eḻutivaippavar paṭṭōlaip perumāṉ. ūravai kaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟa atikārikaḷiṭattiruntum varum ōlaikaḷaip paṭittup pārttu, avaṟṟiṟkut takka viṭaiyaṉuppuvōr viṭaiyilār eṉpōr. vēntaṉ kaṭṭaḷaikaḷ pativu ceyyap paṭum pottakam kēḷvi vari eṉappaṭum.
nāṭṭut talainakarilum kōṭṭat talainakarilum, nilap pativu ceyyum āvaṇak kaḷariyum aṟaṅkūṟavaiyam eṉṉum vaḻakkut tīrppu maṉṟamum, iruntatākat terikiṉṟatu. aracar vaḻakkuk kaḷaiyum aṟaṅkūṟavaiyattāriṉ tavaṟāṉa tīrppukkaḷaiyum, vēntaṉē kavaṉittu vantāṉ.
varip paṇamum putaiyalum ciṟṟaracariṭum tiṟaiyum tōṟṟup pōṉa pakaiyaracar koṭukkum taṇṭamum, vēntaṉ varuvāy kaḷākum.
paṇam, acciṭṭa kācākavum niṟaip poṉṉākavum iruvakai yil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. akkam, kācu, kāṇam, poṉ, māṭai mutaliyaṉa kācu vakaikaḷ. poṟkācukaḷaiyum poṟ kaṭṭikaḷaiyum nōṭṭañ ceyyum atikārikaḷ vaṇṇakkar eṉṉappaṭṭār. kācu- E. cash 
kōnakar kāvalum pāṭikāval eṉṉum ūrk kāvalum pōkkuvarattuc cālaikkāvalum, iravum pakalum oḻuṅkāy naṭaipeṟṟaṉa.
nilaṉakaḻ uḷiyar kalaṉnacaiik koṭkum
kaṇmā ṟāṭavar oṭukkam oṟṟi
vayakkaḷiṟu pārkkum vayappuli pōlat
tuñcāk kaṇṇar añcāk koḷkaiyar
aṟintōr pukaḻnta āṇmaiyar ceṟinta
nūlvaḻip piḻaiyā nuṇaṅkunuṇ tērcci
ūrkāp pāḷar ūkkaruṅ kaṇaiyiṉar
tērvaḻaṅku teruvil nīrtiraṇ ṭoḻuka
maḻaiyamain tuṟṟa araināḷ amayamum
acaivilar eḻuntu nayamvantu vaḻaṅkaliṉ
kaṭavuḷ vaḻaṅkum kaiyaṟu kaṅkulum
accam aṟiyā tēma mākiya
maṟṟai yāmam pakaluṟak kaḻippi
eṉṉum maturaik kāñcip pakuti (641-53), maturaiyil ūrkāvalar perumaḻai poḻinta naḷḷiravilum ūkkamāyc cuṟṟi vantu ūr kāttamaiyait terivikkum.
marutanilat talainakarellām, akaḻiyālum pala vakaip poṟi kaḷaik koṇṭa kōṭṭai matilālum cūḻap peṟṟiruntaṉa. kirēkkarum urōmarum matil vāyiṟ kāvalarāyiruntaṉar.
kaṭimatil vāyil kāvaliṟ ciṟanta
aṭalvāḷ yavaṉar  (cilap. 14: 66-7)
karipari tērkāl eṉṉum nālvakaip paṭaikaḷ vēntaṉuk kiruntaṉa. terinteṭukkappeṟṟa ciṟanta porunaraik koṇṭa taṉip paṭaippirivu kaḷumiruntaṉa. avai terinta eṉṉum aṭaimoḻiyāl vitantu kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
e-ṭu: uttama cōḻat terinta antaḷakattāḷar  (S.I.I.ii , 97) 
antaḷakam, meymmaṟai (kavacam).
iṉi, vēntaṉ mēl aḷaviṟanta paṟṟuṭaiyavarāy, avaṉukkut tuṉpam nērnta vēḷaiyil uyiraik koṭuttuk kākkavum uṭaṉ māyavum cūḷiṭṭuk koṇṭa pōr maṟavarumiruntaṉar. avar vēḷaikkārar eṉappaṭṭār. avar uyir koṭuttal ceñcōṟṟuk kaṭaṉ kaḻittal eṉappaṭṭatu.
vēntaṉ iṟantapiṉ, allatu tuṟantapiṉ, allatu kaḻiperu mūp paṭaintapiṉ, avaṉ mūttamakaṉ, mūtta makaṉ illāviṭṭāl iḷaiya makaṉ, makaṉē illāviṭṭāl makaḷ, makaḷum illā viṭṭāl takutiyuḷḷa neruṅkiya uṟaviṉaṉ, muṭicūṭṭap paṭṭār. taṭātakai yeṉṉum kayaṟkaṇṇiyār pāṇṭi nāṭṭai yāṇṭataiyum, cēramāṉ perumāḷ nāyaṉār cēranāṭṭai yāṇṭataiyum kāṇka. muṭicūṭṭu viḻāvilum vēntaṉ piṟanta nāḷākiya veḷḷaṇi viḻāvilum, piṟa ciṟanta nikaḻccikaḷilum, kōpperuntēviyum uṭaṉ kolu viruppāḷ.
kāviti eṉpatu ciṟanta amaiccaṉukkum, ēṉāti eṉpatu ciṟanta paṭait talaivaṉukkum, vēḷ allatu piḷḷai eṉpatu ciṟanta kuṟunila maṉṉarukkum, māvaraiyaṉ eṉpatu ciṟanta araciyaṟ karumat talaivaṉukkum, eṭṭi eṉpatu ciṟanta vaṇikaṉukkum, ciṟutaṉam, peruntaṉam, talaikkōl eṉpaṉa nāṭakak kaṇikaiyarkkum, vēntaṉ aḷikkum paṭṭaṅkaḷākum.
ēṉai + ari = ēṉāri (yāṉaikaḷai aḻippavaṉ) -  ēṉāti.      ta-ra oṉṟiṟ koṉṟu pōliyāka varum. e-ṭu:  vitai- virai, kuralvaḷai- kutavaḷai (koccaiyulaka vaḻakku). māvaraiyaṉ - māvaraiyam- mārāyam.
mārāyam  peṟṟa neṭumoḻi yāṉum  (tol. puṟat. 8)
araciyal viṉaiñar ūtiyattiṟkuk kaimmāṟākak koṭukkap paṭṭatu, nellum poṉṉumāyiṉ campaḷam eṉṟum, nilamāṉiyamāyiṉ umpaḷam eṉṟum, peyar peṟṟaṉa.
13. kalvi
kalvi oru kulattārkku maṭṭum eṉṟu varaiyaṟukkappaṭātu, ellāt toḻil vakuppārkkum potuvāyiruntatu. itai,
tantaimakaṟ kāṟṟum naṉṟi yavaiyattu
munti yiruppac ceyal.
eṉṉum kuṟaḷālum (67),
īṉṟupuṟan tarutal eṉṟalaik kaṭaṉē
cāṉṟō ṉākkutal tantaikkuk kaṭaṉē
vēlvaṭittuk koṭuttal kollaṟkuk kaṭaṉē
oḷiṟuvāḷ aruñcamam murukkik
kaḷiṟeṟintu peyartal kāḷaikkuk kaṭaṉē.  (puṟam. 312)
vēṟṟumai terinta nāṟpā luḷḷum
kīḻppāl oruvaṉ kaṟpiṉ
mēṟpā loruvaṉum avaṉkaṭ paṭumē  (puṟam. 183)
eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭukkaḷālum, palvēṟu toḻilār kaṭaik kaḻakak kālattup pulavarāyiruntamaiyālum, aṟiyalām.
kūlavāṇikaṉ cīttalaic cāttaṉār, poṉ vāṇikaṉār makaṉār nappūtaṉār, aṟuvai vāṇikaṉ iḷavēṭṭaṉār, iḷam poṉ vaṇikaṉār, maruttuvaṉ tāmōtaraṉār, taṇ kāṟpūṭ kollaṉār, ōlaikkaṭaik kaṇṇam pukuntārāyat taṉār, vaṭama vaṇṇakkaṉ peruñcāttaṉār mutaliya pulavar palvēṟu toḻilarā yiruntamai kāṇka.
kalvi peṇṭirkku vilakkappaṭavillai. okkūr mācāttiyār, auvaiyār, kākkai pāṭiṉiyār nacceḷḷaiyār, kāvaṟ peṇṭu, kuṟamakaḷ iḷaveyiṉi, nakkaṇṇaiyār, pāri makaḷir, pūtappāṇṭiyaṉ tēviyār, pēymakaḷ iḷaveyiṉi, māṟōk kattu nappacalaiyār, veṇṇik kuyattiyār, veṟipāṭiya kāmak kaṇṇiyār mutaliyōr peyar veḷivanta kaṭaikkaḻakak kālap pulattiyar. akkālattuk kuṭittaṉap peṇṭir tam kalviyaṟivai yellām illaṟattiṟkē payaṉpaṭuttiyamaiyāl, avaruṭ perumpālār peyar veḷivaravillai. kāraikkālammaiyār ciṟanta pulattiyarā yiruntum, kaṇavarāl vilakkap paṭumvarai, avar pāvaṉmai veḷippaṭā tiruntamai kāṇka. civappirakāca aṭikaḷ kālamāṉa patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭilum, tirukkāṭṭup paḷḷiyil teruvil uppu viṟkum peṇ orutti, aṭikaḷ niṟaiyavuḷatō  eṉṟu pāṭi viṉaviya veṇpāviṟku viṭaiyāka,
teṉṉōṅku tillaic civappirakā capperumāṉ
poṉṉōṅku cēvaṭiyaip pōṟṟiṉōm - aṉṉōṉ
tirukkūṭṭam attaṉaikkum teṇṭaṉiṭṭōm tīrāk
karukkūṭṭam pōkkiṉōm kāṇ.
eṉṟu kaṭuttup pāṭiṉamai kāṇka. kaṭuttal - viraital.
pukaḻēntip pulavar kālamāṉa 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭil, uḻavaṉ, kollaṉ, taṭṭāṉ, taccaṉ, mañcikaṉ (muṭi tiruttāḷaṉ) mutaliya palvakait toḻilāḷarum ciṟanta ceyyuḷ ceyyum āṟṟal peṟṟiruntamaiyāl, paṇṭaikkālak kalvip parappu aṟiyappaṭum. āṟalaikkum kaḷvar kūṭa akkālattiṟ kaṟṟuvalla pāvalarā yiruntirukkiṉṟaṉar.
ikkālattiṟpōl kalvit tuṟai eṉṉum araciyal tuṟai akkālattillai. makkaḷ taṉippaṭṭa muṟaiyil, āciriyaṉukkup poruḷ koṭuttum toṇṭu ceytum kaṟṟu vantaṉar.
uṟṟuḻi yutaviyum uṟuporuḷ koṭuttum
 piṟṟainilai muṉiyātu kaṟṟal naṉṟē
eṉṟu āriyappaṭai kaṭanta neṭuñceḻiyaṉ pāṭiyiruttal kāṇka. (puṟam. 183).
kaṟpippōr kaṇakkāyaṉ eṉṟum āciriyaṉ eṉṟum kuru allatu kuravaṉ eṉṟum mūvakaiyar. kaṇakkāyaṉ eḻuttum ciṟṟilakkiyamum uriccollum (nikaṇṭum) kaṇakkum kaṟpip pōṉ; āciriyaṉ, piṟkālattil aintāka virikkappaṭṭa mūvilakkaṇamum, avaṟṟiṟ keṭuttuk kāṭṭāṉa pērilakkiyamum kaṟpippōṉ; kuravaṉ camaya nūlum paṭṭāṅku (tattuva) nūlum kaṟpippōṉ.
kaṟṟōrkkuc ceṉṟaviṭamellām ciṟappiruntatu. vēntaraip pukaḻntu pāṭiya pulavarkkuc ciṟanta paricum muṟṟūṭṭum (carva māṉiyamum) aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa.
pāṇṭiyar talaiciṟanta pulavarai yellāṅ kūṭṭi, tamiḻk kaḻakam niṟuvip pōṟṟiṉar.
pulavariṉ pāṭalkaḷum nūlkaḷum, pāṇṭiyar tamiḻk kaḻakattil kuṟṟamaṟṟavaiyeṉa oppuk koḷḷappaṭṭāloḻiya, ulakil vaḻaṅkā. aṅṅaṉam oppam peṟutal araṅkēṟṟam eṉap paṭṭatu.
nāṭakak kaṇikaiyariṉ āṭal pāṭalkaḷum vēntar muṉṉilai yilēyē araṅkēṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. araṅkēṟiya kaṇikaiyar talaik kōṟ paṭṭamum āyiratteṇ kaḻañcu paricamum peṟṟaṉar.
ittakaiya araṅkēṟṟa muṟaiyāl, akkālattuk kalvi talai ciṟantiruntatu. araip paṭippiṉarum tiripuṇarcciyarum talai yeṭukka iṭamillai, pala tuṟaiyilum pulamai peṟṟa pēraṟiñar, tavaṟākak kaṟpittu makkaḷaik keṭuppavaraik koṭikaṭṭi aṟaikūvi varavaḻaittu, coṟpōr purintu tōṟkaṭittut taṇṭittu aṟivu pukaṭṭuvatum akkāla vaḻakkam.
palkēḷvit tuṟaipōkiya
tollāṇai nallāciriyar
uṟaḻkuṟit teṭutta uyarkeḻu koṭi  (paṭṭiṉap. 167-171)
kalvi eṉṉum col, kal eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākum. kallutal, tōṇṭutal. māntaṉ mutaṉ mutaṟ kaṟṟa kalvi uḻavut toḻilātalāl, kalvi eṉṉum col mutalil uḻavut toḻilaiyē kuṟittiruttal vēṇṭum. āṅkilattil nilap paṇpāṭṭaiyum uḷappaṇpāṭṭaiyum oruṅkē uṇarttum culture, cultivation eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum, col eṉṉum orē ilattīṉ vērc colliṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyiruppatu, iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkatu.

14. kalaikaḷ
payiṟciyāṟ kaṟkappaṭuvaṉa kalaikaḷ.
(1) icai
kumarikkaṇṭat tamiḻar nuṇmāṇ nuḻai pulattarāyum talai ciṟanta nākarikarāyum eḵkuc ceviyarāyu miruntatiṉāl, ēḻu pēricaiyum aintu ciṟṟicaiyumākiya paṉṉīricaiyai (curattai) yum kaṇṭu āyappālai eṉṉum muṟaiyil eḻupālaip paṇkaḷait tirittatumaṉṟi, ap paṉṉīricaiyaiyum vaṭṭap pālai eṉṉum muṟaiyil 24 ākavum, tirikōṇappālai eṉṉum muṟaiyil 48 ākavum caturappālai eṉṉum muṟaiyil 96 ākavum, nuṭpamāyp pakuttu ellaiyaṟṟa icaip pēriṉ pattai nukarntirukkiṉṟaṉar. paṉṉīricaikku mēṟpaṭṭa nuṇṇi caiyiṉai eṭuttuk kūṟum nūlē icai nuṇukkam pōlum!  ēḻicaiyum kural, tuttam, kaikkiḷai, uḻai, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. ciṟṟicai ākaṇam eṉṟum, kuralum iḷiyumallāta pēricai antaram eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
tiri, caturam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ tamiḻē eṉpatu eṉ vaṭamoḻi varalāṟṟil viḷakkappeṟum. catur eṉpatu, nāṉku eṉṉum eṇṇup poruḷilaṉṟi, nāṟ kōṇam eṉṉum vaṭivup poruḷil vaṭamoḻiyilillai.
akanilai marutam, puṟanilai marutam, arukiyal marutam, perukiyal marutam eṉṉum paṇ vakuppum, kiḻamai niṟaikuṟai eṉpaṉavum, tamiḻariṉ icai nuṇukkattaik kāṭṭum.
ikkālattu āriya icaiyaṟiñar, paṇṭait tamiḻariṉ aṟivu nuṇukkattai ōrātu, tampōl avaraiyuṅ karuti 96 icaikaḷ pāṭaṟkiyalātavai eṉpar.
icaik karuvikaḷ tōl,tuḷai, narampu, veṇkalam (kañcam) eṉa nālvakaip paṭṭiruntaṉa.
tōṟ karuvikaḷ āṭal muḻā, pāṭal muḻā, potu muḻā eṉṟum;  akamuḻavu, akappuṟa muḻavu, puṟamuḻavu, puṟappuṟa muḻavu, paṇṇamai muḻavu, nāḷ muḻavu, kālai muḻavu, eṉṟum; maṇappaṟai, piṇappaṟai eṉṟum;  vakukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. paṟai eṉpatu tōṟkaruvip potup peyar. teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puṭpaṟai (tol. 964).
iṉṉicait tuḷaik karuvikaḷ pullāṅ kuḻal, icaikkuḻal (nātacuram), mukavīṇai eṉṉum ēḻicaik kuḻalkaḷākavum; ottu (ūmaik kuḻal), eṉṉum ōricaik kuḻalākavum;  iruvakaip paṭṭiruntaṉa. tārai, ciṉṉam, vāṅkā, kompu, caṅku mutaliya ōricait tuḷaik karuvikaḷ, ūrvalam pōr mutaliya nikaḻccikaḷil āravāra icaik karuvikaḷāyp payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭaṉa. makuṭi eṉpatu nalla pāmpai mayakkutaṟkeṉṟē amaikkappaṭṭa ēḻicaic ciṟappiṉṉicaik kuḻalākum.
narappuk karuvikaḷ oru narampuḷḷa curaiyāḻiliruntu āyiram narampukaḷ peruṅkalam (ātiyāḻ) varai, palvēṟu vakaip paṭṭiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuḷ, pēriyāḻ, makarayāḻ, cakōṭayāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṉum nāṉkum ciṟantavai. avaṟṟuḷḷum talaiciṟantatu ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ. atuvē piṉṉar vīṇaiyākat tirintatu. āyiram narampeṉpatu, āyiraṅkāṟ pūcci eṉpatiṟ pōl, narampiṉ peruntokaiyaiyē kuṟikkum.
iṉṉicai vīṇaiyar yāḻiṉar orupāl eṉṉum tiruvācakak kūṟṟu (369) ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ vīṇaiyeṉṟu ciṟappup peyar peṟṟamaiyaiyē kāṭṭum. ellā narappukkaruvikaḷum yāḻē. vīṇai eṉṉum peyarum viṇ eṉṉum vēriṉiṉṟu piṟanta teṉcollē. atu vaṭamoḻiyil vīṇā eṉṟu tiriyum. viṇṇeṉal, narampu teṟittolittal. viṇ-vīṇai mutukuṉṟam (paḻamalai) viruttācalameṉṟu peyar māṟiyatāl vēṟu nakaramākiviṭātu. aṅṅaṉamē vīṇaiyeṉappaṭum ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻum eṉka.
iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa cilappatikārattil nārataṉ vīṇai nayanteri pāṭalum eṉṟum, 7ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉatāṉa appar tēvārattil mācil vīṇaiyum eṉṟum, varutalāl, vīṇai 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiyateṉpatu poruntātu. vēta kāla nāratar tamiḻnāṭu vantē icai kaṟṟup pañcapāratīyam eṉṉum icait tamiḻ nūlai iyaṟṟiṉār. pattām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṉatāṉa cīvaka cintāmaṇik kāntaruvatattai yilampakattil, yāḻeṉṟum vīṇaiyeṉṟum orē karuvi kuṟikkap paṭuvatāl, yāḻ vēṟu vīṇai vēṟu alla. ikkālattil curaiyāḻum vīṇaiyeṉṟē peyar peṟṟiruttal kāṇka.
tiruñāṉa campantar pāṭiya yāḻmurip paṇṇiṉ iyalpu iṉṟu evarukkum teriyāmaiyāl, yāḻmuri vīṇaikkamaiyum eṉpatum poruntātu.
tāḷak karuvi veṇkalattiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatiṉāl, atu veṇkalak karuviyeṉappaṭṭatu. cal allatu callarai eṉṉum ōcaiyuṭaimaiyāl, tāḷak karuviyiṟ periyatu callariyeṉṟum ciṟiyatu cālar eṉṟum peyar peṟṟaṉa.
kural eṉpatu ma eṉṉum 5ām icaiyeṉṟum, oru narampil orēyicai yeḻuum narappuk karuviyē tamiḻaratu eṉṟum, kūṟuvār tamiḻarāyiṉum icait tamiḻ aṭippaṭaiyē aṟiyātār āvar. `kural mutalēḻum eṉṟu iḷaṅkōvaṭi kaḷum (cilap. 5 : 35), mutaṟṟāṉamākiya kuralilē eṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟu uraiyāciriyarum (puṟam. 11), mūvēḻtuṟaiyum muṟaiyuḷik kaḻippi eṉṟu vaṉparaṇarum (puṟam. 152), kūṟiyiruttalaiyum, avar kavaṉittilar.
kural eṉṉum collē, iyalpāṉa kuralākiya mutalicaiyaiyē kuṟikkum. curaiyāḻum peruṅkalamumē vilyāḻ (Harp) pōl meṭṭiṉṟi ōricaikkoru narampu koṇṭavai. pēriyāḻ mutaliya narampu koṇṭavai. pēriyāḻ mutaliya piṟa yāḻellām meṭṭukkaḷōṭu kūṭi, orē narampil pala vicai yicaikkakkūṭiya paṇmoḻi narampukaḷ koṇṭaṉa vākavē terikiṉṟaṉa.
paṇkaḷai, ēḻicaiyumuḷḷavai paṇ eṉṟum, āṟicaiyuḷḷa paṇṇiyal eṉṟum, aiyicaiyuḷḷavai tiṟam eṉṟum, nālicaiyuḷḷavai tiṟattiṟam eṉṟum, nālvakaiyāka vakuttiruntaṉar. in nālvakaip paṇkaḷum mottam 11,991 eṉak kaṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
icaiyāvatu, narappaṭaivāl uraikkappaṭṭa patiṉōrā yittut toḷḷā yirattut toṇṇūṟṟoṉṟākiya ātiyicai kaḷum; avaiyāvaṉa: uyiruyir... kaṭaṉē eṉṉuñ cūttirat tāṉ uṟaḻntu kaṇṭukoḷka eṉṟu cilappatikāra aruñ colluraikārar (pak. 64) kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka. pālai, kuṟiñci, marutam, cevvaḻi eṉpaṉa nāṟperum paṇkaḷ. ivaṟṟuḷ ovvoṉṟum pala tiṟaṅkaḷākavum ovvoru tiṟamum palavakaikaḷākavum pakukkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
ēḻicaiyum cērnta oru varicai allatu kōvai nilaiyeṉap paṭum. ciṟanta pāṭakariṉ toṇṭaiyil kīḻum iṭaiyum mēlumāka munnilaiyiruppatāl, avaṟṟiṟkēṟpa nālvakaic ciṟappiyāḻum narampum meṭṭum amaikkappeṟṟaṉa. mun nilaiyum, muṟaiyē melivu, camaṉ, valivu eṉap peyar peṟṟaṉa. nālvakai yāḻilum meṭṭut tokai vēṟu paṭṭiruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
paṇmoḻi narampu ēḻum oṟṟu narampu mūṉṟum ārppu narampu patiṉoṉṟumāka, 21 narampukaḷ pēriyāḻilum; paṇmoḻi narampu iraṇṭu kuṟaintu 19 narampukaḷ makarayāḻilum; ārppu narampu aintu kuṟaintu 14 narampukaḷ cakōṭayāḻilum; ārppu narampu aṭiyōṭu illātum paṇmoḻiyiraṇṭu kuṟaintum iṟṟai vīṇaiyiṟpōl 7 narampukaḷ ceṅkōṭṭi yāḻilum; iruntatākak karutalām. oṉpatu narampuḷḷa muṇṭakayāḻ 4 paṇmoḻi narampum 3 oṟṟu narampum 2 ārppu narampum koṇṭatu pōlum!
narappuc cittār eṉṉum mukamatiyar narappicaik karuviyil, iṉṟum 11 ārppu narampukaḷ kaṭṭappeṟutal kāṇka. avaṟṟi ṉāl uṇṭākum atirvu icaiyiṉimaiyaik keṭuttatiṉāl, avaṟṟai varavarak kuṟaittu vantu iṟutiyil aṭiyōṭu nīkki viṭṭaṉar. icaiyiṉpa vuṇarcci perukap peruka, yāḻkaḷ tirunti vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. varavarat tiruntutalē yaṉṟit tirun tāmai iyaṟkaiyaṉṟu. anākarika māntar icaik karuvikaḷiṉ ōcai mikutiyaiyum, nākarika makkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ iṉimai mikutiyai yum,  iṉṟum virumputal iyalpāyiruttal kāṇka.
yāḻppattariṉ mūṭiyākiya pōrvai, muṉpu tōlāyiruntu piṉpu pātukāppiṟkāka maramāka māṟṟap peṟṟatu. yāḻiṉ amaippai aṟiya virumpuvār, tañcai āpirakām paṇṭitar makaṉār varakuṇa pāṇṭiyaṉār ārāynteḻutiya pāṇar kaivaḻi eṉṉum nūlaip pārkka. aruṭṭiru vipulāṉanta aṭikaḷiṉ yāḻnūl aṭippaṭaiyil tavaṟiyatāl mutaṟ kōṇal muṟṟuṅkōṇalāyp pōyiṟṟu.
ulakiṟ ciṟanta icai intiya icaiyē. atiṟ ciṟantatu tamiḻicai. ataiyē iṉṟu uḻaiyicaiyaṭippaṭaiyil tāyp paṇkaḷai yum kiḷaip paṇkaḷaiyum vakuttum, paḻantamiḻk kuṟiyīṭu kaḷaiyum paṇṇuppeyarkaḷaiyum vaṭacollāka māṟṟiyum, karunāṭaka caṅkītam eṉap peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉar. kēḷviyaic curuti yeṉṟum, nilaiyai tāy eṉṟum, moḻipeyarttiruttal kāṇka. karunāṭaka caṅkītattiṟku ilakkaṇam vakutta vēṅkaṭamakiyum (17ām nūṟ). ataṟku ilakkiyamākat teluṅku kīrttaṉaikaḷ pāṭiya tiyākarācarum (18ām nūṟ.) tamiḻ nāṭṭil vāḻntavarē.
koṭṭu, acai, tūkku, aḷavu eṉṉum nāluṟuppukkaḷai yuṭaiya tāḷattai, akak kūttiṟkuriya patiṉoru pāṇikaḷum puṟakkūttiṟkuriya nāṟpattoru tāḷamumāka, aimpattiruvakaip paṭuttiyiruntaṉar.
ēḻicaikkuriya eḻuttukkaḷ mutaṟkālattil ēḻ uyir neṭil kaḷāyiruntaṉa. avai niṟavāḷatti eṉṉum ciṭṭaiyicaikku ēṟkāmaiyāl ca ri ka ma pa ta ni eṉṉum eḻuttukkaḷ nāḷaṭaiviṟ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. ivai ṣaṭjam, riṣapam, kāntāram, mattiyamam, pañcamam, taivatam, niṣātam eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaḷiṉ mutaleḻuttukkaḷākac collap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ik koḷkai mattiyamam pañcamam eṉṉum iraṇṭiṟkē ēṟkum. camaṉ paṭṭaṭai eṉṉum iru teṉ coṟkaḷiṉ muta leḻuttukkaḷē ca pa eṉpatu, cilar karuttu. ivaiyiraṇṭumaṉṟi, icaiyiṉpattiṟkēṟṟa ēḻeḻuttukkaḷ nāḷaṭaiviṟ paṭṭaṟivi ṉiṉṟu terintukoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatē, perumpāl tamiḻicaivāṇar karuttām.
paṉṉiraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭuvarai, icaippāṇarum yāḻp pāṇarum kuḻaṟpāṇarum maṇṭaip pāṇarumāṉa nālvakaip pāṇarē perumpālum tamiḻ nāṭṭil icaivāṇarāyiruntu tamiḻicaiyaip pōṟṟi vantaṉar. pāṇ cēriyil pāṭṭup pāṭukiṟatā? eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum, tirumuṟaikaṇṭa cōḻaṉ tirunīlakaṇṭa yāḻppāṇar marapil vanta oru  peṇṇaik koṇṭu tēvārattiṟku icai vakuppittamaiyum, itai valiyuṟuttum. āriyak kulappiriviṉai ēṟpaṭṭapiṉ, pāṇar tīṇṭātavarākit tam tolvaravup pāṇtoḻilai iḻantaṉar. eḻūum icait tokaiyiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiya ēḻ eṉṉum eṇṇup peyarum,
teyvam uṇāvē māmaram puḷpaṟai
ceyti yāḻiṉ pakutiyoṭu tokaii
avvakai piṟavum karuveṉa moḻipa.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvum (akat. 18), tamiḻicaiyiṉ toṉmaiyai uṇarttum. iṉi icait tamiḻ nūlākiya perunārai peruṅkurukum piṟavum tēva viruṭi nārataṉ ceyta pañca pāratīya mutalāvuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷu miṟantaṉa eṉṉum aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟṟāl, vaṭamoḻiyicai nūlkaṭ kellām tamiḻnūlkaḷē mutaṉūleṉa aṟika.
aḷapiṟan tuyirttalum oṟṟicai nīṭalum
uḷaveṉa moḻipa icaiyoṭu civaṇiya
narampiṉ maṟaiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpāvāl (33), tamiḻicaiyiṉ yāḻc ciṟappu uṇarappaṭum.
(2) nāṭakam
nāṭakam eṉpatu teṉcollē. naḷḷutal = poruntutal, ottal. naḷ - naḷi. naḷiya eṉpatu ōr uvamavurupu. naḷital ottal. naḷi - naṭi. o.nō : kaḷiṟu - kaṭiṟu. naṭittal eṉpatu, iṉṉoru vaṉaip pōl allatu illātatai uḷḷatu pōlac ceytu kāṭṭutal.
muttamiḻ toṉṟutoṭṭu iyalicai nāṭakam eṉṟē vaḻaṅkum.
nāṭakak kalai kūttu, naṭaṉam, nāṭakam eṉa muttiṟappaṭum.
kutittāṭuvatu kūttu. atu vēttiyal, potuviyal; ulakiyal, tēviyal; vacaik kūttu, pukaḻkkūttu; varikkūttu variyamaitik kūttu (variccāntik kūttu; amaitik kūttu). (cāntik kūttu), vēṭikkaik kūttu (vinōtak kūttu); akak kūttu, puṟak kūttu; viḷaiyāṭṭuk kūttu, viṉaik kūttu; veṟṟik kūttu, tōlvik kūttu; eṉap palvēṟu vakaiyil ivviruvakaippaṭum.
naṭaṉam allatu naṭam eṉpatu, aḻakuṟa āṭuvatu. atu nūṟṟeṭṭu uṭaṟkaraṇaṅ kaḷōṭum kaikāl kaṇvāy mutaliya uṟuppukkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷōṭum kūṭiyatu. kaiviṉaikaḷ eḻiṟkai toḻiṟkai, poruṭkai eṉa muttiṟappaṭṭu, piṇṭi allatu iṇaiyā viṉaikkai yeṉappaṭum oṟṟaikkai vaṇṇam muppattu mūṉṟum, piṇaiyal allatu iṇaikkai yeṉappaṭum iraṭṭaikkai vaṇṇam patiṉaintum, koṇṭaṉavākum.
tamiḻ naṭaṉam iṉṟu parata nāṭṭiyam eṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. parata cāttiram vaṭamoḻiyil iyaṟṟappaṭṭatu. ki.mu. 4ām nūṟṟāṇṭākum. ataṟkum muntiyatu tamiḻp paratamē yeṉpatai, nāṭakat tamiḻ nūlākiya paratam akattiya mutalā vuḷḷa toṉṉūlkaḷumiṟantaṉa, eṉṟu aṭiyārkku nallār cilappatikāravuraip pāyirattiṟ kūṟiyiruppatāl aṟintu koḷka.
nāṭakam eṉpatu katai taḻuvivarum kūttu. atu poruḷ, katai (yōṉi), talaimai (virutti), nilai (canti), cuvai, vakuppu (cāti), kuṟippu, viṟal (cattuvam), naḷinayam (apinayam), col, col vakai, vaṇṇam, vari, cētam eṉṉum patiṉāṉ kuṟuppuk kaḷaiyuṭaiyatu.
nāṭaka araṅku nalla keṭṭi nilattil, īraṭi nīḷamuḷḷa kōlāl, eṇkōl nīḷamum eḻukōl akalamum orukōl uyaramum uḷḷatāka amaikkappaṭṭu, mēlē mukaṭum, oru mukaveḻiṉi porumuka veḻiṉi karantuvara leḻiṉi eṉṉum mūvakait tirai kaḷum, pukuvāyil puṟappaṭuvāyil (Exit) eṉṉum iruvāyil kaḷum, uṭaiyatāyiruntatu.
tamiḻ  nāṭakamellām icaippāṭṭuḷḷavaiyē (Operas).
nāṭakam eṉpatu nāṭṭakka eṉṟum, araṅku araṅkam eṉpaṉa raṅka eṉṟum, vaṭamoḻiyil tiriyum.
ar - araṅku = aṟai, araṅku - araṅkam = nāṭaka mēṭai, nāṭakacālai, viḷaiyāṭiṭam, paṭaikkalam payilumiṭam, pōrkkaḷam, āṟṟiṭaik kuṟai, tiruvaraṅkam.
(3) maṭainūl 
maṭai = cōṟu, uṇavu. maṭainūl camaiyal nūl.
kantukak karuttum maṭainūṟ ceytiyum  (maṇi. 2 : 22).
eṉpatu tamiḻil maṭai nūliruntamaiyait terivikkum.
kāveri yūṭṭiya kavarkaṇait tūṇip
pūviri kaccaip pukaḻōṉ taṉmuṉ
paṉivarai mārpaṉ payanta nuṇporuṭ
paṉuvaliṉ vaḻāap palvē ṟaṭicil
eṉṟu ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭai (238 - 41) kūṟum vīmaṉ maṭainūl, tamiḻ maṭai nūlait taḻuviyatēyeṉpatu ataṉ piṉmai yālum, avaṉ pāṇṭiyar kuṭiyāṉa tiṅkaḷ marapiṉaṉāyirun tamaiyālum, aṟiyappaṭum. naḷaṉum tiṅkaḷ marapiṉaṉē.
(4) maruttuvam
tamiḻ maruttuvak kalai cittarāl vaḷarkkap peṟṟatu. ataṉāl atu citta maruttuvam eṉappeṟum. kaṭṭikaḷaiyum piḷavai kaḷaiyum karaippatum, oṭinta elumpai oṭṭavaip patum, mutiyavarai iḷaiñarākkuvatum, nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷ vāḻvippatum, citta maruttuvam.
mikiṉum kuṟaiyiṉum nōyceyyum nūlōr
vaḷimutalā eṇṇiya mūṉṟu  (kuṟaḷ. 941).
ātalāl, ūtai (vātam) pittam kōḻai eṉṉum mun nāṭiyaiyum nāṭi ataṉāl, iṟṟaik karuvikaḷaik koṇṭu talaiciṟanta tērcci peṟṟa mēlai maruttuvarum kaṇṭupiṭikka muṭiyāta.
nōynāṭi nōymutal nāṭi yatutaṇikkum
vāynāṭi vāyppac ceyal  (kuṟaḷ. 648).
citta maruttuvaṉiṉ teyvattiṟamām.
nuḷ - naḷ - nāḷam - nāḷi - nāḻi - nāṭi.
nāṭi pārkkum tiṟamillātāṉ maruntaṉēyaṉṟi maruttuvaṉā kāṉ. intiya maruttuvam citta maruttuvamē. āyurvētam eṉṉum āriya maruttuvam, citta maruttuvattiṉ vaṭanāṭṭu vakaiyē. cēravēntar iruntavarai citta maruttuva māyirunta malaiyāḷa maruttuvam, iṉṟu āriya maruttuvamāy māṟiyirut tal kāṇka. orē nōykkup pala maruntukaḷ uḷa. maruntukaḷ iṭantōṟum vēṟupaṭum. āyiṉ, maruttuvamuṟai oṉṟē.
āriya maruttuvam vērkaḷai mikutiyākak koṇṭa teṉṟum, citta maruttuvam centūrattai mikutiyākak koṇṭa teṉṟum, cilar kūṟuvar. avar aṟiyār. vērpār, taḻaipār, mella mellac centūrac cuṇṇam pār. eṉpatu citta maruttuvap paḻamoḻiyākum. āyiram pēraik koṉṟavaṉ arai maruttuvaṉ. eṉpatu āyiram vēraik koṉṟavaṉ arai maruttuvaṉ eṉṟum vaḻaṅkum. ciṟupañcamūlam, perumpañca mūlam eṉṉum īraintu vērkaḷum, tamiḻ nāṭṭilēyē viḷaiyum tamiḻ maruntuc carakkām. maruntu eṉṉum peyarē maṇamuḷḷa vēraiyum taḻaiyaiyumtāṉ kuṟikkum. maru = maṇam. maru - maruntu.
citta maruttuvattiṉ ciṟanta maruntu, mūvakai uppuc cērnta muppu eṉpatākum. atu irumpu mutaliyavaṟṟaip poṉṉākkavum ōrilakkam āṇṭukaḷ uyirvāḻac ceyyavum vallateṉa, ataṉ āṟṟalai uyarvunaviṟciyākak kūṟuvar.
neṭu nāḷirunta pērum, nilaiyākavēyiṉum kāyakaṟpan tēṭi neñcu puṇṇāvar eṉṟu tāyumāṉavar kūṟiyatu, muppai nōkkittāṉ pōlum!
aṟuvai (Surgery) muṟaiyum cittamaruttuvattiliruntamai
mīṉtēr koṭpiṟ paṉikkayam mūḻkic
ciralpeyarn taṉṉa neṭuveḷ ḷūci
neṭuvaci paranta vaṭuvāḻ mārpiṉ
ampucēr uṭampiṉar  (patiṟ. 42 : 2-5).
(kayam mūḻkic ciral peyarntaṉṉa neṭuveḷḷūci = kuḷattilē muḻukic ciccilik kuruvi eḻukiṉṟa kālattu ataṉ vāyalakaip pōla, puṇkaḷai nūlāltaikkum pōtu appuṇṇiṉ arattattil maṟainteḻukiṉṟa nīṇṭa veḷḷaiyāṉa ūci. neṭuvaci = nīṇṭa ūcit taḻumpu. vaṭu = kāyppu).
eṉṉum patiṟṟup pattaṭikaḷālum,
uṭaliṭait tōṉṟiṟ ṟoṉṟai yaṟuttataṉ utira muṟṟic
cuṭaluṟac cuṭṭu vēṟōr maruntiṉāl tuyaram tīrvar  (kamparā. 	
kumpa. 146)
ārār talaivaṇaṅkār ārārtām kaiyeṭār
ārārtām cattirattil āṟātār - cīrārum
teṉpuliyūr mēvum civaṉaruḷcēr ampaṭṭat
tampipukāṉ vācalilē tāṉ.
eṉṉum kampar pāṭṭukkaḷālum aṟiyappaṭum.
maruttuvattiṉ iṉṟiyamaiyāmai yaṟintē, paṇṭaikkālattil ūrmaruntu vaṉukku iṟaiyili nilam māṉiyamāka viṭappaṭṭatu. atu maruttuvappēṟu eṉappaṭṭatu. (S.I.1,ii. 43).
ulakiṟ ciṟanta citta maruttuvam, ūkkuvāriṉṟi nāḷukku nāḷ maṟaintum kuṟaintum varukiṉṟatu.
kuḻantai maruttuvam, pēṟukāla maruttuvam, aracamaruttuvam (vilakkamaṟṟatu), nañcu maruttuvam, māṭṭu maruttuvam eṉpaṉa, citta maruttuvattiṉ ciṟappuk kūṟukaḷākum. vilakkam pattiyam. (paṟṟiyam)
nīr, karukku (kaṣāyam) kuḻampu, ney allatu eṇṇey (kirutam), kaḷimpu, meḻuku (lēkiyam), kuḷikai, nīṟu (papam) mutaliya pala vaṭivilum citta maruntukaḷ uṇṭu. ovvoru nōykkum ovvoru puṟattai uracum māttiraik kaṭṭikaḷum uḷa.
kuḷikai - kuḷikā (va.) centūḷ - centūḷam - centūram - cintūram - sintūra (va).
maruttuvaṉukkup paṇṭitaṉ eṉṟum, maruttuvattiṟkup paṇṭitam eṉṟum, peyaruṇṭu. maruntu koṭuppavaṉ maruttuvaṉ; pala poruḷkaḷai aṟintavaṉ paṇṭitaṉ. paṇṭitaṉ eṉpatu paṇṭuvaṉ eṉṟum, paṇṭitam eṉpatu paṇṭuvam eṉṟum, maruvum.
paṇṭuvam (medical treatment) maruttuvap paṇṭuvam eṉṟum nampikkaip paṇṭuvam (faith cure) eṉṟum, irutiṟappaṭum. pāmpukkaṭi yuṇṭavaṉukkuk kaṭivāyil pāmpuṇik karuṅkal vaippatum, erukkam pūvait tiṉṉak koṭuppatum, mūkkiṟ paccilaiccāṟu piḻivatum, maruttuvap paṇṭuvam; mantirattiṉāl nañcai yiṟakkuvatu nampikkaip paṇṭuvam.
nampikkaip paṇṭuvam, iṟumpūtu (miracle), nērttikkaṭaṉ; kuḷicam (amulet), maṇi, pārvai, mantiram, ūḻkam (tiyāṉam), pāṇippu (pāvakam), muṭṭi, aracak kāṭci mutaliyaṉavākap palavakaippaṭum.
iyēcu perumāṉ toḻu (kuṭṭa) nōyāḷiyait toṭṭu nalappaṭuttiyatum, tiruñāṉa campantar kūṉpāṇṭiyaṉ curanōyait tīrttatum, iṟumpūtu. vaḻipaṭu teyvattainōkki, oṉṟu paṭaippatāka allatu ceyvatāka nērntukoḷvatu nērttik kaṭaṉ. mantira eḻuttuḷḷa takaṭṭaik kaiyiṟ kaṭṭik koḷvatu kuḷicam. uruttirākkam, tuḻāy (tuḷaci) maṇi, muttumālai mutaliya aṇikaḷ maṇiyākum. mantirikaṉ tēṭkoṭṭup paṭṭavaṉai allatu pāmpukkaṭiyuṇṭa vaṉaip pārttuk kuḻaiyaṭittu mantirippatu pārvai. nañcēṟi yavaṉē mantirattai ōtuvatu mantiram. pāmpukkaṭi yuṇṭavaṉ kaluḻaṉai (karuṭaṉai) uḷḷuvatu ūḻkam. avaṉ taṉṉaik kaluḻaṉākavē karutuvatu pāṇippu.
tiṭaṅkoḷ mantiram tiyāṉapā vakanilai muṭṭi  (periyapu. 34 : 1060).
maṇimantiramātiyāl vēṇṭu cittikaḷ  (tāyumā. paripū.9).
cēramāṉ karuvūrēṟiya oḷvāṭ kōpperuñcēralirum poṟaiyaik kaṇṭavuṭaṉ, nariverūuttalaiyār nalluṭampu peṟṟatāka, 5ām puṟappāṭṭiṉ koḷuk kūṟum. itu, kaṇṭa mālaiyai (scroula) aracaṉ toṭiṉ kuṇamākumeṉṟu karuti ataṟku aracaṉ tīṅku (king’s evil) eṉṟu āṅkilēyar peya riṭṭataṉoṭu oppunōkkat takkatu.
(5) maṇinōṭṭam
kāka pātamum kaḷaṅkamum vintuvum
ēkaiyum nīṅki iyalpiṟ kuṉṟā
nūlavar noṭitta nuḻainuṇ kōṭi
nālvakai varuṇattu nalaṅkēḻ oḷiyavum
ēkaiyum mālaiyum iruḷoṭu tuṟanta
pācār mēṉip pacuṅkatir oḷiyavum
patumamum nīlamum vintamum paṭitamum
vitimuṟai piḻaiyā viḷaṅkiya cātiyum
pūca uruviṉ polanteḷit taṉaiyavum
tītaṟu katiroḷit teṇmaṭ ṭuruvavum
iruḷteḷit taṉaiyavum iruvē ṟuruvavum
orumait tōṟṟat taivēṟu vaṉappiṉ
ilaṅkunīr viṭūum nalaṅkeḻu maṇikaḷum
kāṟṟiṉum maṇṇiṉum kalliṉum nīriṉum
tōṟṟiya kuṟṟam tukaḷaṟat tuṇintavum
cantira kuruvē aṅkā rakaṉeṉa
vanta nīrmaiya vaṭṭat tokutiyum
karuppat tuḷaiyavum kalliṭai muṭaṅkalum
tirukku nīṅkiya ceṅkoṭi valliyum
eṉṉum cilappatikārap pakuti (14:180-198), maṇikaḷiṉ kuṇaṅ kuṟṟaṅkaḷai eṭuttukkūṟutal kāṇka.
6. ōviyam
ōviyac cennūl urainūṟ kiṭakkaiyum
eṉpatu (maṇi 2 : 32), ōviyanūlait terivikkum.
māṭakkuc cittiramum eṉṉum naṉṉūṟ potuppāyirat toṭarum, cuvarai vaittukkoṇṭaṉṟō cittirameḻuta vēṇṭum? eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum, māṭaccuvarkaḷilellām akkālattil ōviyam varaiyap peṟṟiruntamaiyai aṟivikkum.
vampa mākkaḷ kampalai mūtūrc
cuṭumaṇ ōṅkiya neṭunilai maṉaitoṟum
maiyaṟu paṭivattu vāṉavar mutalā
evvakai uyirkaḷum uvamam kāṭṭi
veṇcutai viḷakkattu vittakar iyaṟṟiya
kaṇkavar ōviyam kaṇṭuniṟ kunarum
eṉṟu maṇimēkalai (3 : 126 - 131) kūṟutal kāṇka.
oruvaṉ ōr aracaṉatu avaikkaḷam ceṉṟu aṅku aracaṉ taṉ amaiccaruṭaṉ amarntiruntataik kaṇṭu, tāṉ koṇṭuvanta kāṇikkaiyai nīṭṭa aracaṉ atai vāṅkāmaiyāl neruṅkic ceṉṟu atu ōr ōviyamāyirukkak kaṇṭāṉeṉṟu, oru katai vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. attakaiya ōviyat tiṟavōr akkālat tiruntaṉar.
ōviyakkāraraik kaṇṇuḷ viṉaiñar eṉṟu cilappatikāram kūṟum (5 : 30). āṭai aṇikalam kaṭṭiṭam mutaliya ellāp poruḷum ōviya vēlaippāṭuḷḷaṉa vāyiruntaṉa.
(7) uruvam (Sculpture)
maṇṇāl uruvañ ceypavar maṇṇīṭṭāḷar  (maṇi. 28 : 37)
marattālum kallālum poṉvakaiyālum uruvañceypavar
kammiyar; cāntiṉāṟ ceypavar kottar.
kammiyanūl tolvaram pellai kaṇṭu  (tiruviḷai, tirunakaraṅ. 38).
kōyil tērum kōpuramum uruvaṅkaḷ niṟaintavai. pāvai yuruvamum pūtap paṭimaiyum pukārilum piṟa nakarkaḷilum iruntaṉa.
(8) kaṭṭiṭam
māṭamāḷikaikaḷum kūṭa kōpuraṅkaḷum maṇi maṇṭapaṅ kaḷum, mūvēntar talainakarkaḷilum kōnakarkaḷilum mikun tiruntaṉa.
māḷikai, kōpuram, maṇi, maṇṭapam eṉṉum nāṟcollum teṉcollē.
pakaivar varavu kāṇṭaṟkuk kōpuram ciṟanta amaippeṉṟu kaṇṭapiṉ, nakaraic cūḻnta kōṭṭai matililum, vāyiliṟ peritākavum maṟṟa iṭaṅkaḷiṟ ciṟiyaṉavākavum kōpuraṅkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. ciṟiyaṉa kottaḷam eṉappaṭṭaṉa.
maturai nakaraic cūḻnta matiliṉ nāṟpuṟattilum, vāyilum māṭamum vāṉaḷāviya kōpuramum iruntaṉa. nāṉku vāyil māṭaṅkaḷ iruntatiṉāl, maturai nāṉmāṭakkūṭal eṉappaṭṭatu. kūṭal eṉpatu tamiḻk kaḻakam. atu piṉpu iṭavaṉāku peyarāy maturaiyaik kuṟittatu. itaiyaṟiyātu, tolkataiñar (purāṇikar) oru kataiyaik kaṭṭiviṭṭaṉar.
tamiḻkeḻu kūṭal taṇkōl vēntē  (puṟam. 58 : 13)
tamiḻnilai peṟṟa tāṅkaru marapiṉ
 makiḻnaṉai maṟukiṉ maturai  (ciṟupāṇ. 66-7).
iṉi, kūṭalnakar eṉpatu nāḷaṭaivil kūṭal eṉak kuṟukiṟṟu eṉiṉumām.
maturai nakarvāyil, iṭaiviṭātu oḻukiya vaikaiyāṟu pōl akaṉṟum, iṭaiyaṟāta makkaḷ pōkkuvarattu mikuntum, iruntatu.
maḻaiyāṭu malaiyiṉ nivanta māṭamoṭu
vaiyai yaṉṉa vaḻakkuṭai vāyil
eṉṟu maturaikkāñci (355-6) kūṟutal kāṇka.
kōpura maṉṟi vācalai māṭamākavuñ camaittaliṉ, māṭameṉṟār. eṉṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar ciṟappurai iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkatu.
aracaṉukkuriya ciṟappukkaḷellām iṟaivaṉukkum ceyyap peṟṟatiṉāl, kōyil tēr mikap peritāyc ceyyappeṟṟatu pōl, kōyil matiṟ kōpuramum mikapperiya eḻunilai vāṉaḷāviyākak kaṭṭappeṟṟatu.  ataṉ amaippum tērai ottatākum. ataṉ eḻunilaikaḷum tēriṉ eḻutaṭṭukkaḷaip pōṉṟavai. eḻunilai allatu eḻutaṭṭuk karuttu ēḻulakam eṉṉum karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatu. ēḻulakak karuttum eḻutīvuk karuttiṉiṉṟu tōṉṟiyatākum.
ēḻuṭaiyāṉ poḻil  (tirukkōvai, 7)
taccuk kalaiyil kōyiltērpōl, kaṭṭiṭak kalaiyil kōyiṟkōpuram paṇṭait tamiḻariṉ aṟivaiyum āṟṟalaiyum ciṟappak kāṭṭum. tañcaip peruvuṭaiyār kōyiṟ kōpurat tiṟku, 10 kal tolaiviluḷḷa cārappaḷḷam eṉṉum iṭatti liruntu cāram kaṭṭiyatākavum, kaṭaikālil oḻukiya nīrttuḷai kaḷai aṭaittaṟkuk kuṟavai mīṉkaḷaip piṭittu viṭṭa tākavum, kūṟuvar.
taṇpuṉal paranta pūcal maṇmaṟuttu
mīṉiṟ ceṟukkum yāṇarp
payaṉtikaḻ vaippiṟpiṟar akaṉṟalai nāṭē
eṉpatu (puṟam : 7) tañcai nikaḻcciyai oruvāṟu niṉaivuṟuttum.
maṇi = 1. oḷikkal, 2. nīla oḷikkal.
ivviru poruḷkaḷuḷ, mutalāvatu maṇṇutal eṉṉum viṉaiyiṉiṉṟum, iraṇṭāvatu maḷkutal eṉṉum viṉaiyi ṉiṉṟum, tōṉṟiyavaiyākum. aḻaku eṉpatu ivviraṇṭi ṉiṉṟum tōṉṟiya vaḻipporuḷ.
maṇṇutal kaḻuvutal. maṇṇappaṭṭatu maṇṇi. maṇṇi - maṇi.
maṇṇi yaṟipa maṇinalam  (nāṉmaṇi. 5).
maṇṇuṟu maṇiyum  (peruṅ. 2-5  : 123)
maṇṭapaṅkaḷuḷ nūṟṟukkāl maṇṭapaṅkaḷum āyirakkāl maṇṭapaṅkaḷum iruntaṉa.
māṭaṅkaḷiṉ mukappil puliyuruvam amaikkappaṭṭum, pulittoṭar eṉṉum caṅkili toṅkaviṭappaṭṭum, iruntaṉa.
pulimuka māṭa malira vēṟi  (peruṅ. ilāvāṇa, 9, 69)
pulittoṭar viṭṭa puṉaimāṇ nallil  (mullaip. 62)
māṭa maturai (puṟam. 32), māṭamali maṟukiṟ kūṭal (muruku. 71), māṭam piṟaṅkiya malipukaḻk kūṭal (maturai. 429), nāṉ māṭak kūṭal makaḷirum maintarum (kalit. 92), matimali puricai māṭak kūṭal (tirumukap paracuram).
kōnakarkaḷil aṅkaṇa nīraik kaṇṇiṟ paṭāmaṟ celuttu taṟku, karantupaṭai eṉṉum putaicālakam iruntatu. atu teru naṭuviṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭu yāṉaik kūṭṭam mēṟcellumpaṭi, karuṅkallāl mūṭappaṭṭiruntatu. atiṟceṉṟa nīr yāṉait tutikkai pōṉṟa tūmpiṉ vāyilāy akaḻiyil viḻuntatu.
māṭaṅkaḷ maturaiyil mikuntum ciṟantum iruntatākat terikiṉṟatu.
peruṅkai yāṉai yiṉanirai peyarum
curuṅkai vīti  (cilap. 14 : 64 -5).
kōnakarkaḷil, ūraic cuṟṟik kōṭṭai matil iruntatu. atu puricai eṉappaṭṭatu. purital vaḷaital. puricaiyuḷḷa nakarppeyarkaḷē mutalil puri eṉṉum īṟu peṟṟaṉa. kōṭṭai eṉpatum vaḷaitaṟ poruḷatē. kōṭutal vaḷaital.
uyarvakalam tiṇmai arumaiin nāṉkiṉ
amaivaraṇ eṉṟuraikkum nūl.
eṉṉum tirukkuṟaṭkēṟpa, (743) matilaraṇ matil, eyil, iñci, cō eṉa nālvakaippaṭṭiruntatu.
mika uyaramāṉatu matil; ñāyil eṉṉum ēvaṟaikaḷaiyuṭaiyatu eyil; cempai yurukkic cāntāka vārttuk karuṅkallāṟ kaṭṭiyatu iñci; ariya poṟikaḷai yuṭaiyatu cō.
vāṉuṭkum vaṭinīḷa matil  (puṟam. 18)
ey il (ēvaṟaikaḷ) uḷḷatu eyil.
cempupuṉain tiyaṟṟiya cēṇeṭum puricai
 uvarā vīkait tuvarai  (puṟam. 201)
cempiṭṭuc ceyta iñcit tirunakarc celvam tēṟi  (kamparā. 	
kumpa. 160).
cōvaraṇum pōrmaṭiyat tollilaṅkai kaṭṭaḻitta  (cilap. 17 : 35).
cō eṉpatu araṇvakaiyē yaṉṟic cōṇitapuram eṉṉum nakarp peyaraṉṟu.
pāmpuri, kottaḷam (bastion), vāyiṟ kōpuram pataṇam (rampart), ñāyil mutaliya pala vuṟuppukkaḷai yuṭaiyatu kōṭṭai matil. cila nakarkaḷil ēḻeyilkaḷ iruntaṉa. puṟamatilaic cuṟṟi akaḻi iruntatu.
kōnakarkaḷaip pakaiyaracar muṟṟukaiyiṭṭa kālattil maṟaintōṭit tappik koḷḷa, neṭuntolaiviṟkuc curaṅkam allatu curuṅkaiyeṉṉum kīḻ nilavaḻikaḷum iruntaṉa.
kōṭṭai - kōṭṭa (va.) L. castrum E. caster, chester (suffixes of place names)
òu« - òu (t)., OE. burg, burh OS. burg, OHG burug ON. burg, Goth. baurgs, E. borough, Sc. burgh. òÇ - òß (t.), E. bury (sfx. of place - name. pāḻi = nakar Gk. polis.
(9) poṟiviṉai (Machinery)
vēntaṉ taṉ urimaic cuṟṟattōṭu iṉpamāy nīrāṭuvataṟku, vēṇṭumpōtu nīrai nirappavum vaṭikkavum nīrppoṟiyamainta kuḷam iruntatu. atu ilavantikai yeṉappaṭṭatu.
ilavan tikaiyiṉ eyiṟpuṟam pōki  (cilap. 10 : 31)
niṟaikkuṟiṉ niṟaittup pōkkuṟiṉ pōkkum
poṟippaṭai yamainta poṅkila vantikai  (peruṅ. 1 : 40; 311-2)
muḻumutalaraṇam (puṟat. 10) eṉṟum, varupakai pēṇār āreyil (puṟat. 12) eṉṟum, tolkāppiyam kūṟuvatu, miḷai yum (kāvaṟkāṭum) akaḻiyum cūḻntu palvēṟu poṟikaḷaik koṇṭa cōvaraṇaiyē.
(10) poṉnūl
cāta rūpam kiḷicciṟai āṭakam
cāmpū natameṉa ōṅkiya koḷkai
eṉpatu (cilap. 14 : 201-2), poṉṉiṉ vakaikaḷaik kāṭṭum.
jāta rūppa eṉṉum vaṭacol polinta vaṭivam eṉṟum, hāṭṭaka eṉṉum vaṭacol oḷirvatu eṉṟum poruḷpaṭum. ivaṟṟaip poṉ eṉṟum taṅkam eṉṟum collalām. jampūnata eṉṉum vaṭacol, poṉ (mēru) malaikku vaṭakkil nāvaṟ paḻaccāṟu perukiyōṭuvatāyk karutappaṭṭa āṟṟiṉ peyar. itu āriyat tolkataik koḷkai. itaṉpaṭi, cāmpū natattai nāvalāṟai eṉṟu collal vēṇṭum.
poṉ eṉpatu, poṉ pōṉṟa piṟa kaṉiyapporuḷkaḷaiyuṅ kuṟikkum.
veḷḷi, cempu, irumpu, īyam mutaliya piṟa kaṉiyaṅkaḷum paṇṭait tamiḻarkkut terintiruntaṉa. īyam eṉpatu iḷakuvatu eṉṉum poruḷatu.
īyam - sīsa (va.) iḷ - (iy) - ī - īyam.
o. nō : eḷ - ey. eyttal iḷaittal.
veṇkalam tamiḻar, mutaṉ mutal amaitta kalavaik kaṉiyamākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu. ataṉ oru coṟpeyar uṟai, muṟi eṉpaṉa.
(11) itaḷ māṟṟiyam
cittar itaḷiṉāl (pātaracattāl) tāḻnta kaṉiyaṅkaḷai (ulōkaṅkaḷai) veḷḷiyākavum ciṟappākap poṉṉākavum māṟṟiṉatāka, maruttuva nūlkaḷ kūṟum. ap poṉṉākkam vaṭamoḻiyil (i)racavātam eṉappaṭum. akkalaiyaik kuṟikkum alchemy eṉṉum colliliruntu chemistry eṉṉum col tōṉṟiyiruttalāl, kemiya nūlai rasāyaṉam eṉṟaṉar. immuṟaiyil atait tamiḻil itaḷiyam eṉalām.
poṉṉākkam ekipatu (Egypt) nāṭṭil vaḻaṅkiyatāl, akkalai arapiyil alkimiya (al-kimia) eṉappaṭṭateṉṟum, al eṉpatu anta eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum cuṭṭuc col leṉṟum, kimiya eṉpatu ekipatu nāṭṭiṉ peyareṉṟum, erutantuṟai āṅkilac curukka akaramutali kūṟum. (The Concise Oxford Dictionary of Current English).
aḷakēcaṉ nikarāka ampoṉ mika vaitta pērum nēcittu racavāta vittaik kalaintiṭuvar eṉṟu tāyumāṉavar pāṭuvatāl (paripūra. 10), itaḷ māṟṟiyak kalai tamiḻ nāṭṭil iruntamai aṟiyappaṭum.
potuvāka, itu cittar kalaiyeṉappaṭum. irāmaliṅka aṭikaḷ citta nilaiyaṭaintiruntatiṉāl ikkalaiyai aṟintiruntaṉar.
(12) maṟa nūl
taṉimakkaḷ pōr, paṭaimakkaḷ pōr eṉap pōr iru tiṟappaṭum. cilampam, maṟpōr, kuttuc caṇṭai, vāṭpōr mutaliyaṉa taṉi makkaḷ pōrām.
mukkāval nāṭṭu āmūrmallaṉaip porutu koṉṟa kōpperunaṟkiḷḷi, oru pōravai naṭatti vantāṉ; ataṉāl, pōravaik kōpperu naṟkiḷḷi eṉappaṭṭāṉ. avaṉ naṭatti vantatu maṟpayiṟcik kaḷari. maṟpōril, cila uyirnāṭiyāṉa narampukaḷait toṭṭu, etiriyai vīḻttik kollavum, vīḻntavaṉai mūṉṟē mukkāl nāḻikaikkuḷ eḻacceyyavum, cila marumap piṭikaḷ uḷa.
maṟkaḷari, viṟkaḷari, vāṭkaḷari eṉap pōravai allatu muraṇkaḷari palavakaip paṭum.
iḷampal kōcar viḷaṅkupaṭai kaṉmār,
ikaliṉar eṟinta akalilai murukkiṉ
perumarak kampam  (puṟam. 169 : 9 -11)
eṉpatiliruntu, eypaṭai eṟipaṭai mutaliya paṭaikkalap payiṟci naṭaipeṟṟa vakaiyai aṟiyalām.
kutiraip paṭaip payiṟci naṭaipeṟṟa kaḷam ceṇṭu veḷi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
kari pari tēr kāl ākiya nālvakaip paṭaippōraiyum paṟṟiya tamiḻ nūlkaḷ iṟantupaṭṭaṉa.
(13) ōka nūl
ōvutal = ottal, oṉṟutal. ōvu - ōku. ōvu - ōvam - ōkam = aṟivaṉ uḷattil iṟaivaṉōṭu oṉṟum ūḻkam (tiyāṉam).
ukam eṉpatu vaṭamoḻiyil yuka eṉṟum utti eṉpatu yukti eṉṟum āyatupōl, ōkam eṉpatum ammoḻiyil yōka eṉṟākum. immuṟai paṟṟi ōku eṉpatu yōku eṉappaṭum.
ōkap payiṟci eṇṇuṟuppukkaḷai yuṭaiyatu. avai oḻukkam (iyamam), oḻuṅku (niyamam), irukkai (ācaṉam), vaḷinilai (pirāṇāyāmam), pulaṉaṭakkam (pirāttiyākāram), niṟai (tāraṇai), ūḻkam (tiyāṉam), oṭukkam (camāti) eṉpaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ irukkaiyum vaḷinilaiyum uṭaṟpayiṟci; ēṉaiya uḷap payiṟci.
piṟappāl tammai uyarvākak karutum pētaimaiyum cerukkumuḷḷōr, ōkappayiṟci ceyvatu muyaṟkompām. iṭaikalai, piṉkalai, cuḻimuṉai eṉpaṉa vaḷinilait toṭarpāṉa nāṭikaḷaik kuṟikkum teṉcoṟkaḷ. ivaṟṟai iṭākalā, piṅkalā, suṣumuṉā eṉat tirittuḷḷaṉar vaṭamoḻiyāḷar.
ōka nūliṟ kūṟappaṭum aṟu nilaikkaḷaṅkaḷ allatu narappup piṉṉalkaḷ aṭimutal muṭivarai, muṟaiyē, aṇṭi kuṟiyiṭai nālitaḻt tāmarai vaṭivilum, aṇṭi koppūḻiṭai āṟitaḻt tāmarai vaṭivilum, koppūḻ maṇṭalattil pattitaḻt tāmarai vaṭivilum, neñcāṅkulai maṇṭalattil paṉṉīritaḻt tāmarai vaṭivilum, aṭinā maṇṭalattil patiṉāṟitaḻt tāmarai vaṭivilum, iru puruvattiṭai īritaḻt tāmarai vaṭivilum, iruppatākac collap peṟum.
ōka nūl iṟantupaṭṭatāl aṟunilaikkaḷa (ṣaṭātāra)p peyar kaḷum iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. irukkaikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷum vaṭamoḻi yiṟ palavāṟu tirikkappaṭṭum moḻi peyarkkappaṭṭum uḷa. āsaṉam eṉṉum collē teṉ colliṉ tiripu.
(14) māyam (Conjury)
itu mālam allatu kaṇ kaṭṭu.
(15) vaciyam (Enchantment)
itu makaḷiraiyum piṟaraiyum maruntālum mantirattālum maṉap payiṟciyālum vayappaṭuttal.
vayiṉ - vayam - vacam - vaci - vaciyam.
(16) mantirak kaṭṭu
itu tī kāṟṟu mutaliya iyaṟkaip pūtaṅkaḷaiyum, puli nāy pāmpu mutaliya uyirikaḷaiyum māntaraiyum, iyaṅkātavāṟum tīṅku ceyyātavāṟum, mantirattāl taṭuttal.
karaṭi vempuli vāyaiyum kaṭṭalām  (tāyu. tēcō. 8)
(17) makiṭi (mōṭi)
itu mantirattāṟ poruḷkaḷai maṟaittalum avaṟṟai eṭuttalumākum.
(18) pēyōṭṭal (Exorcism)
palvakaip pēykaḷaiyum kōṭaṅki allatu uṭukkaṭittu, pēy  kōṭpaṭṭāriṉiṉṟu ōṭṭutal pēyōṭṭal ākum.
(19) kuṟaḷi
itu kuṭṭip pēyāl ciṟu kuṟumpukaḷ ceyvittal.
(20) ceyviṉai (Sorcery or Witchcraft) 
itu pēyai āḷum mantirakkāraṉaik koṇṭu, vēṇṭāta varkku nōyum cākkāṭum varuvittal.
itu cūṉiyam eṉṟum ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅkum. cul - cuṉ - cuṉṉam - cūṉya (va.).
(21) karavaṭa nūl
itu kaḷavu nūl.
mantiram teyvam maruntē nimittam
tantiram iṭaṉē kālam karuviyeṉ
ṟeṭṭuṭa ṉaṉṟē iḻukkuṭai marapil
kaṭṭuṇ mākkaḷ tuṇaiyeṉat tirivatu.
eṉpatu (cilap. 16 : 166 - 9), karavaṭa nūliṉ kūṟukaḷait terivikkum. nimittam tantiram eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkaṭku, puḷ viraku eṉpaṉa muṟaiyē nikar teṉcoṟkaḷām.
(22) uṭal nūl
meyp poruḷ toṇṇūṟṟāṟeṉum paṭṭāṅku nūl muṟaiyilum, maruttuva muṟaiyilum, pulāluṇavu muṟaiyilum, maṟpōr muṟaiyilum, uṭaṟkūṟukaḷaip paṇṭait tamiḻar naṉkaṟintiruntaṉar.
(23) kāval nūl
nūlvaḻip piḻaiyā nuṇaṅkunuṇ tērcci
 ūrkāp pāḷar
eṉṉum maturaik kāñciyaṭikaḷaiyum (646-7), avaṟṟiṉ kaḷavu kāṇṭaṟkum kāttaṟkum kūṟiya nūlkaḷ vaḻip pōvār eṉṉum uraiyaiyum, nōkkuka.
15 aṟiviyalkaḷ (Sciences)
perumpālum paṭippiṉāṟ kaṟkappaṭuvaṉa aṟiviyalkaḷ.
(1) ilakkaṇam
ilakku - ilakkiyam. ilakku - ilakkaṇam. ilakku = kuṟi, kuṟikkōḷ. ciṟanta vāḻkkaik kuṟikkōḷāṉa aṟattai eṭuttuk kāṭṭuvatu ilakkiyam. ciṟanta moḻik kuṟikkōḷāṉa amaippai eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu ilakkaṇam.
uḷḷuṟai teyvam oḻintatai nilaṉeṉak
koḷḷum eṉpa kuṟiyaṟin tōrē
eṉṉum tolkāppiya nūṟpā (akat. 47) ilakkaṇattaik kuṟiyeṉak kuṟittal kāṇka.
ilakkaṇattiṟku aṇaṅkam eṉṟum, ilakkiyattiṟku aṇaṅkiyam eṉṟum peyaruṇṭu.
ilakkaṇam ilakkiyam eṉṉum iru coṟkaḷum toṉṟu toṭṭē tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa.
tamiḻ ilakkaṇam, eḻuttu, col, poruḷ eṉa muttiṟappaṭum. poruḷil yāppum yāppil aṇiyum aṭaṅkum. paṇṭai yilakkiyamellām ceyyuḷil iruntatiṉāl, urainaṭaik keṉac ciṟappāy ilakkaṇam vakukkappaṭavillai. colliṟkē poruḷiruppatāl, colliṟkup piṉpu poruḷaiyē ilak kaṇap pakutiyāka eṭuttukkoṇṭaṉar. poruṭ pakutiyiṉṟit tamiḻilakkaṇam niṟaivuḷḷatākātu.
tamiḻ eḻuttu kīṟeḻuttum veṭṭeḻuttum eṉa iruvakaip paṭṭatu. muṉṉatu ōlaiyeḻuttu; piṉṉatu paṭṭaya veḻuttu. paṭṭaya veḻuttaiyē  vaṭṭeḻutteṉpar.
iṉṟu acciluḷḷa ōlaiyeḻuttu toṉṟu toṭṭu varuvatē.
tollai vaṭiviṉa ellā eḻuttumāṇ
ṭeytum ekara okaramey puḷḷi.
eṉṟu 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭu naṉṉūl (98) kūṟutal kāṇka. āṇṭu eṉṟatu muṟkālattai. ekara okaram puḷḷi peṟṟatē muṟkāla eḻuttiṉ vēṟupāṭeṉpatu naṉṉūlār karuttu. avar kālattil ekara okaram puḷḷi peṟavillai; peṟṟirukkumāyiṉ atai ēṉ kūṟa vēṇṭum? ātalāl, atu uraiyaṉmai aṟika. `akkālam eṉpatu iṉṟum muṟkālattaik kuṟittal kāṇka.
eḻuttāṇi koṇṭu ēṭṭil kīṟiyeḻutuvataṟku vaḷai kōṭ ṭeḻuttum, uḷikoṇṭu paṭṭayattil kuḻitteḻutuvataṟku nērkōṭṭeḻuttumē, ēṟṟatātal kāṇka.
intiya āriyarkku mutalil eḻuttillai. avar maṟai eḻutāk kiḷavi yeṉappaṭṭatu. vētakkālap pirāmaṇar tamiḻnāṭu vantapiṉ, vaṭamoḻiyil nūleḻutat tamiḻeḻuttai yoṭṭik kiranta veḻuttai amaittuk koṇṭaṉar. ataṉpiṉ, ki.pi. 10ām allatu 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil tēvanākari tōṉṟiṟṟu.
aṟiyappaṭṭa camaṟkiruta mutaṟ paḻaṅkalveṭṭu, kattiyavāril cuṉākar eṉṉumiṭattil oru pāṟai mēl uḷatu. atu uruttiratāmaṉ kalveṭṭeṉa vaḻaṅkivarukiṉṟatu. ataṉ kālam ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭu. atu nākariyil illai; paḻaiya kal veṭṭeḻuttil uḷatu. ēṟattāḻak ki.pi. 2ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ kuriya pavar kaiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷ, nākariyai nōkkiya mikunta muṉṉēṟṟattaik kāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa; atē camaiyattil tantiturukkaṉiṉ ki.pi. 750ām āṇṭuk kalveṭṭu, iṉṟu vaḻakkiluḷḷa nākariyaip peritum otta kuṟikaḷiṉ muḻut tokutiyaiyum koṇṭuḷḷatu. eṉiṉum, uṇmaiyāṉa iṟṟai nākariyiluḷḷa mutaṟ kalveṭṭu, ki.pi. 11ām nūṟṟāṇ ṭiṟku muntiyataṉṟeṉpatu kavaṉikkattakkatu. (khÅa® cšÈa«R rk‰»Uj - M§»y mfuKjÈ - K‹Diu, g¡., XXVIII, aṭikkuṟippu).
tēvanākariyum tamiḻeḻuttaip piṉpaṟṟiyatē eṉpatu, kūrntu nōkkuvārkkup pulaṉākum.
poruḷilakkaṇam tamiḻilaṉṟi vēṟem moḻiyilumillai.
tamiḻar iṉṉicaik kalaiyilum nāṭakakkalaiyilum ciṟantirunta tiṉāl, moḻiyoṭu avviru kalaikaḷaiyum cērttu, tamiḻai iyalicai nāṭakameṉa muttamiḻāy vaḻaṅkiṉar, ittakaiya moḻiyamaippum vēṟeṅkaṇumillai.
tamiḻar iyalumiṭamellām tam viṉaikaḷai icaiyoṭu ceytu vantaṉar eṉpatu, tālāṭṭup pāṭṭu, ērmaṅkalap pāṭṭu, naṭavaip pāṭṭu, mukavaip pāṭṭu, ēṟṟap pāṭṭu, ēlap pāṭṭu, vaḷḷaip pāṭṭu, kaḻiyaṟ pāṭṭu, kummip pāṭṭu, kōlāṭṭappāṭṭu, ūñcaṟ pāṭṭu, vaḻinaṭaic cintu, oppu (oppāri)p pāṭṭu mutaliyavaṟṟāl aṟiyappaṭum.
mutaliru kaḻakaṅkaḷilum irunta ilakkaṇamellām muttami ḻilakkaṇaṅkaḷē. iyaṟṟamiḻ ilakkaṇam piṇṭam eṉṟum, muttamiḻilakkaṇam māpiṇṭam eṉṟum kūṟappeṟum.
poruḷilakkaṇam poruḷkaḷai akampuṟam eṉa iraṇṭāka vakuttu, kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyar kātaliṉpattai akam eṉac ciṟappittu, maṟṟellāvaṟṟaiyum puṟattuḷ aṭakkum. puṟattuḷ pōrttuṟai mikac ciṟappākak koḷḷappaṭṭu, maṟṟavaiyel lām eḻupuṟap poruḷ tiṇaikaḷuḷ āṟāvatāṉa vākait tiṇaiyuḷ aṭakkappeṟum. 
(2) moḻi nūl
moḻi nūṟku vittūṉṟiyavar tamiḻarē. tamiḻc coṟkaḷai, ilakkaṇa vakaic col mūṉṟum poruḷ vakaic col oṉṟum coṟpiṟappiyal vakaic col mūṉṟumāka vakuttiruntaṉar.
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē.  (tol. peyar.1)
colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉaieṉṟu
āyiraṇ ṭeṉpa aṟintici ṉōrē.  (tol peyar. 4)
iṭaiccol kiḷaviyum uriccol kiḷaviyum
avaṟṟuvaḻi maruṅkil tōṉṟum eṉpa.  (tol peyar. 5)
iyaṟcol tiricol ticaiccol eṉa mūṉṟiyalum ceyyuḷ īṭṭac collē.
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticaiccoṟ kiḷavi.  (tol. ecca. 4)
annāṟ collum toṭukkuṅ kālai
valikkumvaḻi valittalum melikkumvaḻi melittalum
virikkumvaḻi virittalum tokukkumvaḻit tokuttalum
nīṭṭumvaḻi nīṭṭalum kuṟukkumvaḻik kuṟukkalum
nāṭṭal vaḻiya eṉmaṉār pulavar.  (tol. ecca. 7)
innūṟpākkaḷum, mutaṉilai, iṭainilai, īṟu, urupu, puṇarcci, cāriyai mutaliya colluṟuppukkaḷum, paṇṭait tamiḻariṉ moḻi nūlaṟivaik kāṭṭum.
iyaṟcol eṉpatu vērc collum aṭiccollumāṉa iyalpāṉa col (primitive word). tiricol eṉpatu atiṉiṉṟu tirinta col (derivative word). ivai yiraṇṭum centamiḻc col. ticaic col eṉpatu koṭuntamiḻc col. ak kālattil vaṭacollum piṟa ayaṟcollum tamiḻiṟ kalakka villai.
(3) aṟanūl
itu poruḷkaḷai aṟam poruḷ iṉpam vīṭu eṉa nāṉkākap pakuttu, nalloḻukkattaik kūṟum.
(4) poruḷ nūl
itu ellārum tattam toḻilāṟ poruḷīṭṭuvataṟku, iṉṟiyamaiyāta pātukāppuc ceyyum araciyalaip paṟṟik kūṟum.
(5) iṉpa nūl
itu āṭavar peṇṭir kāmaviṉpattaic ciṟappittuk kūṟum. iṉpa vāḻkkaiyaik kūṟuvatu akapporuḷ nūl eṉṟum iṉpat tuyppai maṭṭum kūṟuvatu kāmanūl allatu vēṇūl eṉṟum, peyar peṟum.
(6) maṟai nūl (Theology)
itu perumpālum camayak kuṭumikaḷai allatu koṇmuṭipu kaḷaik kūṟum. itu mantiram eṉavum vāymoḻi eṉavum paṭum. maṉṉum tiram (tiṟam) mantiram. maṉṉutal = karututal, eṇṇutal. itu uṇmaiyākum eṉṟu tiṇmaiyāy eṇṇic colvatu mantiram.
pāṭṭurai nūlē vāymoḻi piciyē  (tol.cey. 78)
niṟaimoḻi māntar āṇaiyiṟ kiḷanta
maṟaimoḻi tāṉē mantiram eṉpa.  (tol.cey.176)
muṉṉutal karututal. muṉṉam = maṉam. muṉ - maṉ - maṉam. iṉi, muṉṉam - muṉam - maṉam eṉṟumām.
mantiram camayak koḷkai paṟṟiyatum cāvippu vāḻttup paṟṟiyatum eṉa iru vakaippaṭum.
camayak koḷkai paṟṟiyatum, kaṭavuḷ vaḻuttu, koṇmuṭipu nūl eṉa iru tiṟappaṭum. nammāḻvār aruḷic ceyta tiruvāymoḻi kaṭavuḷ vaḻuttu; tirumūlar aruḷic ceyta tirumantiram koṇmuṭipu (cittānta) nūl.
niṟaimoḻi māntar perumai nilattu
maṟaimoḻi kāṭṭi viṭum.
eṉṟatu (kuṟaḷ. 28) ittakaiya nūlkaḷai nōkkiyē.
cāvippu vāḻttu moḻikaḷai mantiram eṉpatu ulaka vaḻakku; nūl vaḻakkaṉṟu.
(7) paṭṭāṅku nūl (Philosophy)
paṭṭāṅku uṇmai. paṭṭāṅku nūl meypporuḷ nūl. māntaṉ uṭalamaippup paṟṟit tamiḻar kaṇṭa meyp poruḷkaḷ (tattuvaṅkaḷ), 96. avai ātaṉ (āṉma) meyp poruḷ 24, nāṭi 10, nilai (avattai) 5, malam 3, kuṇam 3, maṇṭalam 3, piṇi 3, tiripu (vikāram) 8, nilaikkaḷam (ātāram) 6, tātu 7, ūtai (vāyu) 10, uṟai (kōcam) 5, vāyil 9, eṉpaṉa.
ātaṉ meyp poruḷ 24 āvaṉa : - pūtam 5, pulaṉ 5, aṟivup pulaṉ 5, karumappulaṉ 5, karaṇam 4.
ellāp poruḷkaḷum aimpūtamāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatum; āṇṭavaṉ (pati), ātaṉ (pacu), ācu (pācam) eṉa mūṉṟāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatum; uyir, mey (uṭampu) eṉa iraṇṭāy aṭaṅkum eṉpatum; tamiḻariṉ vēṟupaṭṭa koḷkaikaḷām.
ātaṉukku iṟaivaṉōṭuḷḷa toṭarpumuṟai irumai (tuvaitam), oṉṟiya irumai (viciṣṭāttuvaitam) eṉa iruvakaiyākavē, paṇṭait tamiḻarāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. orumai (attuvaitam) tamiḻar koḷkaiyaṉṟu.
tamiḻilakkaṇa mutaṉūl muṉivaṉāl iyaṟṟappeṟṟatiṉāl, uyir, mey, uyirmey, kuṟil, neṭil, valliṉam, melliṉam, iṭaiyiṉam, puṇarcci, mutaliya eḻuttilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷum; peyar, viṉaimutal, eṇ vēṟṟumai, viṉai, viṉaimuṟṟu, viṉaiyeccam, iṟantakāla viṉai, nikaḻkāla viṉai, etirkāla viṉai mutaliya collilakkaṇak kuṟiyīṭukaḷum; meypporuḷ nūṟkaruttum taḻuvumāṟu amaikkap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
(8) aḷavai nūl
itu poruḷkaḷiṉ uṇmaiyai aṟitaṟku ētāṉa (ētuvāṉa) aḷavaikaḷai (piramāṇaṅkaḷai) eṭuttuk kūṟuvatu. aḷavaikaḷ mottam pattu. avaṟṟuḷ mutaṉmaiyāṉavai kāṭci karuttu oppu urai eṉṉum nāṉku. karutteṉpatu uyttuṇarvu.
(9) ēraṇam (Logic)
itu aḷavaikaḷaik koṇṭu poruḷkaḷiṉ uṇmaiyai aṟiyum vakaikaḷai eṭuttut kūṟuvatu.
vaṭivēl ceṭṭiyār patippitta tarkka paripāṣai eṉṉum nūliṟutiyil, akattiyar pērāl 20 tarukka nūṟpākkaḷ uḷa. avaṟṟuḷ mutalatu.
poruḷkuṇam karumam potucciṟap poṟṟumai
iṉmai yuṭaṉporuḷ ēḻeṉa moḻipa.
eṉpatu. itu poruḷkaḷai ēḻākap pakuppatu. itaiyē piṟkālattil vaṭavar vaicēṭikam eṉṟaṉar.
ēraṇam uruvam yōkam icaikaṇak kiratam cālam  (taṉippāṭal). 
ēraṇaṅkāṇ eṉpar eṇṇar   (tirukkōvaic ciṟappuppāyiram)
(9) vāṉa nūl (Astronomy)
vāṉattiluḷḷa nāḷ (naṭcattiram), kōḷ (kirakam), ōrai (irāci) mutaliyavaṟṟai viḷakkik kūṟuvatu vāṉa nūl. irupatteḻu nāṭkaḷum eḻu kōḷkaḷum paṉṉīrōraikaḷum kumarik kaṇṭat tamiḻar kaṇṭiruntaṉar.
eḻu kōḷkaḷiṉ peyarāl eḻu nāṭ kiḻamaiyai mutaṉ mutal  ēṟpaṭuttiyavar tamiḻarē. atu piṉṉar ulaka muḻuvatum paraviyuḷḷatu.
koḷ - kōḷ. koḷḷutal vaḷaital. koḷ - koṭku. koṭkutal = cuḻalutal, cuṟṟi varutal. koṭpatu kōḷ. kōḷkaḷ vāṉattiṟ cuḻaṉṟu cuṟṟivarum tōṟṟattāl ap peyar peṟṟaṉa. irāku kētu eṉpaṉa cāyaikaḷākavē koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. niṟam paṟṟi avai aṇivakaiyil karumpāmpu cempāmpu eṉappaṭṭaṉa. iruttal karuttal. ir - irā - irāku. cē - cētu = civappu. cētāmpal cevvāmpal. cētu - kētu. o. nō: cempu - kempu.

	veḷḷi	cukkiraṉ

vaṭamoḻik kiḻamaip peyarkaḷuḷ, maṅkaḷa vāram, caṉivāram ākiya iraṇṭu tavira maṟṟavaiyellām nēr moḻi peyarppāyiruttal kāṇka.
āṅkilap peyarkaḷuḷ, piṉ aintum piṟkālattil māṟiviṭṭaṉa. āyiṉum, eḻunāḷ eṉṉum kāla aḷavu māṟātiruttal kāṇka.
cevvāy cenniṟamuḷḷateṉṟum, viyāḻaṉ poṉṉiṟamuḷḷa teṉṟum periyateṉṟum, veḷḷi veṇmaiyāṉa teṉṟum, kāri kariyateṉṟum, kaṇṭaṟintatu viyakkat takka ceytiyām.
viyal = 1. perumai. mūḻttiṟutta viyaṉṟāṉai (patiṟ. 33, 5). 
2. akalam viyaleṉ kiḷavi akalap poruṭṭē (tol.col. 354). 3. mikuti. (cilap. 3, 7, urai).
viyal - viyalaṉ - viyāḻaṉ.
viḷḷutal = virital, malartal. viḷ - viy - viyal.
pirukapati eṉṉum vaṭacol viyāḻaṉ eṉpataṉ moḻipeyarppā yiruppatōṭu, peruku eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ tiripai nilaimoḻi mutaṉilaiyākak koṇṭiruppatu kavaṉikkat takkatu.
paṉṉiru ōraikaḷ
	tamiḻ	vaṭamoḻi	ilattīṉ
	mēḻam	mēṣam	Aries
	viṭai	riṣapam	Taurus
	iraṭṭai	mituṉam	Gemini
	alavaṉ	karkkaṭakam	Cancer
	āḷi	cimmam	Leo
	kaṉṉi	kaṉṉi	Virgo
	tulai	tulā	Libra
	naḷi	viruccikam	Scorpio
	cilai	cāpam (taṉucu)	Sagitarius
	cuṟavam	makaram	(Capricornus)
	kumpam	kumpam	Aquarius
	mīṉam	mīṉam	Pisces
vaṭamoḻiyil ellāp peyarkaḷum, ilattīṉil patiṉoru peyarkaḷum, tamiḻc coṟkaḷiṉ nērmoḻipeyarppā yiruttal kāṇka.
muḻuttal, tiraḷutal. muḻutta āṇ piḷḷai eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. muḻutu, mottam. muḻu - muḻā = tiraṇṭa muracu. muḻā - muḻavu - muḻavam. muḻā - miḻā = tiraṇṭa māṉ (Stag). miḻā - mēḻam = tiraṇṭa cemmaṟiyāṭṭuk kaṭā. mēḻam - mēṣa (va.) mēḻam - mēḻakam - ēḻakam - ēṭakam - ēṭu - yāṭu - āṭu.
tullutal = poruntutal, ottal. tul - tuṉ, tuṉṉutal poruntutal. tul - tulai = oppu. iru puṟamum otta niṟaikōl. tul - tulā = tulai pōṉṟa ēṟṟam. tulā - tulām - tulāṉ = oru niṟai. o. nō :  oppu = tulai. opparāvi = tulai ceyvōṉ. kummutal, kuvital. kum - kumpu - kumpam - kumpa (va.). miṉ - mīṉ - mīṉam - mīṉa (va.).
paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭil paṉṉī rōraip peyarkaḷē paṉṉiru mātap peyarkaḷāka vaḻaṅki vantaṉa.
mati - mātam - māsa (va.). ovvoru piṟai nilaiyum pakkam eṉappaṭṭatu. vaḷarpiṟai veṇpakkam eṉṟum, tēypiṟai karumpakkam eṉṟum collappaṭṭaṉa.
paku - pakkam. paku - bhaj (va.). pakkam - pakṣa (va.).
irupattēḻu nāṭkaḷum, puravi, aṭuppu, āral, cakaṭu, māṉṟalai, mūtirai, kaḻai, koṭiṟu, aravu, koṭunukam, kaṇai, uttaram, kai, aṟuvai, viḷakku, muṟam, paṉai, tuḷaṅkoḷi, kuruku, muṟkuḷam, kaṭaikkuḷam, mukkōl, kākkai, cekku, nāḻi, muracu, tōṇi eṉap peyar peṟṟiruntaṉa. ivaṟṟiṟkup piṟa peyarkaḷumuṇṭu. ivaiyallāta potu uṭukkaḷ veḷḷi yeṉappaṭṭaṉa. pukaikkōḷ (comet) vālveḷḷi yeṉap paṭṭatu.
oru pakalum ōr iravum cērntatu oru nāḷ eṉṟum, ēḻu nāḷ koṇṭatu oru kiḻamaiyeṉṟum, oru vaḷarpiṟaiyum oru tēypiṟaiyum cērntatu oru mātam eṉṟum, katiravaṉ paṉṉīrōraikkuḷḷum pukuvatu paṉṉiru mātam eṉṟum, ataṉ oru vaṭacelavum oru teṉ celavum cērntu ōr āṇ ṭeṉṟum, kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭiruntatu. oru nāḷai āṟu ciṟupoḻutākap pirittatu pōṉṟu, ōr āṇṭai āṟu perum poḻutākap pirittiruntaṉar.
oru kōnakart tōṟṟam allatu oru pēraracaṉ piṟappup pōṉṟa nikaḻcciyai aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu, toṭarāṇṭu kaṇittu vantatākat terikiṉṟatu.
ceññā yiṟṟuc celavum añ ñāyiṟṟup
parippum parippuc cūḻntamaṇ ṭilamum
vaḷitiritaru ticaiyum
vaṟitu nilaiiya kāyamum eṉṟivai
ceṉṟaḷan taṟintōr pōla eṉṟum
iṉaitteṉ pōrum uḷarē
eṉṉum (puṟam. 30) uṟaiyūr mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉār kūṟṟum,  mākavicumpu (puṟam. 35, akam. 253, maturaikkā. 454, pari. 1) eṉṉum vaḻakkum, mākamāvatu pūmikkum cuvarkkattukkum naṭuvu eṉṉum parimēlaḻakar uraiyum,
iṉṉicai yeḻiliyai irappavum iyaivatō
vaḷitarum celvaṉai vāḻttavum iyaivatō?
eṉṉum pālaipāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkōviṉ pāṭṭaṭikaḷum (kalit. 15), kavaṉikkat takkaṉa.
maḻaikkum kāṟṟiṟkum katiravaṉ karaṇiyam eṉpatai muṉṉait tamiḻar kaṇṭiruntaṉar. eḻili mukil. eḻiliyait tōṟṟuvikkum katiravaṉai eḻili eṉṟatu ilakkaṇaiyeṉṉum ākupeyarp pōli.
(11) kaṇiyam (Astrology)
oruvar piṟanta nāḷaiyum vāṉattiluḷḷa nāḷ kōḷ nilaiyaiyum aṭippaṭaiyāka vaittu, avarukku varum iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷai muṟpaṭak kaṇittuk kūṟuvatu kaṇiyam. vāḻnāḷ muḻuvataṟkum varaiyappaṭum kaṇiyamē piṟappiyam (jātakam).
kōccēramāṉ yāṉaikkaṭ cēy māntarañ cēralirumpoṟai iṉṉanāḷ iṟappāṉ eṉṟu, kūṭalūrkiḻār oru viṇvīḻ koḷḷiyaik kaṇṭu kaṇittaṟinta ceytiyai 229ām puṟappāṭṭuk kūṟum. nalla nāḷum vēḷaiyum pārttu viṉaikaḷait toṭaṅkuvatu, paṇṭaināḷiṟ peruvaḻakkamā yiruntatu. nāḷēraṭittal, kuṭaināṭkōḷ, vāḷnāṭkōḷ eṉpaṉa itait terivikkum.
toṉṟutoṭṭuk kaṇiyat toḻil ceytu varum tamiḻ vakuppāṉ vaḷḷuvaṉ. vaḷ kūrmai. vaḷḷuvaṉ kūrmatiyaṉ. āriyak kulap pirivāl, avaṉ tāḻttappaṭṭup pēriṭaṅkaḷiṟ perumpālum piḻaip piḻantāṉ.
kaṇṇutal = akakkaṇṇāṟ pārttal, karututal, matittal. kaṇṇiyam = matippu. kaṇ - kaṇi. kaṇittal = matittal, kaṇakkiṭutal, aḷaviṭutal. kaṇi - kaṇitam - kaṇicam = matippu (approximation). kaṇi - kaṇikai = tāḷam kaṇittāṭupavaḷ. kaṇi - kaṇikaṉ, kaṇiyaṉ = nāḷkōḷ nilaikaṇṭu varuṅkāla naṉmai tīmai kaṇippavaṉ. kaṇ, kaṇi, kaṇitam, kaṇikai eṉṉum teṉcoṟkaḷ, vaṭamoḻiyil mutaleḻut teṭuppōcai yuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. kaṇitam eṉpatu kaṇita eṉṟum, kaṇikai eṉpatu kaṇikā eṉṟum, īṟu kuṉṟiyum tirintum vaḻaṅkum.
(12) kaṇakku
kaḷḷutal = poruntutal, ottal, kūṭutal, kaḷḷa = okka (uvama urupu), kaḷ - kaḷam = kūṭumiṭam. kaḷ - kaḷa - kaṇa. kaṇattal = poruntutal, kūṭutal, ottal. kaṇakka = pōla. kuraṅku kaṇakka ōṭukiṟāṉ eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka. kaṇa - kaṇam = kūṭṭam. kaṇavaṉ kūṭukiṉṟavaṉ. kaṇa - kaṇakku = kūṭiya tokai, aḷavu.
kaṇakku eṉpatu mutalil kūṭṭal kaṇakkai maṭṭum kuṟittu, piṉpu nālvakaik kaṇakkiṟkum potup peyarāyiṟṟu.
paṇṭait tamiḻar kaṇakkil mikat tērntavar eṉpatu, avar kaiyāṇṭa nuṇṇiya aḷavaikaḷāl aṟiyappaṭum.

akkālattil araciyalārāl nilam evvaḷavu nuṭpamāy aḷakkap paṭṭateṉpatu, iṟaiyili nīṅkunilam mukkālē iraṇṭu mākāṇi araikkāṇi muntirikaik kīḻ araiyē iraṇṭumā mukkāṇik kīḻ mukkālē nāṉkumā araikkāṇi muntirikaik kīḻ nāṉkumāviṉāl iṟai kaṭṭiṉa kāṇik kaṭaṉ eṉpatāl viḷaṅkum.
